questions_for_human: []
proposed_invariants:
- The text passed to Compartir and Copiar is exactly the `text` field of `GET /catalog`,
  never assembled, trimmed or altered in the front.
- Every old Spanish path that moves or disappears gets one entry in the redirects
  table with its own test in the same diff as the route that replaces it.
- 'A front path whose first segment is also an API segment is served only through
  the ADR-0024 mechanism (GET/HEAD with `Accept: text/html`), never by widening the
  SPA fallback.'
- A share or copy failure shows only the fixed Spanish string, and no catalog text,
  variety name or amount reaches the console, storage or a message.
- No front code states why a variety is left out of the catalog or derives a status
  mark; it renders what the API returns.
- Every route path the front declares under `varieties` has a matching entry in `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS`
  or `SHARED_FRONT_PATTERNS`, checked by a test that lists the declared routes and
  requests each one.
- Every mutation of a stock entry, a price, a threshold, `shown` or a catalog attribute
  invalidates the catalog query on success through one shared helper, never an inline
  `invalidateQueries` of its own.
- A page component of a variety receives its id only as a prop, and only the route
  element reads the URL parameter.
- The warning switch's state is derived from the saved threshold, and changes only
  after the server answers.
- Every reader of `GET /catalog` gets its query options from the one `catalogQueryOptions`
  factory, and no component or hook names the catalog query key inline.
- A form opened over a variety's page, with no path of its own, is opened by pushing
  a history entry, so browser Back closes the form.
- A stub screen makes no data call and no in-app link points at it.
- A route is added to `router.tsx` only in the diff that ships its screen, and no
  screen is a placeholder or "Próximamente".
discard_reason: null
branch: agentic/GH-47
pull_request: null
subtasks:
  routing-server:
    id: routing-server
    title: 'ADR-0027: serve /varieties/* front paths and carry legacy ids across,
      server side'
    brief: "Backend routing layer only; no route, use case or schema changes.\n\n\
      Build:\n- `docs/adr/0027-…md`, amending ADR-0024. It covers: `{low}` is the\
      \ integer id; exact `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS` plus a closed `SHARED_FRONT_PATTERNS`\
      \ (anchored, only `[0-9]+`); `LEGACY_FRONT_PATTERNS` that carry an id across;\
      \ `contract.json`'s `sharedFrontRoutes`; the rewritten intersection rule; and\
      \ what this widens (§8).\n- `app/static_spa.py`:\n  - `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS` gains\
      \ `/varieties`, `/varieties/new`, `/varieties/catalog`, `/varieties/entries`,\
      \ `/varieties/entries/new`.\n  - New `SHARED_FRONT_PATTERNS` (compiled, anchored):\
      \ `^/varieties/[0-9]+$`, `^/varieties/[0-9]+/prices$`, `^/varieties/[0-9]+/prices/new$`,\
      \ `^/varieties/[0-9]+/entries$`, `^/varieties/entries/[0-9]+$`.\n  - The same\
      \ `Accept: text/html` GET/HEAD gate and one-trailing-slash 308 apply to the\
      \ patterns.\n  - New `LEGACY_FRONT_PATTERNS` mechanism: a match is a 308 to\
      \ its target with the captured id substituted, for any method or `Accept`, keeping\
      \ the query string.\n  - The production Spanish rows for varieties stay empty\
      \ here; routing-front adds them.\n- `web/src/api/contract.json`: `frontSegments`\
      \ gains `varieties`. New `sharedFrontRoutes`: `/varieties`, `/varieties/new`,\
      \ `/varieties/catalog`, `/varieties/entries`, `/varieties/entries/new`, `/varieties/entries/7`,\
      \ `/varieties/7`, `/varieties/7/prices`, `/varieties/7/prices/new`, `/varieties/7/entries`,\
      \ `/expenses`, `/expenses/new`.\n- `tests/test_refusal_codes.py`: the API's\
      \ first segments ∩ `frontSegments` ⊆ the first segments derived from `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS`\
      \ ∪ `SHARED_FRONT_PATTERNS`. A test refuses any pattern that is not fully anchored\
      \ or has a variable other than `[0-9]+`.\n\nAcceptance (pytest through `FrontNavigation`/`TestClient`):\n\
      - Every `sharedFrontRoutes` path gets `index.html` for GET and HEAD with `Accept:\
      \ text/html`, and gets the API's own answer with `Accept: application/json`\
      \ (401 signed out).\n- `/varieties/7/prices` with JSON `Accept` reaches the\
      \ API.\n- Near-misses never get the shell: `/varieties/7a`, `/varieties/7/entries/x`,\
      \ `/varieties/entries/7a`, `/varieties/7/prices/new/x`.\n- `LEGACY_FRONT_PATTERNS`\
      \ is tested on an injected table: 308, id carried, query kept, any method.\n\
      - The OpenAPI document, the gated-route test and the isolation sweep are unchanged."
    seams:
    - FrontNavigation ASGI middleware over HTTP (TestClient)
    - contract.json ↔ static_spa tables (tests/test_refusal_codes.py)
    depends_on: []
    status: approved
    branch: agentic/GH-47.routing-server
    base: 7c1f018
    reviewed_head: ebef51f
  routing-front:
    id: routing-front
    title: Move existing variety, catalog and entry screens to English routes; shared
      catalog/stock helpers
    brief: "Front routing and shared helpers, plus the legacy rows for the paths it\
      \ retires.\n\nRoutes:\n- `web/src/routes.ts` gains constants for every `/varieties/*`\
      \ path: `VARIETIES`, `VARIETY(id)`, `VARIETY_PRICES(id)`, `VARIETY_PRICE_NEW(id)`,\
      \ `VARIETY_ENTRIES(id)`, `VARIETY_NEW`, `CATALOG`, `ENTRIES`, `ENTRY_NEW`, `ENTRY(id)`.\n\
      \  - `router.tsx` names `/varieties` paths only through these.\n- `router.tsx`\
      \ remounts **only existing screens**, unchanged:\n  - `Variedades` on `/varieties`,\
      \ `VariedadDetalle` on `/varieties/:id`, `Catalogo` on `/varieties/catalog`;\n\
      \  - `EntradaStock` on `/varieties/entries/new` (mode nuevo) and `/varieties/entries/:id`\
      \ (mode editar).\n  - It removes the Spanish client routes `variedades`, `variedades/:id`,\
      \ `catalogo`, `mi-stock/nueva-entrada` and `mi-stock/entradas/:id`.\n  - `mi-stock`\
      \ (Stock.tsx) and its tab stay until list-and-panes. No stubs: no route for\
      \ a screen not yet built.\n- Every in-app link to the moved paths is updated\
      \ (Variedades, VariedadDetalle, Stock.tsx, Inicio, PinPublish, the shell's Variedades\
      \ tab), along with the e2e `goto`s.\n- `app/static_spa.py` legacy rows, each\
      \ with its own 308 test: `/variedades` → `/varieties`, `/variedades/{n}` → `/varieties/{n}`,\
      \ `/catalogo` → `/varieties/catalog`, `/mi-stock/nueva-entrada` → `/varieties/entries/new`,\
      \ `/mi-stock/entradas/{n}` → `/varieties/entries/{n}`.\n- `scripts/design/screens.json`:\
      \ `route` = `path` for the moved screens (Variedades, VariedadDetalle, Catalogo,\
      \ EntradaStock).\n\nShared helpers (no later subtask edits these files):\n-\
      \ `web/src/api/catalog.ts`: `catalogQueryOptions()` is the only place that names\
      \ the catalog key, with `staleTime: 0`, `gcTime: 0` and no refetch on focus\
      \ or reconnect. `Catalogo.tsx` switches to it.\n- `web/src/api/keys.ts`: a `catalog`\
      \ resource, and `CROSS_INVALIDATES` so that `stock` and `varieties` writes also\
      \ invalidate `catalog`. Test-first in `keys.test.ts`.\n- `web/src/api/stock.ts`:\
      \ `entriesOf(stock, varietyId)`, keeping the server's order, with unit tests.\n\
      - An `architecture` rule: the catalog query key appears only in `api/keys.ts`\
      \ and `api/catalog.ts`.\n- A vitest: every route constant whose first segment\
      \ is an API segment from `contract.json`, with ids written as `7`, appears in\
      \ `contract.json`'s `sharedFrontRoutes`.\n\nAcceptance:\n- The app works end\
      \ to end on the new paths, and reloading each mounted path opens the screen.\n\
      - The existing suite passes, with only `goto`s and links of moved paths edited."
    seams:
    - routes.ts ↔ contract.json sharedFrontRoutes (vitest)
    - invalidateResource / CROSS_INVALIDATES (keys.test.ts)
    - entriesOf (unit)
    - FrontNavigation legacy 308s over HTTP (pytest)
    depends_on:
    - routing-server
    status: approved
    screens:
    - Variedades
    - VariedadDetalle
    - Catalogo
    - EntradaStock
    branch: agentic/GH-47.routing-front
    base: ebef51f
    reviewed_head: c73980f
    reason: 'Fix first: the routes.ts ↔ sharedFrontRoutes vitest''s negative case
      at web/src/routes.test.ts:136 checks its own copy of the filter, not the check
      itself, so nothing shows the real check rejecting a forgotten constant. Everything
      else verified: legacy 308s, CROSS_INVALIDATES, entriesOf, the catalog-key rule
      and the router literal scan.'
    return_category: spec-missing-test
  catalog-screen:
    id: catalog-screen
    title: 'Catalog screen: Compartir, Copiar, reasons by word, pin status and links
      out'
    brief: 'Redraw `Catalogo.tsx` at `/varieties/catalog` from `pantallas/Catalogo`
      and `CatalogoCompu`.


      Layout and copy:

      - A detail: "‹ Variedades", no "+ Nueva venta".

      - "Cómo se ve en Telegram" through `telegramPreview.ts`, with the empty state
      when `text` is null.

      - The pin status and Publicar stay through `PinPublish` (its unavailable link
      stays `/settings/integrations`).

      - "What does not go out, and why": every variety whose `state` ≠ `in`, by name,
      with one word from one table: `hidden` → oculta, `no_price` → sin precio, `sold_out`
      → agotada, `no_entries` → sin entradas. It never compares grams, prices or `shown`
      itself.

      - Each variety named links to `VARIETY(id)`, opening on its En el catálogo section
      (a hash or state the page honours; agree the anchor with variety-page via the
      route constant).

      - "Editar encabezado y pie" links to `/settings/catalog-message`.

      - The existing attribute editing stays on this screen for now; section-forms
      removes it.


      Share seam:

      - New `web/src/share.ts`, the only module that names `navigator.share`. It exports
      `canShare()` (secure context and a share function) and `shareText(text): Promise<''shared''|''cancelled''|''failed''>`,
      where a `DOMException` `AbortError` is `cancelled`.

      - It never logs, and drops the error object.

      - New rule in `architecture/forbiddenApis.ts`, allowed only for `src/share.ts`.
      Write the failing fixture in `forbiddenApis.test.ts` first.


      Behaviour:

      - Compartir is the main action, shown only when `canShare()`. Shared and cancelled
      show nothing; failed shows only "No se pudo compartir.", and Copiar stays.

      - Copiar uses `clipboard.ts`: success shows the toast "Catálogo copiado", failure
      shows "No se pudo copiar.".

      - Both pass exactly the current query''s `text`, and are enabled only once this
      mount''s own fetch has resolved (`isFetchedAfterMount && !isFetching`).


      Tests:

      - `share.ts` unit tests for: present, absent, abort, other rejection.

      - Screen tests with `share.ts` and `clipboard.ts` mocked: every outcome above;
      the argument equals the faked API `text` byte for byte; reasons in priority
      order, one per variety.

      - A test that a second `GET /catalog` goes out when the screen is opened after
      another reader unmounted, and that Copiar is disabled until it resolves.

      - `catalogInsecureOrigin.spec.ts`: over http, Compartir is absent and Copiar
      works.

      - No catalog text, name or amount in the console or in storage.

      - `compare.sh` before and after, phone and computer.'
    seams:
    - share.ts (shareText, canShare)
    - forbiddenApis rule
    - Catalog screen via Testing Library with fetch faked
    depends_on:
    - routing-front
    status: approved
    screens:
    - Catalogo
    - CatalogoCompu
    branch: agentic/GH-47.catalog-screen
    base: c73980f
    reviewed_head: e082008
    reason: 'Fix first: the catalog route still shows "+ Nueva venta" on a detail.
      After that, build the CatalogoCompu two-column layout and replace the raw 10px
      gap with a token.'
    return_category: spec-behaviour
  entries-screens:
    id: entries-screens
    title: Todas las entradas, a variety's entries, and the entry form on its new
      paths
    brief: "Build:\n- **One entries list component**, `EntriesList`, with an optional\
      \ `varietyId`:\n  - `/varieties/entries` (\"Todas las entradas\", card \"‹ Variedades\"\
      ) and `/varieties/:id/entries` (card \"‹ <variety>\"; Back with no history goes\
      \ to `VARIETY(id)`), both mounted in `router.tsx` in this diff.\n  - It reads\
      \ `GET /stock` and filters only through `entriesOf`.\n  - Each row: date, variety,\
      \ origin, grams, total cost, cost per gram, newest first.\n  - Each entry can\
      \ be edited (link to `ENTRY(id)`) and deleted (\"Antes de borrar\" naming date,\
      \ variety and grams; toast \"Entrada eliminada\").\n  - Empty state: \"Sin entradas\
      \ de stock · Cargá tu stock inicial o una compra\".\n- **The `EntradaStock`\
      \ form** on `ENTRY_NEW` and `ENTRY(id)`:\n  - It honours `?variety={id}`: that\
      \ variety is chosen, and \"Stock inicial\" is preselected only when `entriesOf`\
      \ returns none for it.\n  - \"Cerrar\" and a save go back by history. With no\
      \ history: edit → `ENTRIES`, new → `VARIETIES`.\n  - A missing or foreign entry\
      \ gets the same 404 `stock.entry_not_found`, through the app's existing gone\
      \ dispatch: \"Eso ya no está.\" + \"Volver a Variedades\".\n  - Its two inline\
      \ `invalidateQueries` calls move onto `invalidateResource('stock')`.\n- **`screens.json`**:\
      \ rows for `/varieties/{low}/entries` (`design: TodasLasEntradas`) and `/varieties/entries/{id}`\
      \ (`design: EntradaStock`); `route` = `path` for `TodasLasEntradas`.\n\nTests:\n\
      - List, filtered list, edit and delete through Testing Library with `fetch`\
      \ faked.\n- Preselection with and without entries.\n- The gone screen for a\
      \ 404.\n- After a save, the `catalog` key is stale.\n- `compare.sh` before and\
      \ after."
    seams:
    - EntriesList and EntradaStock via Testing Library with fetch faked
    depends_on:
    - routing-front
    status: approved
    screens:
    - TodasLasEntradas
    - EntradaStock
    - EntradaStockCompu
    branch: agentic/GH-47.entries-screens
    base: c73980f
    reviewed_head: 6ae9882
    reason: 'Fix first: screens.json still marks EntradaStockCompu as "not in the
      app", although its route now exists. compare.sh was never run on the delivered
      list and form, so invariant 16 and §10''s "keep its entry in screens.json true"
      do not hold yet.'
    return_category: plan-invariant
  prices-and-rename:
    id: prices-and-rename
    title: A variety's price history, the price form and rename
    brief: "Build three screens, each mounted in `router.tsx` in this diff:\n- **Price\
      \ history**, `VerTodas` at `VARIETY_PRICES(id)`:\n  - a card \"‹ <variety>\"\
      \ with no \"+ Nueva venta\"; Back with no history goes to `VARIETY(id)`;\n \
      \ - reads `GET /varieties/{id}/prices`, newest first, with \"Rige desde\";\n\
      \  - each row has its delete action, confirmed with \"Antes de borrar\" using\
      \ the existing `priceDeleteNotice` copy (what applies again). Toast \"Precio\
      \ eliminado\".\n- **The price form**, `PrecioNuevo` at `VARIETY_PRICE_NEW(id)`:\n\
      \  - \"Cerrar\", and the discard guard when something is typed;\n  - `POST /varieties/{id}/prices`;\
      \ refusals inline under each field from the `variety.*` codes, keeping what\
      \ was typed;\n  - toast \"Precio guardado\"; back by history, else to `VARIETY(id)`.\n\
      - **The rename form**: `PATCH /varieties/{id}`, with the same form chrome.\n\
      \  - It is opened from the page through history state (`navigate('.', {state:{form:'rename'}})`)\
      \ as the section forms are, so it needs no new path.\n  - Export the form component;\
      \ variety-page wires the opener.\n  - Toast \"Variedad guardada\".\n\nWhere\
      \ they mutate, use `invalidateResource('varieties')`.\n\nTests:\n- Through Testing\
      \ Library with `fetch` faked: list order, delete copy and notice, each refusal\
      \ under its field, the discard guard, the gone variety (\"Eso ya no está.\"\
      ).\n- After a save, the `catalog` key is stale.\n- `screens.json` `route` =\
      \ `path` for VerTodas and PrecioNuevo.\n- `compare.sh` before and after."
    seams:
    - Price list, price form and rename form via Testing Library with fetch faked
    depends_on:
    - routing-front
    status: approved
    screens:
    - VerTodas
    - PrecioNuevo
    - PrecioNuevoCompu

    base: c73980f
    reviewed_head: 3c931dd
  variety-page:
    id: variety-page
    title: 'A variety''s page: summary, + Entrada / Nuevo precio, four read-only sections'
    brief: "Replace `VariedadDetalle.tsx` with `VarietyPage({varietyId})` plus `VarietyRoute`.\n\
      - `VarietyRoute` is the only reader of `:id`; a non-integer id is \"Eso ya no\
      \ está.\". It is mounted on `VARIETY(id)`.\n- The page is a detail: \"‹ Variedades\"\
      , no \"+ Nueva venta\".\n\nContent:\n- **One summary line**: grams, the status\
      \ word with `MarcaEstado`, days left, today's list price or \"Sin precio: no\
      \ se puede vender\", and \"Oculta\" when hidden.\n- **Two equal buttons**: \"\
      + Entrada\" → `ENTRY_NEW?variety={id}`, and \"Nuevo precio\" → `VARIETY_PRICE_NEW(id)`.\n\
      - **One component per section**, read-only, with their opener links:\n  - `VarietyStockSection`:\
      \ last 3 via `entriesOf(...).slice(0,3)` + \"Ver todas\" → `VARIETY_ENTRIES(id)`;\n\
      \  - `VarietyPricesSection`: last 3 price entries with their delete action +\
      \ \"Ver todas\" → `VARIETY_PRICES(id)`;\n  - `VarietyThresholdSection`: \"Avisame\
      \ abajo de 15 g\" / \"Avisando: quedan 18,5 g\" / \"Aviso apagado\", the switch\
      \ and \"Editar\";\n  - `VarietyCatalogSection`: shown or hidden, emoji, % índica,\
      \ aroma/sabor (via `catalogQueryOptions` with `select`) and \"Editar\". It honours\
      \ the anchor the catalog screen links to.\n- **Rename** opener wired to prices-and-rename's\
      \ form via history state.\n- In this subtask, \"Editar\" and the switch's \"\
      on\" push history state for a form that section-forms renders. Until then, render\
      \ nothing for an unknown form state (no placeholder).\n\nBehaviour:\n- All reads\
      \ go through the shared helpers.\n- A gone variety shows \"Eso ya no está.\"\
      \ + \"Volver a Variedades\".\n\nTests:\n- Through Testing Library with `fetch`\
      \ faked: summary per status; only Stock y entradas and Precios carry the last\
      \ 3 and \"Ver todas\"; the \"+ Entrada\" link carries the variety; the gone\
      \ and foreign id screens.\n- `compare.sh` before and after."
    seams:
    - VarietyPage via Testing Library with fetch faked (prop varietyId)
    - VarietyRoute param parsing
    depends_on:
    - prices-and-rename
    - entries-screens
    status: approved
    screens:
    - VariedadDetalle
    branch: agentic/GH-47.variety-page
    base: 3c4c48b
    conflicted_paths: []
    reviewed_head: 7aaa72b
    reason: 'Remove the new routes.test.ts:36 test: it checks router.tsx''s handle
      from a suite that is not one of this subtask''s declared seams. The same chrome
      is already covered at the VarietyPage seam. Everything the last pass asked for
      is in: the before/after compare.sh runs are recorded, and the 58 tests in the
      two touched files pass.'
    return_category: standard-seams
  section-forms:
    id: section-forms
    title: Aviso de stock bajo and En el catálogo forms; remove attribute editing
      from the catalog screen
    brief: "Build two forms rendered over `VarietyPage` when `location.state.form`\
      \ is `threshold` or `catalog`. Each has form chrome (\"Cerrar\", no bar, no\
      \ \"+ Nueva venta\"); \"Cerrar\" and a successful save call `navigate(-1)`;\
      \ `useBlocker` guards only typed, unsaved text.\n\n- **`ThresholdForm`**:\n\
      \  - \"Avisar con menos de\" (grams), \"Guardá el aviso\";\n  - `PUT /stock/varieties/{id}/threshold`\
      \ with `{grams}`;\n  - `stock.threshold_invalid` shows under the field, keeping\
      \ what was typed;\n  - toast \"Aviso guardado\".\n- **The switch** in `VarietyThresholdSection`\
      \ (edit it here) is derived from `threshold !== null`:\n  - \"on\" only opens\
      \ `ThresholdForm`, and closing without saving leaves it off;\n  - \"off\" sends\
      \ `{grams: null}`. It is disabled and still on while the request runs, and on\
      \ success shows \"Aviso guardado\".\n  - A refusal shows its message, offline\
      \ shows \"Sin conexión\", and a 404 shows \"Eso ya no está. La lista ya está\
      \ al día.\". In all three it stays on.\n- **`CatalogAttributesForm`**:\n  -\
      \ \"Mostrar en el catálogo\", Emoji, % índica, Aroma/sabor, ending in \"Guardá\
      \ el catálogo\";\n  - `PUT /catalog/varieties/{id}`;\n  - `catalog.emoji_invalid`,\
      \ `catalog.indica_share_invalid` and `catalog.aroma_too_long` each show under\
      \ their own field. This code → field mapping is written test-first;\n  - toast\
      \ \"Catálogo guardado\"; `invalidateResource('catalog')`.\n- **In the same diff**,\
      \ remove all attribute editing from `Catalogo.tsx` (its `setAttr*` state and\
      \ `catalog.*` code map). Move the matching tests from `Catalogo.test.tsx` to\
      \ the new forms' tests.\n\nTests through Testing Library with `fetch` faked:\n\
      - read-only until \"Editar\";\n- switch on opens the form, and \"Cerrar\" leaves\
      \ it off;\n- switch off saves at once;\n- a too-long aroma is refused under\
      \ its field, with everything typed kept;\n- Back closes the form, and with typed\
      \ text asks first;\n- a reload with that state reopens the form with the saved\
      \ values;\n- the `catalog` and `stock` keys are stale after save.\n\nThe PR\
      \ lists the two agreed departures from the drawing."
    seams:
    - CatalogAttributesForm refusal code → field mapping (test-first)
    - ThresholdForm and switch via Testing Library with fetch faked
    depends_on:
    - variety-page
    - catalog-screen
    status: approved
    screens:
    - VariedadDetalle

  list-and-panes:
    id: list-and-panes
    title: Variedades list, summary line, two panes, Nueva variedad; remove Stock
    brief: "Redraw the list from `pantallas/Variedades` and `VariedadesCompu`.\n\n\
      The list (tab root):\n- Every variety, hidden ones included, sorted by name.\n\
      - Each row: name; `MarcaEstado` and its word, or \"Sin entradas\", with its\
      \ detail line; \"Oculta\" beside the status, never in its place; grams and days\
      \ left; today's list price or \"Sin precio: no se puede vender\".\n- Rows read\
      \ `GET /stock`, `GET /varieties` and `catalogQueryOptions`.\n\nAbove and below\
      \ the list:\n- **Summary line** from one pure `summaryLine(statuses)`, written\
      \ test-first with unit tests:\n  - no varieties: none, and the fixed empty state;\n\
      \  - all ok: \"N variedades · todo ok\";\n  - otherwise: each non-ok status\
      \ counted by word, in stock-status order.\n- **\"Mandar catálogo\"** on top\
      \ → `CATALOG`, disabled with its reason in words only while the first-run checklist\
      \ shows and nothing qualifies (use the existing source).\n- **\"+ Entrada de\
      \ stock\"**, a secondary button → `ENTRY_NEW`.\n- **\"Nueva variedad\"** → `VARIETY_NEW`:\
      \ a new `VariedadNueva` form (`POST /varieties`, form chrome, refusals inline,\
      \ toast \"Variedad guardada\"), mounted in this diff.\n- **\"Todas las entradas\"\
      ** at the foot → `ENTRIES`.\n\nPanes:\n- `VarietiesLayout` nests `/varieties`\
      \ and `/varieties/:id`, as `ClientesLayout` does.\n- On a computer, list and\
      \ outlet side by side. `useSelectedVariety`: `/varieties` redirects with `replace`\
      \ to the first variety by name, only once the list has resolved and is non-empty.\
      \ Loading or empty: no redirect; the empty right pane holds no figure.\n- On\
      \ a phone, the list alone, and `/varieties/:id` shows the page alone.\n- It\
      \ embeds `VarietyPage` through `VarietyRoute` and never edits it.\n\nRemove\
      \ Stock:\n- Delete `Stock.tsx`, `Stock.test.tsx`, the `mi-stock` route and the\
      \ Stock tab, leaving the bar as Inicio · Ventas · Clientes · Variedades · Más,\
      \ fitting 320 px at 130 %.\n- Add the `/mi-stock` → `/varieties` 308 row in\
      \ `app/static_spa.py`, with its test.\n\nAlso:\n- `screens.json`: `route` =\
      \ `path` for VariedadesCompu and VariedadNueva.\n- `compare.sh` before and after,\
      \ phone and computer.\n\nTests with a faked viewport:\n- a computer at `/varieties`\
      \ lands on the first id;\n- a direct `/varieties/7` shows both panes;\n- Back\
      \ from a form returns to `/varieties/7`;\n- loading and empty lists do not redirect;\n\
      - the phone never opens a variety on its own."
    seams:
    - summaryLine (unit, test-first)
    - useSelectedVariety via Testing Library with faked viewport
    - Variedades list via Testing Library with fetch faked
    - FrontNavigation /mi-stock 308 (pytest)
    depends_on:
    - variety-page
    status: approved
    screens:
    - Variedades
    - VariedadesCompu
    - VariedadNueva
    - VariedadNuevaCompu

  integration:
    id: integration
    title: Integration
    brief: 'Merge all subtasks into one PR, so no intermediate state (Stock gone before
      the list carries status, or attribute editing gone before its form) reaches
      master. After merging, run the full e2e suite:

      - tap budget (catalog screen and stock entry form ≤2 taps from every tab root,
      phone and computer);

      - console and storage helpers;

      - catalogInsecureOrigin;

      - reloads on every sharedFrontRoutes path;

      - a no-curl run that adds a variety, adds and deletes a price, renames, sets,
      turns off and turns on a threshold, edits catalog attributes from the page,
      edits header/footer in Configuración, opens Mandar catálogo and taps Copiar
      (clipboard equals the API text).


      Confirm scripts/design/screens.json has route = path for every shipped screen
      and the two new rows. Attach the compare.sh before/after comparisons for the
      list, the page and the catalog screen (phone and computer). List in the PR every
      design-vs-docs wording difference and the two agreed departures (the Editar
      forms of Aviso de stock bajo and En el catálogo).'
    conflicts: []
    seams: []
    depends_on: []
    status: approved
    branch: agentic/GH-47
    base: 844942f
    reviewed_head: 9a9b00b12e4eb88cdee927171006128ab8d50089
    reason: VarietyEntriesRoute is a second reader of the variety :id parameter, which
      breaks invariant 12. Mount /varieties/:id/entries through VarietyRoute's render
      prop instead.
    return_category: plan-invariant
invariants:
- No backend use case, schema, endpoint or refusal code changes; the only backend
  edits are in app/static_spa.py and its tests.
- 'A front path whose first segment is an API segment is served only through SHARED_FRONT_PATHS
  or SHARED_FRONT_PATTERNS (GET/HEAD with Accept: text/html); every pattern is fully
  anchored and its only variable is [0-9]+.'
- Every route constant in routes.ts under an API segment appears in contract.json's
  sharedFrontRoutes, and router.tsx names /varieties paths only through routes.ts
  constants.
- Every old Spanish path that moves gets one row in LEGACY_FRONT_PATHS or LEGACY_FRONT_PATTERNS
  with its own 308 test, in the diff that retires the old screen or route.
- A route is added to router.tsx only in the diff that ships its screen; no placeholder
  or "Próximamente" screen exists.
- The text passed to the preview, Compartir and Copiar is exactly GET /catalog's text,
  enabled only after the current mount's fetch resolved; nothing assembles, trims
  or caches it.
- Only catalogQueryOptions (web/src/api/catalog.ts) names the catalog query key or
  sets its options.
- Every mutation invalidates through invalidateResource and its CROSS_INVALIDATES
  rules, never an inline invalidateQueries.
- Only web/src/share.ts names navigator.share, only clipboard.ts the clipboard; neither
  logs, and a failure shows only its fixed Spanish string.
- No front code decides a stock status, a catalog exclusion reason or the catalog
  text; reasons map GET /catalog's state through one table, statuses come from GET
  /stock, and the summary is counted only by summaryLine.
- Entries of one variety are filtered only by entriesOf.
- VarietyPage receives its id only as a prop; VarietyRoute is the only reader of the
  :id parameter.
- A form opened over a variety's page has no path of its own and is opened by pushing
  a history entry, so Back closes it.
- The low-stock switch is derived from the saved threshold and changes only after
  the server answers.
- No catalog text, name, amount or grams reach the console, browser storage or an
  error message; no business id in any URL.
- Every screen uses design tokens only, tells every state by shape or word, and runs
  scripts/design/compare.sh before and after.
integration_brief: 'Merge all subtasks into one PR, so no intermediate state (Stock
  gone before the list carries status, or attribute editing gone before its form)
  reaches master. After merging, run the full e2e suite:

  - tap budget (catalog screen and stock entry form ≤2 taps from every tab root, phone
  and computer);

  - console and storage helpers;

  - catalogInsecureOrigin;

  - reloads on every sharedFrontRoutes path;

  - a no-curl run that adds a variety, adds and deletes a price, renames, sets, turns
  off and turns on a threshold, edits catalog attributes from the page, edits header/footer
  in Configuración, opens Mandar catálogo and taps Copiar (clipboard equals the API
  text).


  Confirm scripts/design/screens.json has route = path for every shipped screen and
  the two new rows. Attach the compare.sh before/after comparisons for the list, the
  page and the catalog screen (phone and computer). List in the PR every design-vs-docs
  wording difference and the two agreed departures (the Editar forms of Aviso de stock
  bajo and En el catálogo).'

- subtask: list-and-panes
  version: 1
  location: web/src/components/useIsComputer.ts:1

  severity: important
  scope: subtask

  status: open
- subtask: list-and-panes
  version: 1
  location: web/src/screens/Variedades.tsx:98
  claim: The Developer lists ways the screen knowingly differs from the design. In
    the phone list, the shape and the word sit together on the right, where the design
    puts the shape left of the name and the word as a chip. The computer modal is
    vertically centred instead of at 80 px from the top, and has no header divider
    or shadow.
  severity: important
  scope: subtask
  category: standard-other
  status: open

  version: 1
  location: web/src/screens/Variedades.test.tsx:252

  status: open
- subtask: list-and-panes
  version: 1

  version: 1

  severity: observation

  category: spec-missing-test
  status: dismissed
- subtask: list-and-panes
  version: 1
  location: web/src/screens/Variedades.tsx:77
  claim: Rows come from flatMap over GET /varieties joined with GET /stock, so any
    variety with no stock row would vanish from the list, while useSelectedVariety
    picks from the unfiltered list. This is harmless as long as /stock answers one
    row per variety.
  severity: observation
  scope: subtask
  category: spec-behaviour
  status: dismissed
- subtask: list-and-panes
  version: 1
  location: web/src/screens/Variedades.tsx:13

design_items:
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: blocking
  step: Situation 4 · VariedadDetalle / VariedadesCompu · Aviso de stock bajo
  text: The flow says to tap the edit action, set or turn off the threshold, and save.
    The prototype draws only an on/off toggle and the fixed text "Avisar con menos
    de 20 g". There is no edit action, no amount field and no Save. As drawn, the
    seller cannot set or change the amount, and the flow describes a step that does
    not exist on the screen.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: blocking
  step: Situation 4 · VariedadDetalle / VariedadesCompu · En el catálogo
  text: The flow describes an edit action that opens a sheet with "Cerrar" and Save.
    The prototype draws the toggle and the Emoji, % índica and Aroma/sabor fields
    inline on the page, with no edit action, no Save and no Cerrar. The flow has to
    say how an inline change is saved, when "Catálogo guardado" shows, where the refusals
    for emoji, % índica outside 0–100 and aroma over 200 characters appear, and what
    happens to typed text when the seller leaves the page.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Situation 2 / 10 · VariedadDetalle → Ver todas in Stock y entradas
  text: 'In the prototype, on both phone and computer, "Ver todas" in Stock y entradas
    opens TodasLasEntradas: every variety''s entries, with "‹ Variedades". It is not
    a card for this variety with "‹ <variety>". The PRD and the flow assume the per-variety
    card. Either the flow names the screen it actually reaches, or this is a gap in
    the prototype to raise.'
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Situation 4 · Rename
  text: The flow has no path for renaming. In the prototype, "Editar" next to the
    name opens VariedadNueva(Compu), the new-variety form. The flow should name that
    step and its touch count.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Situation 8 · temporary place
  text: Sin prototipo. The catalog screen links "Editar encabezado y pie" to ConfiguracionMensaje
    and Publicar's "unavailable" text to ConfiguracionIntegraciones, so the prototype
    assumes Configuración exists. The flow's conditional branch covers both cases.
    The temporary place's title and layout are judged on the flow's text alone.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Situation 5 · Catalogo · nothing qualifies
  text: Sin prototipo for the catalog's empty state. Catalogo draws Compartir and
    Copiar only with a full catalog. Whether they are hidden or disabled on an empty
    catalog is still undefined, as the flow already flagged.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Situation 5 · Catalogo · Compartir unavailable / failures
  text: Catalogo and CatalogoCompu draw Compartir and Copiar side by side. Neither
    the version without Compartir (no share sheet) nor the "No se pudo compartir."
    and "No se pudo copiar." lines are drawn. These steps are judged on the flow's
    text alone (sin prototipo).
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Situation 6 / 7 · Inicio notice
  text: Sin prototipo. Inicio and InicioCompu do not draw "Tu catálogo en Telegram
    está desactualizado", so the 1-tap entry from Inicio could not be walked.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Situation 7 · Catalogo · Publicar unavailable
  text: The prototype's reason reads "Conectá el bot en Integraciones para poder publicarlo
    en Telegram." The flow quotes "Probalo primero para ver cómo queda" and a missing-channel
    reason, which are not drawn. The exact wording should be lined up with the design.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Situation 1 · Variedades list rows
  text: 'The prototype has no row that is hidden ("Oculta" beside the status) and
    none that reads "Sin precio: no se puede vender". These are judged on the flow''s
    text alone. The computer list drops the status word and shows only the mark, so
    on the computer the status is told by shape and color without the word. Check
    this against criterion 4.'
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Counting rule · tab roots
  text: The flow counts Gastos and Análisis as tab roots. The phone prototype's bar
    has only Inicio, Ventas, Clientes, Variedades and Más, so on the phone those screens
    are reached through Más. The 2-tap counts still hold from the tab roots that are
    drawn.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: blocking
  step: VariedadDetalle · Aviso de stock bajo · set or change the threshold (Situation
    4)
  text: 'The prototype draws only the toggle, the status "Avisando: quedan 18 g" and
    the fixed text "Avisar con menos de 20 g". No control sets or changes the amount,
    and nothing says what amount applies when the warning is first turned on. The
    PRD requires the current threshold with its edit action, and the end-to-end check
    "sets and turns off a threshold". As drawn, setting a threshold is a dead end.'
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: blocking
  step: VariedadDetalle · En el catálogo · saving a change (Situation 4)
  text: The toggle and the Emoji, % índica and Aroma/sabor fields are inline, with
    no Save button, no Cerrar and no drawn saved state. The design does not say how
    a change is saved, when "Catálogo guardado" shows, where a refusal (bad emoji,
    % outside 0–100, aroma over 200 characters) appears, or what happens to unsaved
    typing when the seller leaves the page. The flow cannot show the job completing,
    and the developer would have to invent this, which is the human's design call.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: VariedadDetalle → "Ver todas" in Stock y entradas → TodasLasEntradas (Situation
    10)
  text: 'The link opens the business-wide list with "‹ Variedades", not a list of
    this variety''s entries with "‹ <variety>" as the PRD says. The job still completes:
    the entry is there and Back lands on the list. But the seller loses their place
    on the variety''s page. The design and the PRD need to agree on which one is right.'
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: VariedadesCompu · list rows (Situation 1)
  text: The computer rows show only the status mark and drop the word. Success criterion
    4 asks for shape and word.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Catalogo · Publicar unavailable (Situation 7)
  text: The prototype says "Conectá el bot en Integraciones…" and links to ConfiguracionIntegraciones.
    The product's reasons are different ("Probalo primero…", or the channel is missing).
    The temporary place the link should lead to while Configuración does not exist
    is sin prototipo.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Catalogo · "Editar encabezado y pie" (Situation 8)
  text: It links to ConfiguracionMensaje, which is out of scope here. The temporary
    place is sin prototipo and is judged on the flow's text alone. That text is consistent
    with the PM's rules (Cerrar goes back by history, or to the catalog screen when
    there is none).
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Catalogo · Compartir/Copiar edge states (Situation 5)
  text: 'Sin prototipo: no share sheet, "No se pudo compartir.", "No se pudo copiar.",
    the empty catalog, and whether Compartir and Copiar are hidden or disabled when
    it is empty. The only way out when both fail is selecting the text by hand, and
    that is not drawn.'
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: VariedadDetalle · Precios row delete (Situation 3)
  text: The delete button points to "#borrar", which goes nowhere in the prototype.
    The "Antes de borrar" confirmation and the copy saying what takes the price's
    place are sin prototipo.
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Variedades rows and Catalogo "Qué no sale y por qué" (Situations 1 and 6)
  text: 'Sin prototipo: a hidden row ("Oculta" beside the status), "Sin precio: no
    se puede vender", and oculta and sin precio as reasons a variety is left out.'
- role_slug: ux_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Inicio · "Tu catalogo en Telegram está desactualizado" notice (Situations
    6 and 7)
  text: Sin prototipo on Inicio and InicioCompu. The flow does not depend on it.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: blocking
  step: Variety's page · Aviso de stock bajo (set or change the threshold)
  text: The PRD requires the current threshold and an action to edit it, and the metrics
    check that a threshold is set from this page. The screens design has only the
    toggle and a fixed 'Avisar con menos de N g', with no way to set or change N and
    no rule for the amount used when the warning is first turned on. Componentes already
    has the grams field this needs, and 'Aviso guardado' has no moment to fire.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: blocking
  step: Variety's page · En el catálogo (save, refusals, unsaved typing)
  text: The PRD requires these attributes to be edited here, saved with 'Catálogo
    guardado', with refusals shown inline and keeping what was typed. The design draws
    the toggle and three fields but gives no save moment, no toast timing, no inline
    errors (emoji not exactly one, % outside 0–100, aroma over 200) and no rule for
    unsaved typing. Componentes' 'Campo … con error' and the existing save treatments
    can carry all of it.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Variedades list (computer) · row status
  text: VariedadesCompu shows the status mark without its word, which goes against
    'shape and word' (criterion 4). Add the word as on the phone; the same component
    does it.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: 'Variedades list · ''Oculta'' and ''Sin precio: no se puede vender'''
  text: Neither is drawn. Building them from the existing chip and secondary-text
    treatments, as UI Designer proposes, keeps the design recognisably the same, so
    this is accepted.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Variety's page · Precios · delete-price confirmation
  text: The delete button points to '#borrar' and the 'Antes de borrar' confirmation
    isn't drawn. The existing confirmation treatment covers it, but the copy for what
    takes the price's place should be written out.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Variety's page · Stock y entradas · 'Ver todas'
  text: The prototype opens the global TodasLasEntradas ('‹ Variedades'); the PRD
    asks for a card with '‹ <variety>'. The job still completes. The human's prototype
    leads, so this is recorded for the PR's design-vs-docs list, not patched.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Catalog screen · empty catalog; Compartir and Copiar both failing
  text: Not drawn. Say whether Compartir and Copiar are hidden or disabled when nothing
    qualifies, and show the two failure lines together. Both can use the existing
    states.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
  step: Catalog screen · 'Qué no sale' reasons 'oculta' and 'sin precio'
  text: Only agotada and sin entradas are drawn. The other two reuse the same row
    and are told by word.
- role_slug: ui_lead
  kind: observation
