# Variedades as redesigned: one place per variety, and the catalog screen with Compartir

## Original request

Waits for #58 (the new structure in the product docs, the glossary and the design). The `agentic` label goes on once #58 is merged. The decisions are in #58; the design in `docs/product/design-system/pantallas/` as #58 leaves it is the reference, by eye (CODING_STANDARDS.md section 10). Measure before and after with `scripts/design/compare.sh` and attach the comparisons to the PR.

#### Expected

Variedades as #58 draws it (Stock, Variedades and the per-variety catalog attributes merged), phone and computer (two panes on the computer):
- **List**: name, status mark with shape and word, grams and days left, list price per gram; sorted by name; summary line on top; "Mandar catálogo" on top; "+ Entrada de stock" as a secondary button; "Todas las entradas" at the foot.
- **A variety's page**: one-line summary; "+ Entrada" and "Nuevo precio"; sections Stock y entradas, Precios, Aviso de stock bajo, En el catálogo (emoji, % índica, aroma, shown or not), each with its last 3 rows and "Ver todas". An entry started here has the variety chosen and "Stock inicial" preselected when it has none.
- **Catalog screen** ("Mandar catálogo"): preview; "Compartir" (native share sheet, `navigator.share`, with "Copiar" as fallback and where sharing is unavailable); the pinned message's state with Publicar (says why in words when unavailable, links to Integraciones); what does not go out and why; links to each variety's catalog section and to Configuración → Mensaje del catálogo.
- Stock, the old Catálogo screen and the old Variedades screens are gone (their routes redirect).

#### Out of scope

Configuración (its own issue), other areas.

## PjM

Feature: users see a redesigned Variedades (one page per variety, a new catalog screen with Compartir, and Stock and the old screens removed), and the product docs in #58 define it.

## Interview: Product Analyst→PM

### Round 1: questions

### Round 1: the job to be done

The `grilling` and `domain-modeling` skills are not installed in this environment, so I am running the interview by hand in the format the task gives. I read the product docs first. Navigation.md, varieties.md, stock.md, catalog.md and pinned-catalog.md already describe the target structure. This round asks only about the job. I looked up everything else and will not ask it.

❓ **Q1** - **The job and the situation**: Today a seller does three things in three places. They check stock in Stock, change a price or hide a variety in Variedades, and send the catalog from Catálogo. Which situation sends the seller to the redesigned Variedades? My reading of the docs is that it is mostly mid-chat, when a customer asks "¿tenés X?" or "¿a cuánto está?", or after flower arrives or runs out. Do you agree, or is there a different main situation?
➡️ Recommendation: adopt this job story.
**When** a customer asks what I have, or flower comes in or runs out or a price changes, **I want** everything about one variety (stock, entries, prices, low-stock warning, catalog attributes) on one page, and the catalog to be one tap away from the list and shareable from my phone, **so I can** answer, restock and send an up-to-date catalog without hunting through screens, and customers never see a stale price or a variety I can't sell.

❓ **Q2** - **What the seller does today without it**: The docs say one variety is split across Stock, Variedades and Catálogo. Sending the catalog means copying text, and the bot settings sit inside Catálogo. Is that the real pain, or is there a bigger one I can't see in the docs (for example, forgetting to update the catalog after a price change, or not knowing which variety is about to run out)?
➡️ Recommendation: state the pain as "three screens per variety, and copy-and-paste to send the catalog". Pinned-catalog.md already covers forgetting to update, so it would not be this issue's pain.

❓ **Q3** - **How the seller judges it went well**: These are the success criteria I propose, taken from navigation.md, varieties.md, stock.md and catalog.md. Do you accept them as the measure for this issue?
1. From the Variedades list, the stock entry form (for the chosen variety) and the catalog screen open in at most 2 taps. The catalog screen is reached with "Mandar catálogo" and the entry form with "+ Entrada de stock".
2. A seller can read a variety's stock, status, days left and today's price, and edit its price, threshold and catalog attributes, from that variety's page alone. No fact is editable from two screens.
3. From the phone, a seller sends today's catalog through the native share sheet in one tap from the catalog screen. Copiar remains where sharing is unavailable.
➡️ Recommendation: accept all three. They already match the docs, so the PRD adds no new criterion beyond them.

❓ **Q4** - **Was anything dropped?**: Stock, the old Catálogo screen and the old Variedades screens go away, and their paths redirect. Navigation.md says "nothing lost, nothing invented". Is there anything a seller does on the old screens that you want to keep and that the new structure does not carry?
➡️ Recommendation: no. Every old action has a new home in the docs. The bot settings and the header and footer moved to Configuración, which is a separate issue. Until that issue ships, those edits have no home. Confirm this is acceptable, or tell me the order the two issues must ship in.

### Round 1: answers

**Q1**: Accepted, with my own wording. The main situation is mid-chat, between orders. Flower arriving, running out or being repriced comes second.

**Job story**: **When** a customer asks me what I have or how much something costs, or flower comes in, runs out or gets a new price, **I want** to open one variety and find everything about it on its own page: its stock and entries, its prices, its low-stock warning and how it shows in the catalog. I also want the list to tell me at a glance what I have, what is running out and what I can't sell. And I want today's catalog one tap from that list, ready to share from my phone. **So I can** answer the customer, record what came in and send an up-to-date catalog without moving between screens. My customers never get a stale price or a variety I can't sell.

This is the navigation job (navigation.md) as it applies to varieties, stock and the catalog. It is not a new job. The brief's stock and catalog jobs stay where they are defined (stock.md, catalog.md). The pinned catalog's upkeep stays in pinned-catalog.md. The computer is the same job on a bigger screen, with two panes.

**Q2**: Accepted, with one thing added. The pain has three parts:
- one variety is split across three screens: Stock, Variedades and Catálogo;
- sending the catalog means copying it and switching apps;
- the list doesn't show, on its own, what is running out or can't be sold.

That third part already belongs to stock.md (criterion 4) and varieties.md (criterion 5). This issue owns it because the list is the screen that carries it. Forgetting to update the pinned message stays with pinned-catalog.md, as you say.

**Q3**: Accepted, with additions. These are this issue's success criteria. All of them already come from the docs, so none is new to the product:
1. **Two taps at most**: from any tab root, the catalog screen ("Mandar catálogo") and the stock entry form ("+ Entrada de stock") open in at most 2 taps. On a computer it takes no more taps than on the phone. An entry started from a variety's page comes with that variety chosen, and with "Stock inicial" preselected only when the variety has no entries yet.
2. **One variety, one page**: a variety's stock, status, days left and today's price can all be read from its page. Its price entries, threshold and catalog attributes are edited from that page and nowhere else. No fact is editable on two screens.
3. **Sharing in one tap**: from the catalog screen, Compartir hands the native share sheet exactly the text Copiar copies, `**` markers included. Where the browser has no share sheet, Compartir is not shown and Copiar remains. This works from the phone on the home network's plain http.
4. **Nothing guessed**: every status on the list and on the page is told by shape and word, never by color alone, and the summary line is never blank. Every variety that does not go out in the catalog shows exactly one reason, in words.
5. **Nothing lost, nothing invented**: everything a seller could do on Stock, the old Catálogo and the old Variedades can still be reached. Every old path redirects permanently. No link points at a screen that does not exist.
6. **Businesses never mix** (ADR-0004): nothing here shows another business's data.

**Q4**: Your recommendation stands, but I'm adding one condition on the order things ship in. Navigation.md's "nothing lost, nothing invented" rule and the precedent in expenses.md ("no link may point at a screen that doesn't exist, and no seller may lose category management") decide this between them, so it doesn't need to go to the human:
- **The order**: the old Catálogo screen may stop editing the bot settings and the header and footer only once Configuración → Integraciones and Configuración → Mensaje del catálogo exist.
- **The links**: the catalog screen's "Editar encabezado y pie" and the link it shows when Publicar is unavailable may only point at groups that exist.
- **If Configuración ships first** (it is its own issue), this issue simply links to it.
- **If this issue ships first**, those edits stay reachable until the groups exist. A seller is never left without a way to connect the bot or edit the header and footer.

Two more decisions this issue owns:
- **The catalog screen's address**: navigation.md hands its move to an English route under Variedades, with the permanent redirect from `/catalogo`, to "the issue that moves Variedades to its English address". That is this issue. The exact paths are the ones in `scripts/design/screens.json`.
- **Shared paths with the API**: where a front path shares its first segment with the API, ADR-0024 settles how it is served. Each moved screen adds its own entry in the same change.

Assumption: this issue removes nothing beyond the screens the request names. The customer channel was already removed by customers.md, so it is not this issue's exception.

### Round 2: questions

### Round 2: behaviour that serves the job

The job and its criteria are settled, and I won't reopen them. I checked the repo for facts. `web/src/screens/` has no Configuración screen yet, and `scripts/design/screens.json` already fixes the new routes (`/varieties`, `/varieties/{low}`, `/varieties/catalog`, `/varieties/entries`, and others). So I am not asking about routes.

❓ **Q1** - **Where the bot settings and the header and footer live until Configuración exists**: Today they are edited inside the old Catálogo screen, and Configuración is a separate issue that is not built. Your condition from round 1 says a seller must never be left unable to connect the bot or edit the header and footer. The new catalog screen's "Editar encabezado y pie" and Publicar's "link to Integraciones" would otherwise point at screens that don't exist yet. What should this issue do in the meantime?
➡️ Recommendation: keep the bot settings and the header and footer editing reachable exactly where they work today, as a temporary section reached from the catalog screen. The two links ("Editar encabezado y pie", and Publicar's "Probalo primero" link) open that temporary place. When Configuración ships, its own issue replaces those two targets with Configuración → Mensaje del catálogo and Configuración → Integraciones. This issue adds no "coming soon" entry and no new Configuración group.

❓ **Q2** - **What the right pane shows on a computer before any variety is picked**: On a computer, Variedades is two panes: the list on the left and the selected variety's page on the right. Opening `/varieties` with nothing selected needs a defined state.
➡️ Recommendation: select the first variety in the list automatically, so the right pane is never empty. A business with no varieties shows the list's fixed empty state inviting the seller to add the first variety, and the right pane stays blank.

❓ **Q3** - **"Ver todas" on the variety page's single-setting sections**: Your request says every section has "its last 3 rows and Ver todas". That fits Stock y entradas and Precios, which are lists. Aviso de stock bajo (one threshold) and En el catálogo (emoji, % índica, aroma, shown) are one value or one set of fields. They have no rows.
➡️ Recommendation: only Stock y entradas and Precios carry "Ver todas". The other two sections show their current values with an edit action, and nothing else. The PRD will say it that way.

❓ **Q4** - **Feedback after Compartir**: The docs say closing the share sheet without sending changes nothing, and Copiar shows "Catálogo copiado" or "No se pudo copiar.". They do not say what Compartir does after it succeeds or fails.
➡️ Recommendation: success and cancel show nothing, because the share sheet is its own feedback and a toast would add noise. A real failure, one that is not the seller cancelling, shows the fixed line "No se pudo compartir." and leaves Copiar available. Neither message carries any of the catalog text.

### Round 2: answers

**Q1**: Accepted, with limits. This applies the condition from round 1 (a seller is never left unable to connect the bot or edit the header and footer):
- **No rework**: the bot settings (token, channel chat, own chat, Probarlo) and the header and footer editing keep working exactly as they do today. This issue does not redesign them.
- **Reached from the catalog screen**: they sit in one temporary place reached from the new catalog screen. "Editar encabezado y pie" and the link shown when Publicar is unavailable both open it.
- **No new entries**: it gets no tab, Más entry, Configuración group or "coming soon" mark.
- **Replaced later**: Configuración's own issue moves the two link targets to Mensaje del catálogo and Integraciones, and removes the temporary place.
- **Status and Publicar stay on the catalog screen**: the temporary place shows no pin status and no Publicar, as Integraciones will (one fact, one place).
- **The PRD names it**: it lists this as a deliberate, temporary exception to catalog.md's "the catalog screen edits no header or footer" and to "Integraciones is the only place".

**Q2**: Accepted, on a computer only.
- **Something picked**: opening Variedades with nothing selected shows the first variety by name in the right pane, so a figure is always in view.
- **Nothing to pick**: a business with no varieties shows the list's fixed empty state, and the right pane shows nothing that could be read as data.
- **The phone**: it never opens a variety on its own, and the list is the screen.
- **A gone variety**: one picked that is gone follows accounts.md's "Eso ya no está." rule.

**Q3**: Accepted. Only Stock y entradas and Precios carry their last 3 rows and "Ver todas". Aviso de stock bajo and En el catálogo show their current values and their edit action, and nothing else. My request's wording was loose, and the PRD says it this way.

**Q4**: Accepted.
- **Success or cancel**: nothing shows, because the share sheet is its own feedback.
- **A real failure** (not the seller cancelling): it shows the fixed "No se pudo compartir." and leaves Copiar available.
- **No data in either**: neither message carries any of the catalog text, a name or an amount.
- **No toast**: there is no "Catálogo compartido". Accounts.md's rule keeps toasts short and fixed, and this one adds nothing.

### Closing

### Closing summary

Shared understanding is reached. Nothing is left that would change the PRD.

**Job** (settled in round 1)
- **Job story**: when a customer asks what I have or what it costs, or flower comes in, runs out or gets a new price, the seller wants everything about one variety on its own page. They also want the list to show at a glance what they have, what is running out and what they can't sell. And they want today's catalog one tap away and shareable from the phone. The outcome is that they answer, record stock and send an up-to-date catalog without moving between screens, and customers never see a stale price or a variety that can't be sold.
- **Pain today**: one variety is split across three screens. Sending the catalog means copying text and switching apps. The list doesn't show what is running out or can't be sold.
- **Success criteria**:
  1. The catalog screen and the stock entry form open in at most 2 taps from any tab root. An entry started from a variety's page has that variety chosen, with "Stock inicial" preselected only when the variety has no entries.
  2. One variety, one page, and no fact is editable on two screens.
  3. Compartir hands the share sheet exactly the text Copiar copies. Without a share sheet, Compartir is hidden and Copiar remains.
  4. Every status is told by word and shape, and the summary line is never blank. Every variety left out of the catalog shows one reason, in words.
  5. Nothing is lost and nothing is invented. Every old path redirects permanently, and no link points at a screen that doesn't exist.
  6. Businesses never mix (ADR-0004).

**Behaviour decisions** (rounds 1 and 2)
- **Routes**: they are the ones in `scripts/design/screens.json`. This issue moves the catalog screen to its English route under Variedades, with the permanent redirect from `/catalogo`. Shared paths with the API follow ADR-0024.
- **Bot settings and header and footer** (Configuración doesn't exist yet): they keep working as today, in one temporary place reached from the catalog screen.
  - "Editar encabezado y pie" and Publicar's "unavailable" link both open it.
  - It gets no tab, Más entry, Configuración group or "coming soon" mark.
  - It shows no pin status and no Publicar.
  - Configuración's own issue replaces the two link targets and removes the place.
  - The PRD names it as a deliberate, temporary exception to catalog.md's and pinned-catalog.md's "only in Configuración" rules.
- **Two panes on a computer**: the first variety by name is selected automatically. With no varieties, the list's empty state shows and the right pane carries no data. The phone never opens a variety on its own.
- **A variety's page**: only Stock y entradas and Precios carry their last 3 rows and "Ver todas". Aviso de stock bajo and En el catálogo show their values and an edit action.
- **Compartir feedback**: success and cancel show nothing. A real failure shows the fixed "No se pudo compartir." and leaves Copiar available. Neither message carries data, and there is no toast.
- **Removed**: the Stock tab, the old Catálogo screen and the old Variedades screens are gone, and their paths redirect.

**Out of scope**
- Configuración and its groups.
- A redesign of the bot settings or of the header and footer.
- Other areas.

## Product Analyst

### Job to be done

**Job story (PM's wording).** **When** a customer asks me what I have or how much something costs, or flower comes in, runs out or gets a new price, **I want** to open one variety and find everything about it on its own page: its stock and entries, its prices, its low-stock warning and how it shows in the catalog. I also want the list to tell me at a glance what I have, what is running out and what I can't sell. And I want today's catalog one tap from that list, ready to share from my phone. **So I can** answer the customer, record what came in and send an up-to-date catalog without moving between screens, and my customers never get a stale price or a variety I can't sell.

- The main situation is mid-chat, between orders. Flower arriving, running out or being repriced comes second.
- This is the navigation job (navigation.md) applied to varieties, stock and the catalog, not a new job. The stock and catalog jobs stay in stock.md and catalog.md. The pinned catalog's upkeep stays in pinned-catalog.md. The computer is the same job on two panes.

**The pain today.**
- One variety is split across three screens: Stock, Variedades and Catálogo.
- Sending the catalog means copying the text and switching apps.
- The list does not show, on its own, what is running out or can't be sold.

**Success criteria (the seller's).**
1. **Two taps at most.** From any tab root, the catalog screen ("Mandar catálogo") and the stock entry form ("+ Entrada de stock") open in at most 2 taps. A computer takes no more taps than the phone. An entry started from a variety's page has that variety chosen, and "Stock inicial" preselected only when the variety has no entries.
2. **One variety, one page.** Stock, status, days left and today's price are read from the variety's page. Its prices, threshold and catalog attributes are edited there and nowhere else. No fact is editable on two screens.
3. **Sharing in one tap.** On the catalog screen, Compartir hands the share sheet exactly the text Copiar copies, `**` markers included. Without a share sheet, Compartir is not shown and Copiar remains. It works on the phone over the home network's plain http.
4. **Nothing guessed.** Every status on the list and the page is told by shape and word, never by color alone. The summary line is never blank. Every variety left out of the catalog shows exactly one reason, in words.
5. **Nothing lost, nothing invented.** Everything a seller could do on Stock, the old Catálogo and the old Variedades is still reachable. Every old path redirects permanently. No link points at a screen that does not exist.
6. **Businesses never mix** (ADR-0004): nothing here shows another business's data.

**Asked for, but left out because it does not serve the job.**
- Configuración and its groups (its own issue).
- Any redesign of the bot settings or of the header and footer editing.
- Any other area of the app.
- The customer channel: it was already removed by customers.md, so it is not this issue's exception.
- Assumption (PM): this issue removes nothing beyond the screens the request names.

### Competitors

No real data. I did not study any competing product for this PRD, and I will not invent one. The criteria above come from the product's own docs and from what PM decided, not from a comparison. If PM wants a benchmark (for example, how other small-seller tools share a catalog from a phone), it needs a separate study.

### Behaviour definition

**Routes and redirects**
- Routes are the ones fixed in `scripts/design/screens.json`: `/varieties`, `/varieties/{low}`, `/varieties/catalog`, `/varieties/entries`, `/varieties/entries/new`, `/varieties/{low}/prices`, `/varieties/{low}/prices/new`, `/varieties/new`.
- This issue moves the catalog screen to its English route under Variedades. `/catalogo` redirects to it permanently.
- The old Variedades and Stock paths (`/variedades`, `/mi-stock`, `/mi-stock/nueva-entrada` and the others still in the registry) redirect permanently to their new route. Each redirect is tested one by one.
- Where a front path shares its first segment with the API (`/varieties`), it is served through ADR-0024's exact allowlist. This change adds the entry for each such path.
- The Stock tab, the old Catálogo screen and the old Variedades screens no longer exist.

**The list (Variedades)**
- A tab root. On the phone it is the list alone. On a computer it is two panes: the list on the left, the selected variety's page on the right.
- It shows every variety, hidden ones included, sorted by name. Each row shows:
  - the name;
  - its stock status mark and word (square = agotada, filled circle = baja, empty circle = ok, "Sin entradas" when it has none) and its detail line;
  - "Oculta" beside the status when hidden, never in place of it;
  - grams and days left;
  - today's list price per gram, or "Sin precio: no se puede vender".
- **Summary line** above the list:
  - with no varieties: no summary line, and the fixed empty state inviting the seller to add the first one;
  - with every variety ok: "N variedades · todo ok";
  - otherwise: each status that is not ok, counted by word, in stock-status order. It is never blank.
- **On top**: "Mandar catálogo". It is disabled, with its reason in words, only while the first-run checklist shows and nothing qualifies.
- **Secondary button** above the list: "+ Entrada de stock", which opens the entry form with the variety to be chosen.
- **"Nueva variedad"** adds a variety. "Todas las entradas" sits at the foot.
- **Phone**: opening the list never opens a variety on its own.
- **Computer, first open**: with nothing selected, the first variety by name is selected automatically, so the right pane always shows a figure. With no varieties, the right pane carries nothing that could be read as data.
- **A selected variety that is gone** follows accounts.md's "Eso ya no está." rule.

**A variety's page**
- A detail: the bar or sidebar and "‹ Variedades", without "+ Nueva venta".
- **One summary line** at the top: grams, status, days left, today's list price, and "Oculta" when hidden.
- **Two equal buttons**: "+ Entrada" (the stock entry form with this variety chosen, and "Stock inicial" preselected only when the variety has no entries) and "Nuevo precio".
- **Four sections:**
  - **Stock y entradas** and **Precios** are lists. Each shows its last 3 rows and "Ver todas" for the full list.
  - **Aviso de stock bajo** is one value. It shows the current threshold and its edit action, with no "Ver todas".
  - **En el catálogo** is one set of fields (shown or hidden, emoji, % índica, aroma/sabor). It shows the current values and their edit action, with no "Ver todas". It is the only place these attributes are edited.
- Rename lives here. Price deletion follows varieties.md, with "Antes de borrar" and copy that says what takes the entry's place.
- Forms opened from here have "Cerrar" and ask before discarding something typed.
- Toasts: "Variedad guardada", "Precio guardado", "Precio eliminado", "Aviso guardado", "Entrada guardada", "Catálogo guardado". None carries data.
- Refusals show inline and keep what was typed.

**The catalog screen ("Mandar catálogo")**
- A detail ("‹ Variedades"). Reached from "Mandar catálogo" on Variedades and from Inicio's "Tu catálogo en Telegram está desactualizado" notice. It always opens, including when nothing qualifies.
- **"Cómo se ve en Telegram"**: the text as a customer reads it, with bold following catalog.md's left-to-right `**` pairing. When nothing qualifies it shows the empty state saying nothing is in the catalog today.
- **Compartir** (main action):
  - It opens the device's native share sheet with exactly the text Copiar copies, `**` markers included.
  - Where the browser offers no share sheet, it is not shown and Copiar remains.
  - **Success or cancel**: nothing shows. The share sheet is its own feedback, and there is no toast.
  - **A real failure** (not the seller cancelling): the fixed line "No se pudo compartir.", with Copiar still available.
  - Neither message carries catalog text, a name or an amount.
- **Copiar** (fallback): it copies the raw text and shows the toast "Catálogo copiado". A failure shows "No se pudo copiar." and no toast. It works over plain http on the home network.
- **Pinned catalog status and Publicar**: "Al día" or "Desactualizado" with its fixed reason, and Publicar. When Publicar is unavailable, it says why in words and links to the temporary place below.
- **What does not go out, and why**: every variety out of today's catalog, by name, with one reason, the first that applies: oculta, sin precio, then agotada or sin entradas. Told by word, never by color alone.
- **Links**: each variety named in the preview or in the left-out list opens that variety's "En el catálogo" section. "Editar encabezado y pie" opens the temporary place below.

**Temporary place for the bot settings and the header and footer** (a deliberate, temporary exception)
- Configuración does not exist yet. Until it does, no seller may lose the way to connect the bot or edit the header and footer.
- They keep working exactly as today, with no rework: token, channel chat, own chat, Probarlo, and the header and footer.
- They sit in one temporary place reached only from the catalog screen. "Editar encabezado y pie" and Publicar's "unavailable" link both open it.
- It is a form: "Cerrar", and a confirmation when leaving with unsaved changes.
- It gets no tab, Más entry, Configuración group or "coming soon" mark.
- It shows no pin status and no Publicar. Those stay on the catalog screen.
- The PRD records this as an exception to catalog.md's "header and footer are edited only in Configuración" and to pinned-catalog.md's "Integraciones is the only place". It must also not be read as breaking navigation.md's "catalog screen edits no header or footer": the screen links out, and the editing happens in the temporary place.
- Configuración's own issue replaces the two link targets with Configuración → Mensaje del catálogo and Configuración → Integraciones, and removes the temporary place.
- If Configuración ships first, this issue links to it directly and the temporary place is not built.

**Empty states and errors**
- No varieties: the list's fixed empty state, and no summary line.
- No entries at all: "Sin entradas de stock · Cargá tu stock inicial o una compra".
- Nothing qualifies for the catalog: the catalog screen opens with its empty state, and the pinned catalog keeps its own empty message and "Al día".
- A variety, entry or price that is gone: accounts.md's "Eso ya no está." rule, with "Volver a Variedades".
- No network shows "Sin conexión". A server error shows the generic retry.
- Another business's variety opened by id shows the same "Eso ya no está." as a deleted one.

**Unchanged**
- The rules of stock, status, days left, prices, catalog text, catalog attributes, the pinned catalog, upkeep and isolation do not change. Only where they are reached and shown changes.
- Language is Rioplatense Spanish with vos. Money is `$ 11.000`, grams `3,5 g`, dates `dd/mm`. The catalog text keeps `$11.000`. No state is told by color alone, in either theme.

### Metrics and dashboard

There is no telemetry in this product. Metrics are tests and by-eye checks, as in the other capabilities. Each derives from a success criterion.

- **Two taps (1)**: the end-to-end run, on phone and computer viewports, counts the taps from every tab root to the catalog screen and to the stock entry form. 100% within 2. A computer never takes more taps than the phone. An entry started from a variety's page has that variety chosen, and "Stock inicial" preselected only when it has no entries.
- **One page (2)**: front tests and a check against the docs and the design show no other screen edits a variety's prices, threshold or catalog attributes. The end-to-end run adds a variety, adds a price, renames, deletes a price, sets and turns off a threshold, and edits the catalog attributes, all from the variety's page.
- **Sections (2)**: front tests show only Stock y entradas and Precios carry "Ver todas" and their last 3 rows. Aviso de stock bajo and En el catálogo show values and an edit action only.
- **Share = copy (3)**: with a stubbed share sheet, Compartir hands exactly the API's text. With none available, Compartir is not shown and Copiar works. Cancel shows nothing. A forced failure shows "No se pudo compartir." and leaves Copiar. No message holds catalog text.
- **Status told by word and shape (4)**: front tests cover no varieties, all ok and a mix for the summary line, and each status with "Oculta" beside it. Every variety left out of the catalog has exactly one reason, in priority order. Color checked by eye in both themes.
- **Always opens (4)**: the catalog screen opens with nothing qualifying, and "Mandar catálogo" is disabled only while the checklist shows and nothing qualifies.
- **Two panes**: on a computer viewport, opening Variedades with nothing selected shows the first variety by name. With no varieties, the right pane holds no figure. On the phone, opening the list never opens a variety.
- **Nothing lost (5)**:
  - every old path redirects permanently, tested one by one, `/catalogo` included;
  - the shared-path allowlist entries (ADR-0024) are covered by the existing contract test;
  - every action from the old screens maps to a place in the new ones;
  - no link points at a missing screen.
- **Temporary place (5)**: a front test shows the bot settings and the header and footer still save from it, both links open it, and it shows no pin status and no Publicar. Another shows no tab, Más or Configuración entry points to it. The end-to-end run connects the bot with Probarlo, publishes from the catalog screen and edits the header and footer through it.
- **Isolation (6)**: no new route answers business data. The existing routes stay in the isolation sweep. Another business's variety opened by id shows "Eso ya no está.". After a full front run, the console and browser storage hold no names, amounts or catalog text.
- **Design**: `scripts/design/compare.sh` runs before and after for the Variedades list, the variety's page and the catalog screen, phone and computer. The comparisons are attached to the PR and judged by eye. Any wording difference between the design and the docs is listed in the PR.
- **Speed**: Variedades and the catalog screen answer in under 300 ms with a few dozen varieties.
- **No regression**: the existing suite passes with no test edited to fit the new look, other than tests of the removed screens.

### Flags

- `touches_ui`: yes. Variedades' list, a variety's page and the catalog screen are redrawn, and the Stock tab and the old screens are removed.
- `touches_copy`: yes. It adds "No se pudo compartir.", the temporary place's wording, and the summary and section texts of the new screens.

### For the next role

PM, please validate these points:
1. **The temporary place** is named as a deliberate exception, with its limits: no rework, no new entries, no status or Publicar, and it is removed when Configuración ships. If Configuración ships first, the place is not built.
2. **Tension to confirm**: navigation.md says the catalog screen edits no header or footer, and the PRD keeps that by having the screen only link out. Please confirm that reading.
3. **Competitors**: I have no real data, so the section says so instead of inventing any.
4. **Assumption (yours)**: this issue removes nothing beyond the screens named in the request.
5. **Wording to settle in design**: the temporary place's title and the exact reason texts are design's call. Only "No se pudo compartir." is fixed here.

## PM (v1)

### Verdict

`approved`: the PRD matches every decision from both interview rounds and serves the job story as worded. The gaps left are small, and I closed them below.

### Adjustments

1. **Out of scope section added.** It existed only as the Analyst's "left out" list, so I moved it into the PRD.
2. **Where the header and footer are edited (Analyst's point 2): confirmed.** The catalog screen itself edits nothing; it only links out. navigation.md's rule holds. The temporary place is an exception only to:
   - catalog.md's "header and footer edited only in Configuración";
   - pinned-catalog.md's "Integraciones is the only place".
3. **Leaving the temporary place.** It is a form, so "Cerrar" goes back by history. With no history, it goes to the catalog screen. The PRD did not say where it returns to.
4. **"Ver todas" lists.** These are cards or details, as navigation.md classifies them: the bar or sidebar and "‹ <variety>", with no "+ Nueva venta".
5. **Routes.**
   - The PRD now states that navigation.md's "one screen has not moved yet" paragraph ends with this issue.
   - `/catalogo` and `/mi-stock/nueva-entrada` are among the redirects tested one by one.
   - An unknown address still goes to Inicio.
6. **Two panes on a computer.** The automatic selection of the first variety does not count as a tap. It is also not a step the seller has to undo: picking another variety simply replaces it.
7. **Toasts.**
   - "Entrada guardada" belongs to the entry form, wherever it was opened from.
   - Compartir adds no toast.
8. **Competitors.** Saying there is no data, instead of inventing any, is accepted. It blocks nothing.
9. **Assumption confirmed:** nothing is removed beyond the screens named in the request.

### Validated flags

- `touches_ui`: **yes**. Variedades' list and page, the two panes and the catalog screen are redrawn. The Stock tab and the old screens go.
- `touches_copy`: **yes**. "No se pudo compartir." is new, plus the new screens' summary and section texts and the temporary place's wording.

### PRD

#### Job to be done

**When** a customer asks me what I have or how much something costs, or flower comes in, runs out or gets a new price, **I want** to open one variety and find everything about it on its own page: its stock and entries, its prices, its low-stock warning and how it shows in the catalog. I also want the list to tell me at a glance what I have, what is running out and what I can't sell. And I want today's catalog one tap from that list, ready to share from my phone. **So I can** answer the customer, record what came in and send an up-to-date catalog without moving between screens, and my customers never get a stale price or a variety I can't sell.

- **The main situation** is mid-chat, between orders. Flower arriving, running out or being repriced comes second.
- **Not a new job.** This is the navigation job (navigation.md) applied to varieties, stock and the catalog.
  - The stock and catalog jobs stay in stock.md and catalog.md.
  - The pinned catalog's upkeep stays in pinned-catalog.md.
  - The computer is the same job, on two panes.

**The pain today:**
- one variety is split across three screens (Stock, Variedades, Catálogo);
- sending the catalog means copying the text and switching apps;
- the list does not show, on its own, what is running out or can't be sold.

**Success criteria:**

1. **Two taps at most.**
   - From any tab root, the catalog screen ("Mandar catálogo") and the stock entry form ("+ Entrada de stock") open in at most 2 taps.
   - A computer never takes more taps than the phone.
   - An entry started from a variety's page has that variety chosen. "Stock inicial" is preselected only when the variety has no entries.
2. **One variety, one page.**
   - Stock, status, days left and today's price are read from the variety's page.
   - Its prices, threshold and catalog attributes are edited there and nowhere else.
   - No fact is editable on two screens.
3. **Sharing in one tap.**
   - Compartir hands the share sheet exactly the text Copiar copies, `**` markers included.
   - Without a share sheet, Compartir is not shown and Copiar remains.
   - It works on the phone over the home network's plain http.
4. **Nothing guessed.**
   - Every status is told by shape and word, never by color alone.
   - The summary line is never blank.
   - Every variety left out of the catalog shows exactly one reason, in words.
5. **Nothing lost, nothing invented.**
   - Everything a seller could do on Stock, the old Catálogo and the old Variedades is still reachable.
   - Every old path redirects permanently.
   - No link points at a screen that does not exist.
6. **Businesses never mix** (ADR-0004).

#### Competitors

There is no real data, and none was studied. The criteria come from the product's own docs and PM's decisions.

#### Behaviour

**Routes and redirects**

- **Routes** are those in `scripts/design/screens.json`: `/varieties`, `/varieties/{low}`, `/varieties/catalog`, `/varieties/entries`, `/varieties/entries/new`, `/varieties/{low}/prices`, `/varieties/{low}/prices/new` and `/varieties/new`.
- **The catalog screen moves** to `/varieties/catalog`, and `/catalogo` redirects there permanently. This ends navigation.md's "one screen has not moved yet" exception.
- **Old paths** redirect permanently to their new routes, each tested one by one: `/variedades`, `/mi-stock`, `/mi-stock/nueva-entrada`, `/catalogo` and every other old Stock, Variedades or Catálogo path still in the registry.
- **Unknown addresses** still go to Inicio.
- **Paths shared with the API** (first segment `varieties`) are served under ADR-0024. Each such path's entry is added in the same change.
- **Removed**: the Stock tab, the old Catálogo screen and the old Variedades screens.

**The list (Variedades)**

- **A tab root.** On the phone, the list alone. On a computer, two panes: the list on the left, the selected variety's page on the right.
- **Rows**: every variety, hidden ones included, sorted by name. Each row shows:
  - the name;
  - the status mark and word: square = agotada, filled circle = baja, empty circle = ok, or "Sin entradas";
  - its detail line;
  - "Oculta" beside the status when hidden, never in its place;
  - grams and days left;
  - today's list price per gram, or "Sin precio: no se puede vender".
- **Summary line** above the list:
  - with no varieties: none, and the fixed empty state inviting the seller to add the first one;
  - with every variety ok: "N variedades · todo ok";
  - otherwise: each status that is not ok, counted by word, in stock-status order.

  It is never blank.
- **"Mandar catálogo"** sits on top. It is disabled, with its reason in words, only while the first-run checklist shows and nothing qualifies.
- **"+ Entrada de stock"** is a secondary button above the list. It opens the entry form with the variety still to be chosen.
- **"Nueva variedad"** adds a variety. **"Todas las entradas"** sits at the foot.
- **On the phone**, opening the list never opens a variety on its own.
- **On a computer**:
  - with nothing selected, the first variety by name is selected automatically. That counts as no tap, and picking another variety replaces it;
  - with no varieties, the right pane holds nothing that could be read as data.
- **A selected variety that is gone** follows accounts.md's "Eso ya no está." rule.

**A variety's page**

- **A detail**: the bar or sidebar and "‹ Variedades", without "+ Nueva venta".
- **One summary line**: grams, status, days left and today's list price, with "Oculta" when hidden.
- **Two equal buttons**:
  - "+ Entrada": this variety chosen, and "Stock inicial" preselected only with no entries;
  - "Nuevo precio".
- **Sections**:
  - **Stock y entradas** and **Precios**: their last 3 rows and "Ver todas". A "Ver todas" list is a card or detail: the bar or sidebar and "‹ <variety>", with no "+ Nueva venta".
  - **Aviso de stock bajo**: the current threshold and its edit action only.
  - **En el catálogo**: shown or hidden, emoji, % índica and aroma/sabor, with their edit action only. It is the only place these attributes are edited.
- **Rename** lives here. **Deleting a price** follows varieties.md ("Antes de borrar", saying what takes the entry's place).
- **Forms** opened from here have "Cerrar" and ask before discarding something typed.
- **Toasts carry no data**:
  - "Variedad guardada", "Precio guardado", "Precio eliminado", "Aviso guardado" and "Catálogo guardado";
  - "Entrada guardada", from the entry form wherever it opened.
- **Refusals** show inline and keep what was typed.

**The catalog screen ("Mandar catálogo", `/varieties/catalog`)**

- **A detail** ("‹ Variedades").
  - Reached from "Mandar catálogo" and from Inicio's "Tu catálogo en Telegram está desactualizado" notice.
  - It always opens, including when nothing qualifies.
- **"Cómo se ve en Telegram"**: the text with left-to-right `**` pairing. When nothing qualifies, the empty state.
- **Compartir** (the main action):
  - it opens the native share sheet with exactly the text Copiar copies;
  - where the browser has no share sheet, it is not shown;
  - success or cancel shows nothing, and there is no toast;
  - a real failure shows the fixed "No se pudo compartir." and leaves Copiar available;
  - no message carries catalog text, a name or an amount.
- **Copiar** (the fallback):
  - it copies the raw text and shows the toast "Catálogo copiado";
  - a failure shows "No se pudo copiar." and no toast;
  - it works over plain http.
- **Pin status and Publicar**: "Al día" or "Desactualizado" with its fixed reason, and Publicar. When Publicar is unavailable, it says why in words and links to where the bot settings are: the temporary place below, or Configuración → Integraciones once that exists.
- **What does not go out, and why**: every variety out of today's catalog, with one reason, the first that applies: oculta, sin precio, then agotada or sin entradas. Told by word.
- **Links**:
  - each variety named opens its "En el catálogo" section;
  - "Editar encabezado y pie" opens the temporary place, or Configuración → Mensaje del catálogo once that exists.
- **The screen itself edits no header, footer or bot setting.** It only links out (navigation.md).

**Temporary place for the bot settings and the header and footer** (a deliberate, temporary exception)

- **Why**: until Configuración → Integraciones and Mensaje del catálogo exist, no seller may lose the way to connect the bot or edit the header and footer.
- **No rework**: they work exactly as today (token, channel chat, own chat, Probarlo, header and footer).
- **Reached only from the catalog screen**: "Editar encabezado y pie" and Publicar's "unavailable" link both open it.
- **A form**:
  - "Cerrar" goes back by history, and with no history to the catalog screen;
  - it confirms before leaving with unsaved changes.
- **No entries**: no tab, Más entry, Configuración group or "coming soon" mark.
- **No status**: it shows no pin status and no Publicar.
- **The exception it makes**: to catalog.md's "header and footer only in Configuración" and pinned-catalog.md's "Integraciones is the only place", for as long as those groups do not exist.
- **Removal**: Configuración's own issue retargets both links and removes the temporary place. If Configuración ships first, the temporary place is not built and the links go straight to Configuración.

**Empty states and errors**

- No varieties: the list's fixed empty state, and no summary line.
- No entries at all: "Sin entradas de stock · Cargá tu stock inicial o una compra".
- Nothing qualifies: the catalog screen opens with its empty state. The pin keeps its own empty message and "Al día".
- A variety, entry or price that is gone, or another business's variety opened by id: "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades".
- No network: "Sin conexión". A server error: the generic retry.

**Unchanged**

- No rule of stock, status, days left, prices, catalog text, catalog attributes, the pinned catalog, upkeep or isolation changes. Only where they are reached and shown changes.
- Language and formats: Rioplatense Spanish with vos; `$ 11.000`, `3,5 g`, `dd/mm`. The catalog text keeps `$11.000`.
- No state is told by color alone, in either theme.

#### Metrics

There is no telemetry. Each metric is a test or a by-eye check.

- **Two taps (1)**:
  - the end-to-end run, on phone and computer viewports, counts the taps from every tab root to the catalog screen and to the stock entry form: 100% within 2, and a computer never more than the phone;
  - an entry started from a variety's page has the variety chosen, with "Stock inicial" preselected only when it has no entries.
- **One page (2)**:
  - no other screen edits a variety's prices, threshold or catalog attributes;
  - the end-to-end run adds a variety, adds a price, renames, deletes a price, sets and turns off a threshold, and edits the catalog attributes, all from the page.
- **Sections (2)**: only Stock y entradas and Precios carry their last 3 rows and "Ver todas". The "Ver todas" lists show the card chrome.
- **Share = copy (3)**:
  - with a stubbed share sheet, Compartir hands exactly the API's text;
  - with none, Compartir is not shown and Copiar works;
  - cancel shows nothing;
  - a forced failure shows "No se pudo compartir." and leaves Copiar;
  - no message holds catalog text.
- **Status by word and shape (4)**:
  - the summary line is covered for no varieties, all ok and a mix;
  - each status shows its mark and word, with "Oculta" beside it;
  - every left-out variety has exactly one reason, in priority order;
  - color is checked by eye in both themes.
- **Always opens (4)**: the catalog screen opens with nothing qualifying. "Mandar catálogo" is disabled only while the checklist shows and nothing qualifies.
- **Two panes**:
  - on a computer, nothing selected shows the first variety by name;
  - no varieties leaves the right pane with no figure;
  - on the phone, the list never opens a variety.
- **Nothing lost (5)**:
  - every old path, `/catalogo` included, redirects permanently, tested one by one;
  - the ADR-0024 allowlist is covered by the contract test;
  - every old action maps to a new place;
  - no link points at a missing screen.
- **Temporary place (5)**:
  - the bot settings and the header and footer save from it;
  - both links open it;
  - it shows no pin status and no Publicar;
  - no tab, Más or Configuración entry points to it;
  - the end-to-end run connects the bot with Probarlo, publishes from the catalog screen and edits the header and footer through it.

  If Configuración shipped first, the same checks run against its groups instead.
- **Isolation (6)**:
  - no new route answers business data;
  - another business's variety shows "Eso ya no está.";
  - after a full front run, the console and browser storage hold no names, amounts or catalog text.
- **Design**: `scripts/design/compare.sh` runs before and after for the list, the page and the catalog screen, on phone and computer. The comparisons are attached to the PR, and every design-vs-docs wording difference is listed there.
- **Speed**: Variedades and the catalog screen answer in under 300 ms with a few dozen varieties.
- **No regression**: the existing suite passes with no test edited to fit the new look, apart from tests of the removed screens.

#### Out of scope

- Configuración and its groups (its own issue).
- Any redesign of the bot settings or of the header and footer editing.
- Any other area of the app.
- Removing anything beyond the screens named.
- A competitor study.

### For the next role

For the Tech Lead:

1. **Route shape: decide before building.** ADR-0024 allows only exact paths. `/varieties/{low}` and `/varieties/{low}/prices(/new)` carry a parameter, and `/varieties/{id}` is also an API path, so an exact allowlist cannot list them. The product requires that a reload on any of these routes opens the screen and never the API's JSON. ADR-0024 is accepted and binding, so if it can't serve parameterised paths, the way out is an amendment or a new ADR that says how, with the same `Accept` gate. Neither I nor this PRD changes ADR-0024.
2. **The temporary place is conditional.**
   - Check whether Configuración → Integraciones and Mensaje del catálogo exist when you plan. If they do, don't build the temporary place: link to them.
   - If you build it, reuse today's bot and header/footer components as they are. It gets no new route entry in navigation and no Configuración group.
3. **Compartir.**
   - Tell a cancel (`AbortError`) apart from a real failure.
   - Feature-detect `navigator.share`. It must hand exactly `GET /catalog`'s `text`, per ADR-0022.
   - No catalog text goes to the console or storage.
4. **No rule changes in the domain.** No API or domain behaviour of stock, prices, catalog or the pinned catalog changes. This is front structure, routes and redirects.
5. **Docs.** Updated definitions are attached for varieties, catalog, pinned-catalog and navigation (routes: the catalog screen moved, `/catalogo` redirects). There is no glossary change.

## PM

### Verdict

`approved`: the PRD matches every decision from both interview rounds and serves the job story as worded. The gaps left are small, and I closed them below.

### Adjustments

1. **Out of scope section added.** It existed only as the Analyst's "left out" list, so I moved it into the PRD.
2. **Where the header and footer are edited (Analyst's point 2): confirmed.** The catalog screen itself edits nothing; it only links out. navigation.md's rule holds. The temporary place is an exception only to:
   - catalog.md's "header and footer edited only in Configuración";
   - pinned-catalog.md's "Integraciones is the only place".
3. **Leaving the temporary place.** It is a form, so "Cerrar" goes back by history. With no history, it goes to the catalog screen. The PRD did not say where it returns to.
4. **"Ver todas" lists.** These are cards or details, as navigation.md classifies them: the bar or sidebar and "‹ <variety>", with no "+ Nueva venta".
5. **Routes.**
   - The PRD now states that navigation.md's "one screen has not moved yet" paragraph ends with this issue.
   - `/catalogo` and `/mi-stock/nueva-entrada` are among the redirects tested one by one.
   - An unknown address still goes to Inicio.
6. **Two panes on a computer.** The automatic selection of the first variety does not count as a tap. It is also not a step the seller has to undo: picking another variety simply replaces it.
7. **Toasts.**
   - "Entrada guardada" belongs to the entry form, wherever it was opened from.
   - Compartir adds no toast.
8. **Competitors.** Saying there is no data, instead of inventing any, is accepted. It blocks nothing.
9. **Assumption confirmed:** nothing is removed beyond the screens named in the request.

### Validated flags

- `touches_ui`: **yes**. Variedades' list and page, the two panes and the catalog screen are redrawn. The Stock tab and the old screens go.
- `touches_copy`: **yes**. "No se pudo compartir." is new, plus the new screens' summary and section texts and the temporary place's wording.

### PRD

#### Job to be done

**When** a customer asks me what I have or how much something costs, or flower comes in, runs out or gets a new price, **I want** to open one variety and find everything about it on its own page: its stock and entries, its prices, its low-stock warning and how it shows in the catalog. I also want the list to tell me at a glance what I have, what is running out and what I can't sell. And I want today's catalog one tap from that list, ready to share from my phone. **So I can** answer the customer, record what came in and send an up-to-date catalog without moving between screens, and my customers never get a stale price or a variety I can't sell.

- **The main situation** is mid-chat, between orders. Flower arriving, running out or being repriced comes second.
- **Not a new job.** This is the navigation job (navigation.md) applied to varieties, stock and the catalog.
  - The stock and catalog jobs stay in stock.md and catalog.md.
  - The pinned catalog's upkeep stays in pinned-catalog.md.
  - The computer is the same job, on two panes.

**The pain today:**
- one variety is split across three screens (Stock, Variedades, Catálogo);
- sending the catalog means copying the text and switching apps;
- the list does not show, on its own, what is running out or can't be sold.

**Success criteria:**

1. **Two taps at most.**
   - From any tab root, the catalog screen ("Mandar catálogo") and the stock entry form ("+ Entrada de stock") open in at most 2 taps.
   - A computer never takes more taps than the phone.
   - An entry started from a variety's page has that variety chosen. "Stock inicial" is preselected only when the variety has no entries.
2. **One variety, one page.**
   - Stock, status, days left and today's price are read from the variety's page.
   - Its prices, threshold and catalog attributes are edited there and nowhere else.
   - No fact is editable on two screens.
3. **Sharing in one tap.**
   - Compartir hands the share sheet exactly the text Copiar copies, `**` markers included.
   - Without a share sheet, Compartir is not shown and Copiar remains.
   - It works on the phone over the home network's plain http.
4. **Nothing guessed.**
   - Every status is told by shape and word, never by color alone.
   - The summary line is never blank.
   - Every variety left out of the catalog shows exactly one reason, in words.
5. **Nothing lost, nothing invented.**
   - Everything a seller could do on Stock, the old Catálogo and the old Variedades is still reachable.
   - Every old path redirects permanently.
   - No link points at a screen that does not exist.
6. **Businesses never mix** (ADR-0004).

#### Competitors

There is no real data, and none was studied. The criteria come from the product's own docs and PM's decisions.

#### Behaviour

**Routes and redirects**

- **Routes** are those in `scripts/design/screens.json`: `/varieties`, `/varieties/{low}`, `/varieties/catalog`, `/varieties/entries`, `/varieties/entries/new`, `/varieties/{low}/prices`, `/varieties/{low}/prices/new` and `/varieties/new`.
- **The catalog screen moves** to `/varieties/catalog`, and `/catalogo` redirects there permanently. This ends navigation.md's "one screen has not moved yet" exception.
- **Old paths** redirect permanently to their new routes, each tested one by one: `/variedades`, `/mi-stock`, `/mi-stock/nueva-entrada`, `/catalogo` and every other old Stock, Variedades or Catálogo path still in the registry.
- **Unknown addresses** still go to Inicio.
- **Paths shared with the API** (first segment `varieties`) are served under ADR-0024. Each such path's entry is added in the same change.
- **Removed**: the Stock tab, the old Catálogo screen and the old Variedades screens.

**The list (Variedades)**

- **A tab root.** On the phone, the list alone. On a computer, two panes: the list on the left, the selected variety's page on the right.
- **Rows**: every variety, hidden ones included, sorted by name. Each row shows:
  - the name;
  - the status mark and word: square = agotada, filled circle = baja, empty circle = ok, or "Sin entradas";
  - its detail line;
  - "Oculta" beside the status when hidden, never in its place;
  - grams and days left;
  - today's list price per gram, or "Sin precio: no se puede vender".
- **Summary line** above the list:
  - with no varieties: none, and the fixed empty state inviting the seller to add the first one;
  - with every variety ok: "N variedades · todo ok";
  - otherwise: each status that is not ok, counted by word, in stock-status order.

  It is never blank.
- **"Mandar catálogo"** sits on top. It is disabled, with its reason in words, only while the first-run checklist shows and nothing qualifies.
- **"+ Entrada de stock"** is a secondary button above the list. It opens the entry form with the variety still to be chosen.
- **"Nueva variedad"** adds a variety. **"Todas las entradas"** sits at the foot.
- **On the phone**, opening the list never opens a variety on its own.
- **On a computer**:
  - with nothing selected, the first variety by name is selected automatically. That counts as no tap, and picking another variety replaces it;
  - with no varieties, the right pane holds nothing that could be read as data.
- **A selected variety that is gone** follows accounts.md's "Eso ya no está." rule.

**A variety's page**

- **A detail**: the bar or sidebar and "‹ Variedades", without "+ Nueva venta".
- **One summary line**: grams, status, days left and today's list price, with "Oculta" when hidden.
- **Two equal buttons**:
  - "+ Entrada": this variety chosen, and "Stock inicial" preselected only with no entries;
  - "Nuevo precio".
- **Sections**:
  - **Stock y entradas** and **Precios**: their last 3 rows and "Ver todas". A "Ver todas" list is a card or detail: the bar or sidebar and "‹ <variety>", with no "+ Nueva venta".
  - **Aviso de stock bajo**: the current threshold and its edit action only.
  - **En el catálogo**: shown or hidden, emoji, % índica and aroma/sabor, with their edit action only. It is the only place these attributes are edited.
- **Rename** lives here. **Deleting a price** follows varieties.md ("Antes de borrar", saying what takes the entry's place).
- **Forms** opened from here have "Cerrar" and ask before discarding something typed.
- **Toasts carry no data**:
  - "Variedad guardada", "Precio guardado", "Precio eliminado", "Aviso guardado" and "Catálogo guardado";
  - "Entrada guardada", from the entry form wherever it opened.
- **Refusals** show inline and keep what was typed.

**The catalog screen ("Mandar catálogo", `/varieties/catalog`)**

- **A detail** ("‹ Variedades").
  - Reached from "Mandar catálogo" and from Inicio's "Tu catálogo en Telegram está desactualizado" notice.
  - It always opens, including when nothing qualifies.
- **"Cómo se ve en Telegram"**: the text with left-to-right `**` pairing. When nothing qualifies, the empty state.
- **Compartir** (the main action):
  - it opens the native share sheet with exactly the text Copiar copies;
  - where the browser has no share sheet, it is not shown;
  - success or cancel shows nothing, and there is no toast;
  - a real failure shows the fixed "No se pudo compartir." and leaves Copiar available;
  - no message carries catalog text, a name or an amount.
- **Copiar** (the fallback):
  - it copies the raw text and shows the toast "Catálogo copiado";
  - a failure shows "No se pudo copiar." and no toast;
  - it works over plain http.
- **Pin status and Publicar**: "Al día" or "Desactualizado" with its fixed reason, and Publicar. When Publicar is unavailable, it says why in words and links to where the bot settings are: the temporary place below, or Configuración → Integraciones once that exists.
- **What does not go out, and why**: every variety out of today's catalog, with one reason, the first that applies: oculta, sin precio, then agotada or sin entradas. Told by word.
- **Links**:
  - each variety named opens its "En el catálogo" section;
  - "Editar encabezado y pie" opens the temporary place, or Configuración → Mensaje del catálogo once that exists.
- **The screen itself edits no header, footer or bot setting.** It only links out (navigation.md).

**Temporary place for the bot settings and the header and footer** (a deliberate, temporary exception)

- **Why**: until Configuración → Integraciones and Mensaje del catálogo exist, no seller may lose the way to connect the bot or edit the header and footer.
- **No rework**: they work exactly as today (token, channel chat, own chat, Probarlo, header and footer).
- **Reached only from the catalog screen**: "Editar encabezado y pie" and Publicar's "unavailable" link both open it.
- **A form**:
  - "Cerrar" goes back by history, and with no history to the catalog screen;
  - it confirms before leaving with unsaved changes.
- **No entries**: no tab, Más entry, Configuración group or "coming soon" mark.
- **No status**: it shows no pin status and no Publicar.
- **The exception it makes**: to catalog.md's "header and footer only in Configuración" and pinned-catalog.md's "Integraciones is the only place", for as long as those groups do not exist.
- **Removal**: Configuración's own issue retargets both links and removes the temporary place. If Configuración ships first, the temporary place is not built and the links go straight to Configuración.

**Empty states and errors**

- No varieties: the list's fixed empty state, and no summary line.
- No entries at all: "Sin entradas de stock · Cargá tu stock inicial o una compra".
- Nothing qualifies: the catalog screen opens with its empty state. The pin keeps its own empty message and "Al día".
- A variety, entry or price that is gone, or another business's variety opened by id: "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades".
- No network: "Sin conexión". A server error: the generic retry.

**Unchanged**

- No rule of stock, status, days left, prices, catalog text, catalog attributes, the pinned catalog, upkeep or isolation changes. Only where they are reached and shown changes.
- Language and formats: Rioplatense Spanish with vos; `$ 11.000`, `3,5 g`, `dd/mm`. The catalog text keeps `$11.000`.
- No state is told by color alone, in either theme.

There is no telemetry. Each metric is a test or a by-eye check.

  - the end-to-end run, on phone and computer viewports, counts the taps from every tab root to the catalog screen and to the stock entry form: 100% within 2, and a computer never more than the phone;

For the Tech Lead:
