### Clarifications (round 1)

### Applying the human's decisions

Both decisions are settled, and I have written them into varieties.md and navigation.md.

1. **Aviso de stock bajo**
   - The section shows the current warning read-only: "Avisame abajo de 15 g", "Avisando: quedan 18,5 g" or "Aviso apagado".
   - Its on/off switch sits beside an "Editar" link. "Editar" opens a small form with "Avisar con menos de" in grams, "Guardá el aviso" and "Cerrar".
   - **Turning the warning on** opens that same form. When the warning is off, the variety has no threshold (stock.md), so there is never a saved amount to bring back. Closing the form without saving leaves the warning off.
   - **Turning the warning off** saves at once, with the toast "Aviso guardado".
2. **En el catálogo**
   - The section shows its values read-only: shown or hidden, emoji, % índica and aroma/sabor.
   - "Editar" opens one form holding all four fields, including the switch "Mostrar en el catálogo". The form ends in "Guardá el catálogo".
   - Errors show under each field and keep what was typed.
   - Leaving with unsaved text asks before discarding it.
   - A save shows "Catálogo guardado".

Both forms follow the form chrome in navigation.md: "Cerrar", no bar and no "+ Nueva venta". They need no address of their own. If you give them one, register it in `scripts/design/screens.json`.

### Answer to Tech Lead, round 1

1. **Editing a stock entry: `/varieties/entries/{id}` is accepted.**
   - `/mi-stock/entradas/{id}` redirects to it permanently, tested like the other old paths.
   - It is a form: "Cerrar" goes back by history. With no history, it goes to "Todas las entradas" (`/varieties/entries`).
   - An entry that is gone, or belongs to another business, shows "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades".
   - A record id in the path is fine. ADR-0004 forbids only a business in the URL.
2. **A variety's "Ver todas" of entries goes to `/varieties/{low}/entries`, not `/varieties/entries?variety={id}`.**
   - The reason is that the prices list already lives at `/varieties/{low}/prices`. A variety's two "Ver todas" lists should have addresses of the same shape, so both stay under the variety and both go back to it.
   - You may build it with the same component as "Todas las entradas", filtered to that variety.
   - Its chrome is a card's: "‹ <variety>", and back with no history goes to the variety's page.
   - Its entries can be edited and deleted there, through the same form at `/varieties/entries/{id}`. It is one form for one record, so no fact is edited in two places.
3. **Register both new addresses in `scripts/design/screens.json` in this change.** If they share their first segment with the API (`varieties`), they fall under the parameterised-path question I already raised (ADR-0024 or an amendment to it). The rule is the same: a reload opens the screen, never the API's answer.

## UX Designer (v1)

### Flow

The PRD describes one job story (answer a customer, record what came in or got repriced, and send an up-to-date catalog without moving between screens). It has several situations, so each gets its own subsection. I read the flows from the product docs and the PRD. Where a flow depends on a screen the design must still show, I say so. I do not invent screens.

Counting rule: a touch is one tap. Typing is counted as one touch per field filled. The OS share sheet's own steps are not counted. "Tab root" means any of Inicio, Ventas, Clientes, Variedades, Gastos, Análisis, Más.

#### Situation 1: A customer asks what I have or what something costs

- **Starts:** on any tab root, phone or computer, mid-chat.
- **Phone steps**
  1. Tap the Variedades tab (touch 1). Screen: Variedades list.
     - The summary line is already readable. It reads "N variedades · todo ok", or lists each non-ok status by word. It is never blank.
     - Each row shows the status mark and word, "Oculta" beside it when hidden, grams, days left, and today's list price or "Sin precio: no se puede vender".
  2. If the list answers the question, the job ends here. Cost: 1 touch.
  3. Otherwise tap the variety's row (touch 2). Screen: the variety's page.
     - It shows the summary line (grams, status, days left, price, "Oculta"), "+ Entrada", "Nuevo precio", and the four sections.
- **Computer steps**
  1. Click Variedades in the sidebar (touch 1). The first variety by name is selected on its own, so its page is already in the right pane (0 extra touches).
  2. Click another row to replace it (touch 2).
- **Ends:** reading the list, or reading one variety's page.
- **Touch count:** 1 for the list answer; 2 for a variety's detail. The computer never takes more than the phone.
- **What can go wrong, and recovery**
  - No varieties: the list shows its fixed empty state inviting the first variety and no summary line. Recovery is "Nueva variedad".
  - Phone: the list never opens a variety on its own, so the seller is never dropped into a page they did not choose.
  - The variety was deleted by a partner, or belongs to another business, or is a selected pane that is gone: "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades". It carries no id or name.
  - No network: "Sin conexión". Server error: the generic retry.
- **Unchanged vs. today:** the information is the same. It moves from three screens (Stock, Variedades, Catálogo) to one list and one page.

#### Situation 2: Flower comes in, I record a stock entry

**Path A: from the list (variety chosen in the form)**
- **Starts:** any tab root.
- **Steps**
  1. Tap the Variedades tab (touch 1), unless already there. Screen: Variedades list.
  2. Tap "+ Entrada de stock", the secondary button above the list (touch 2). Screen: the entry form, a form with "Cerrar" and no bar.
  3. Choose the variety, which shows "Hoy tiene N g" (touch 3). The date is already today.
  4. Choose the origin: Compra · Cosecha · Stock inicial · Ajuste (touch 4).
  5. Type grams (touch 5). Type the total cost when it is a purchase (touch 6). The form shows "Te queda en $ X el gramo" before saving. A note is optional.
  6. Save (touch 7). Toast "Entrada guardada", with no data.
- **Ends:** back on the screen that opened the form, with stock updated.
- **Touch count:** form opens in 2 touches from any tab root (1 from Variedades). End to end about 6 to 7 touches for a purchase, 5 to 6 for a harvest.

**Path B: from the variety's page (variety already chosen)**
- **Steps**
  1. Variedades tab (touch 1), then the variety's row (touch 2).
  2. Tap "+ Entrada" (touch 3). The variety is chosen. "Stock inicial" is preselected only when the variety has no entries.
  3. Type grams and cost, then Save: about 3 touches, 4 for a purchase.
- **Ends:** back on the variety's page; "Stock y entradas" shows the new entry among its last 3.
- **Touch count:** about 6 to 7. It saves the variety and origin choice when the seller is already on the page.
- **Computer:** the first variety is already open, so "+ Entrada" is 2 touches after the sidebar click.

**What can go wrong, and recovery**
- A refusal (for example a future date, a purchase with no cost, zero grams) shows inline and keeps everything typed. The seller fixes the field and saves again.
- "Cerrar" with something typed asks before discarding. An untouched form closes at once.
- The variety was deleted meanwhile: the save closes the form, the list refreshes and the notice "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día." shows.
- A mistaken entry: edit or delete it from "Todas las entradas" at the foot of Variedades, or from "Ver todas" on the variety's page. Both are confirmed ("Antes de borrar" for delete). Toast "Entrada eliminada".

#### Situation 3: A price changes, I record a new price

- **Starts:** any tab root.
- **Steps**
  1. Variedades tab (touch 1), the variety's row (touch 2). Screen: the variety's page.
  2. Tap "Nuevo precio" (touch 3). Screen: the price form, "Cerrar".
  3. Type the list price (touch 4). Optionally bulk price and bulk minimum (2 more). The date is set.
  4. Save (touch 5). Toast "Precio guardado".
- **Ends:** on the variety's page; "Precios" shows the new entry first; the page and the list show the new price today.
- **Touch count:** 5 for a list price only; 7 with a bulk price.
- **Recovery**
  - Refusals show inline and keep what was typed. These include a duplicate date, a bulk price not below the list price, and a bulk price without its minimum.
  - A typo is fixed by deleting the entry. The delete confirmation says what takes its place ("Vuelve a regir $ X x g desde dd/mm", or that the variety is left "Sin precio: no se puede vender"). Toast "Precio eliminado". Touches: page (2), delete on the row (3), confirm (4).
  - The full history opens from "Ver todas" in Precios, a card with "‹ <variety>" and no "+ Nueva venta".

#### Situation 4: Change the low-stock warning, or how the variety shows in the catalog

- **Starts:** the variety's page, so 2 touches from any tab root.
- **Aviso de stock bajo**
  1. Tap its edit action (touch 3).
  2. Set or turn off the threshold (touch 4), Save (touch 5). Toast "Aviso guardado".
  - The section shows the value and the edit action only, with no "Ver todas".
- **En el catálogo** (shown or hidden, emoji, % índica, aroma/sabor)
  1. Tap its edit action (touch 3).
  2. Edit the fields (1 per field), Save. Toast "Catálogo guardado".
  - The section shows values and the edit action only.
  - This is the only place these attributes are edited.
- **Rename** is also on this page. Toast "Variedad guardada".
- **Touch count:** 5 for one field of one section from any tab root.
- **Recovery:** refusals (emoji that is not exactly one emoji, % índica outside 0–100, aroma over 200 characters, duplicate name) show inline and keep what was typed. "Cerrar" with unsaved changes asks first.
- **Hand-off flag for UX Lead:** the PRD gives the emoji, % índica and aroma refusals no new screen. Check that the design draws the edit forms as sheets with "Cerrar".

#### Situation 5: Send the catalog to a customer (Compartir)

- **Starts:** any tab root.
- **Steps (phone)**
  1. Tap the Variedades tab (touch 1).
  2. Tap "Mandar catálogo" on top (touch 2). Screen: the catalog screen ("‹ Variedades"). It shows "Cómo se ve en Telegram", the pin status, Publicar and what does not go out.
  3. Read the preview. Tap "Compartir" (touch 3). The device's share sheet opens with exactly the text Copiar would copy, `**` markers included.
  4. Pick the app and the chat inside the share sheet (not counted).
- **Ends:** the seller returns to the catalog screen. Nothing shows, and there is no toast.
- **Touch count:** 3 from any tab root; 2 from Variedades. On a computer, the same: sidebar Variedades (1), "Mandar catálogo" (2), then Compartir (3), or Copiar if there is no share sheet.

**Without a share sheet** (for example some desktop browsers, or where the browser offers none)
- Compartir is not shown. The seller taps "Copiar" (touch 3). Toast "Catálogo copiado". They paste into the chat by hand. Same touch count, plus the switch to the other app.

**What can go wrong, and recovery**
- The seller closes the share sheet without sending: nothing shows, no state change. They can tap Compartir again.
- Real sharing failure: the fixed line "No se pudo compartir." appears. Copiar stays available, so the seller continues with Copiar.
- Copy failure: "No se pudo copiar." with no toast. No other recovery path is drawn in the PRD. **Flag for UX Lead:** with sharing and copying both failing, the seller has only the on-screen preview text to select by hand.
- Nothing qualifies for the catalog: the screen still opens and shows the empty state saying nothing is in the catalog today. The pinned catalog keeps its own empty message and "Al día". Compartir and Copiar on an empty catalog is not defined by the PRD. **Flag for UX Lead:** check whether the design draws them disabled or hidden, since catalog.md says there is nothing to copy.
- "Mandar catálogo" is disabled with its reason in words ("Cargá una variedad con precio y su stock") only while the first-run checklist shows and nothing qualifies. The recovery is to price a variety and record stock first (Situations 2 and 3).
- No network: "Sin conexión". It works over plain http on the home network.

#### Situation 6: Find out why a variety is not in the catalog, and fix it

- **Starts:** the catalog screen (3 touches from any tab root as above, or 1 from Inicio's notice "Tu catálogo en Telegram está desactualizado").
- **Steps**
  1. Read "What does not go out, and why": each variety by name with exactly one reason, in priority order (oculta, sin precio, agotada or sin entradas).
  2. Tap the variety's name (touch 3 from a tab root). Screen: that variety's page, at its "En el catálogo" section.
  3. Fix it according to the reason:
     - oculta: edit En el catálogo and set it shown (about 3 more touches);
     - sin precio: "Nuevo precio" (Situation 3);
     - agotada or sin entradas: "+ Entrada" (Situation 2).
- **Ends:** back on the variety's page. The seller goes back to the catalog screen with "‹ Variedades" and the browser or app back (history), and sees the variety in the preview.
- **Touch count:** 3 to reach the variety, plus the fix (about 3 to 7).
- **Recovery:** if the variety is gone, "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades". Another business's variety shows the same.

#### Situation 7: Keep the pinned Telegram message current (status and Publicar)

- **Starts:** the catalog screen.
- **Steps**
  1. See the pin status: "Al día", or "Desactualizado" with one fixed reason. Before the first Publicar ever, no status shows.
  2. Tap Publicar (touch 3 from a tab root). Toast "Catálogo publicado". The status reads "Al día" and "El último fue el dd/mm".
- **Ends:** on the catalog screen, "Al día".
- **Touch count:** 3 from a tab root; 1 more than Compartir because Publicar is the same screen.
- **What can go wrong, and recovery**
  - Publicar is unavailable (no saved channel, or no successful Probarlo since the last change): it shows disabled with its reason in words ("Probalo primero para ver cómo queda", or that the channel is missing) and a link to the temporary place. See Situation 8.
  - Failure on send: a fixed inline reason shows. A text over Telegram's limit reads "Es demasiado largo para Telegram: acortá aromas u ocultá variedades." The seller shortens an aroma or hides a variety on that variety's page (Situation 4), then comes back and publishes again.
  - Inicio's notice "Tu catálogo en Telegram está desactualizado" opens the catalog screen in 1 tap from Inicio.
- **Unchanged:** upkeep, status reasons and isolation rules do not change; only where they show.

#### Situation 8: Connect the bot or edit the header and footer (temporary place)

This is a deliberate, temporary exception. It applies only if Configuración's Integraciones and Mensaje del catálogo do not exist when this is built. If they exist, the same links go straight to those groups and this flow is the same from the seller's side.

- **Starts:** the catalog screen.
- **Steps (header and footer)**
  1. Variedades (touch 1), "Mandar catálogo" (touch 2), "Editar encabezado y pie" (touch 3). Screen: the temporary place, a form with "Cerrar" and no bar.
  2. Edit the header and footer (1 per field), Save. Toast "Catálogo guardado".
  3. "Cerrar" (1 touch): goes back by history to the catalog screen (the fallback with no history is the catalog screen too).
- **Steps (bot and Publicar)**
  1. From Publicar's "unavailable" link (touch 3), open the same temporary place.
  2. Type the token, the own chat and the channel chat (3 touches), tap Probarlo (touch). Toast "Prueba enviada". The values are saved only when the test succeeds.
  3. "Cerrar", back to the catalog screen. Publicar is now enabled. Tap Publicar (Situation 7).
- **Ends:** back on the catalog screen. The preview shows the new header and footer the next time the screen opens.
- **Touch count:** header and footer: about 7 (open 3, edit 2 to 3, save, close). Bot to published: about 12.
- **What can go wrong, and recovery**
  - Probarlo fails: a fixed inline reason shows. Nothing is saved and everything typed stays, so the seller corrects the field and tries again.
  - Leaving with unsaved changes asks first; an untouched form closes at once.
  - After a token or channel change, the status reads "Desactualizado · Falta publicarlo con el bot o el canal nuevo", and the old pin stays as it is. The seller publishes once from the catalog screen.
- **Constraints the flow must respect:** the place has no tab, Más entry, Configuración group or "coming soon" mark, no pin status and no Publicar. The only ways in are the two links on the catalog screen.
- **Flag for UX Lead:** the temporary place's title is design's call, and I could not confirm it in the prototype. The bot-to-published path needs the seller to go to the temporary place and come back to the catalog screen to publish. That round trip is by design (Publicar has to stay on the catalog screen), not a defect I can solve here.

#### Situation 9: Open an old path or a bookmark

- **Starts:** a bookmark or link to `/variedades`, `/mi-stock`, `/mi-stock/nueva-entrada`, `/catalogo`, or another old Stock, Variedades or Catálogo path.
- **Steps:** the address redirects permanently to the new route (`/varieties`, `/varieties/entries/new`, `/varieties/catalog`, and the others). The seller lands on the matching screen without tapping.
- **Ends:** on the new screen. Touch count: 0.
- **Recovery:** an unknown or mistyped address goes to Inicio. A reload on any `/varieties` route, parameterised ones included, opens the screen and never the API's JSON. Whether that works for `/varieties/{low}` depends on ADR-0024, which allows only exact paths. This is a technical dependency, not a flow change for the seller; the seller's flow is unchanged either way.

#### Situation 10: Browse every stock entry ("Todas las entradas")

- **Starts:** Variedades list.
- **Steps**
  1. Variedades tab (touch 1). Scroll to the foot and tap "Todas las entradas" (touch 2). Screen: a card with "‹ Variedades", every entry with date, variety, origin, grams, total cost and cost per gram, newest first.
  2. Tap an entry to edit or delete it (touch 3). Saving an edit is confirmed first; deleting is confirmed with "Antes de borrar".
- **Ends:** back on the list, which has refreshed.
- **Touch count:** 2 to read; 4 to 6 to fix one.
- **Recovery:** with no entries it shows "Sin entradas de stock · Cargá tu stock inicial o una compra". An entry that is gone shows "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades".

#### Flows unchanged

- **Pricing, stock, status, days left, the catalog text, upkeep and isolation:** no rule changes, so no flow in these jobs changes beyond where each is reached and shown.
- **Configuración's own groups, and any area outside Variedades and the catalog screen:** not changed by this task and not described here.

## UX Designer

### Flow

One job story (answer a customer, record what came in or got repriced, send an up-to-date catalog without moving between screens). It has several situations, so each has its own subsection. This revision answers UX Lead's return: Situation 4 now follows the controls the prototype draws (inline, with no sheet), and "Ver todas" in Stock y entradas now leads where the prototype leads.

Counting rule: a touch is one tap, plus one touch per field typed. The OS share sheet's own steps are not counted. A tab root is any of Inicio, Ventas, Clientes, Variedades, Gastos, Análisis, Más. A step the prototype does not draw is marked "sin prototipo". I do not invent a screen for it. Those gaps are listed at the end for UX Lead.

#### Situation 1: A customer asks what I have or what something costs

- **Starts:** any tab root, phone or computer, mid-chat.
- **Phone steps**
  1. Tap Variedades (touch 1). Screen: the Variedades list.
     - The summary line is on top ("N variedades · todo ok", or each non-ok status counted by word). It is never blank.
     - Each row shows a status mark and word, grams and days left, and today's list price. A hidden variety shows "Oculta" beside its status. A variety with no price shows "Sin precio: no se puede vender".
  2. If the list answers the question, the job ends. Cost: 1 touch.
  3. Otherwise tap the variety's row (touch 2). Screen: the variety's page, with the summary line, "+ Entrada", "Nuevo precio" and the four sections.
- **Computer steps**
  1. Click Variedades in the sidebar (touch 1). The first variety by name is already selected in the right pane (0 extra touches).
  2. Click another row to replace it (touch 2).
- **Ends:** reading the list or one variety's page.
- **Touch count:** 1 for the list, 2 for a variety. A computer never takes more than the phone.
- **What can go wrong, and recovery**
  - No varieties: fixed empty state inviting the first one, no summary line, "Mandar catálogo" disabled with its reason in words. Recovery: "Nueva variedad".
  - On the phone the list never opens a variety by itself.
  - A variety gone (deleted by a partner, or another business's): "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades". It carries no id or name.
  - No network: "Sin conexión". Server error: the generic retry.
- **Unchanged vs. today:** the same information, now on one list and one page instead of three screens.
- **Design notes:** the computer list drops the status word and keeps only the mark. The "Oculta" and "Sin precio" rows have no prototype. Both are for UX Lead.

#### Situation 2: Flower comes in, I record a stock entry

**Path A: from the list**
- **Starts:** any tab root.
- **Steps**
  1. Tap Variedades (touch 1), unless already there.
  2. Tap "Entrada de stock", the secondary button above the list (touch 2). Screen: the entry form ("Nueva entrada", "Cerrar", no bar). The date is already today.
  3. Choose the variety, which shows "Hoy tiene N g" (touch 3).
  4. Choose the origin: Compra · Cosecha · Stock inicial · Ajuste (touch 4).
  5. Type grams (touch 5) and, for a purchase, the total cost (touch 6). "Te queda en $ X el gramo" shows before saving. The note is optional.
  6. Tap "Guardá la entrada" (touch 7). Toast "Entrada guardada", with no data.
- **Ends:** back on the screen that opened the form, with stock updated.
- **Touch count:** the form opens in 2 touches from any tab root (1 from Variedades). End to end: 7 for a purchase, 6 for a harvest.

**Path B: from the variety's page**
- **Steps**
  1. Variedades (touch 1), the variety's row (touch 2).
  2. Tap "+ Entrada" (touch 3). The variety is chosen. "Stock inicial" is preselected only when the variety has no entries.
  3. Type grams (touch 4) and cost (touch 5), then save (touch 6).
- **Ends:** on the variety's page. "Stock y entradas" shows the new entry among its last 3.
- **Touch count:** 6 for a purchase. It saves the variety and origin choice. On a computer the first variety is already open, so it is 5.

**What can go wrong, and recovery**
- A refusal (future date, purchase with no cost, zero grams) shows inline and keeps everything typed. The seller fixes the field and saves again.
- "Cerrar" with something typed asks before discarding. An untouched form closes at once.
- The variety was deleted meanwhile: the form closes, the list refreshes, and the notice "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día." shows.
- A mistaken entry is edited or deleted from "Todas las entradas" (Situation 10). Saving an edit is confirmed first. Deleting is confirmed with "Antes de borrar". Toast "Entrada eliminada".

#### Situation 3: A price changes, I record a new price

- **Starts:** any tab root.
- **Steps**
  1. Variedades (touch 1), the variety's row (touch 2). Screen: the variety's page.
  2. Tap "Nuevo precio" (touch 3). Screen: the price form ("Cerrar").
  3. Type the list price (touch 4). Optionally type bulk price and bulk minimum (2 more).
  4. Save (touch 5). Toast "Precio guardado".
- **Ends:** on the variety's page. "Precios" shows the new entry first, and the page and list show the new price.
- **Touch count:** 5 for a list price only, 7 with a bulk price.
- **Recovery**
  - Refusals show inline and keep what was typed. These are a duplicate date, a bulk price not below the list price, and a bulk price without its minimum.
  - A typo is fixed by deleting the entry with the delete button on its Precios row: page (2), delete (3), confirm (4). The confirmation says what takes its place ("Vuelve a regir $ X x g desde dd/mm", or that the variety is left "Sin precio: no se puede vender"). Toast "Precio eliminado". The confirmation copy has no prototype.
  - "Ver todas" in Precios opens the full price history, a card with "‹" back to the variety and no "+ Nueva venta".

#### Situation 4: Change the low-stock warning, or how the variety shows in the catalog

This follows the prototype's variety page. Its Aviso de stock bajo and En el catálogo sections are inline controls on the page. There is no edit action, no sheet and no Save button.

- **Starts:** the variety's page, 2 touches from any tab root.
- **Aviso de stock bajo, as drawn**
  1. The section shows an on/off toggle and the fixed line "Avisar con menos de 20 g".
  2. Turning the warning off or on is one tap on the toggle (touch 3). That is the only control drawn.
  3. **Setting or changing the amount** has no drawn control. The prototype shows the amount only as text. The flow cannot say which touches change it. This is a gap for UX Lead, not something to invent here.
  4. Toast "Aviso guardado" is a product rule (varieties.md). The prototype does not show when it fires.
- **En el catálogo, as drawn**
  1. The section shows the toggle "Mostrar en el catálogo" and three text fields: Emoji, % índica and Aroma/sabor. All sit on the page, already editable.
  2. Showing or hiding the variety is one tap on the toggle (touch 3). Editing a field is one tap to focus and typing it (touch 3 for the first field, then 1 per extra field).
  3. **How the change is saved** is not drawn. There is no Save button and no Cerrar. The product rules say the toast "Catálogo guardado" shows on saving. The flow cannot say whether a change saves on leaving the field, on a tap on the toggle, or by an action the design has yet to draw. This is the main gap for UX Lead.
- **Rename:** the "Editar" link next to the variety's name opens the "Nueva variedad" form (VariedadNueva), which has "Cerrar". Edit the name and save (touches: page 2, Editar 3, type 4, save 5). Toast "Variedad guardada". A duplicate name is refused inline and keeps what was typed.
- **Touch count:** 3 to flip a toggle from any tab root. Typing a field: 4 or more. The rest depends on the gaps above.
- **What can go wrong, and recovery**
  - Refusals the product defines: an emoji that is not exactly one emoji, a % índica outside 0–100 or not whole, an aroma over 200 characters. The product says refusals show inline and keep what was typed. The prototype shows no error state for these inline fields, so where they appear is a gap for UX Lead.
  - Leaving the page with typing not yet saved: the product asks before discarding for forms. These fields are not a form in the design, so what happens to unsaved typing is a gap for UX Lead.
  - The variety is gone while editing: "Eso ya no está." rule, then "Volver a Variedades".
- **Unchanged:** these attributes and the threshold are edited only on this page. No other screen offers them.

#### Situation 5: Send the catalog to a customer (Compartir)

- **Starts:** any tab root.
- **Steps (phone)**
  1. Tap Variedades (touch 1).
  2. Tap "Mandar catálogo" on top (touch 2). Screen: the catalog screen ("‹ Variedades"). It shows "Cómo se ve en Telegram", the pin status with Publicar, and "Qué no sale y por qué".
  3. Read the preview. Tap "Compartir", the main action (touch 3). The share sheet opens with exactly the text Copiar copies, `**` markers included.
  4. Pick the app and chat inside the share sheet (not counted).
- **Ends:** back on the catalog screen. Nothing shows, and there is no toast.
- **Touch count:** 3 from any tab root, 2 from Variedades. The computer is the same (sidebar, "Mandar catálogo", Compartir or Copiar).
- **Without a share sheet:** Compartir is not shown. The seller taps "Copiar" (touch 3). Toast "Catálogo copiado". They paste by hand. Same touch count. It works over plain http.
- **What can go wrong, and recovery**
  - Closing the share sheet without sending: nothing shows and nothing changes. Compartir can be tapped again.
  - A real failure: the fixed line "No se pudo compartir." shows and Copiar stays available.
  - Copy failure: "No se pudo copiar.", no toast. If both fail, the only way out is selecting the preview text by hand. The design does not draw that case.
  - Nothing qualifies: the screen still opens with its empty state. The pin keeps its own empty message and "Al día". Whether Compartir and Copiar are hidden or disabled on an empty catalog has no prototype.
  - "Mandar catálogo" is disabled with its reason ("Cargá una variedad con precio y su stock") only while the first-run checklist shows and nothing qualifies. Recovery: price a variety (Situation 3) and record stock (Situation 2) first.
  - No network: "Sin conexión".

#### Situation 6: Find out why a variety is not in the catalog, and fix it

- **Starts:** the catalog screen, 3 touches from any tab root as above, or 1 from Inicio's notice (when that notice is shown).
- **Steps**
  1. Read "Qué no sale y por qué". Each variety is listed by name with exactly one reason, in order: oculta, sin precio, agotada or sin entradas.
  2. Tap the variety's name (touch 3 from a tab root). Screen: that variety's page, at "En el catálogo".
  3. Fix it by reason:
     - oculta: flip "Mostrar en el catálogo" (1 touch, Situation 4);
     - sin precio: "Nuevo precio" (Situation 3);
     - agotada or sin entradas: "+ Entrada" (Situation 2).
- **Ends:** on the variety's page. Back (history, then "‹ Variedades") returns to the catalog screen, which shows the variety in the preview the next time it opens.
- **Touch count:** 3 to reach the variety, plus the fix (1 to 7).
- **Recovery:** a gone variety shows "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades". Another business's variety shows the same.
- **Design note:** the prototype draws only agotada and sin entradas as reasons. Oculta and sin precio have no prototype.

#### Situation 7: Keep the pinned Telegram message current (status and Publicar)

- **Starts:** the catalog screen.
- **Steps**
  1. Read the status: "Al día", or "Desactualizado" with one fixed reason. Before the first Publicar ever, no status shows.
  2. Tap Publicar (touch 3 from a tab root). Toast "Catálogo publicado". The status reads "Al día" and "El último fue el dd/mm".
- **Ends:** on the catalog screen, "Al día".
- **Touch count:** 3 from any tab root. From Inicio's notice, when shown, it is 2.
- **What can go wrong, and recovery**
  - Publicar unavailable (no saved channel, or no successful Probarlo since the last change): it shows disabled with its reason in words and a link to where the bot settings are (Situation 8). The prototype's wording for it ("Conectá el bot en Integraciones…") differs from the product's ("Probalo primero para ver cómo queda", or that the channel is missing). UX Lead should align them.
  - Send failure: a fixed inline reason. For a text over Telegram's limit it reads "Es demasiado largo para Telegram: acortá aromas u ocultá variedades." The seller shortens an aroma or hides a variety on its page (Situation 4), returns and publishes again.
- **Design note:** Inicio's out-of-date notice is not on the prototype's Inicio or InicioCompu. This flow does not depend on it.

#### Situation 8: Connect the bot or edit the header and footer

- **Where it goes:** the product docs say that until Configuración's Integraciones and Mensaje del catálogo exist, one temporary place holds them, reached only from the catalog screen. The prototype links to ConfiguracionMensaje and ConfiguracionIntegraciones. The prototype's targets are the Configuración groups, and the temporary place has no prototype. The flow below is the same either way, with the target changed.
- **Steps (header and footer)**
  1. Variedades (touch 1), "Mandar catálogo" (touch 2), "Editar encabezado y pie" (touch 3). Screen: the target, a form with "Cerrar" and no bar.
  2. Edit the header and footer (1 touch per field), save. Toast "Catálogo guardado".
  3. "Cerrar" (1 touch) goes back by history to the catalog screen. With no history, it goes to the catalog screen.
- **Steps (bot, then publish)**
  1. From Publicar's unavailable link (touch 3), open the target.
  2. Type the token, the own chat and the channel chat (3 touches), then tap Probarlo (1). Toast "Prueba enviada". Values are saved only when the test succeeds.
  3. "Cerrar", back to the catalog screen. Tap Publicar (Situation 7).
- **Ends:** on the catalog screen, which shows the new header and footer the next time it opens.
- **Touch count:** header and footer about 7. Bot to published about 12.
- **What can go wrong, and recovery**
  - Probarlo fails: a fixed inline reason, nothing saved, everything typed stays. The seller corrects and retries.
  - Leaving with unsaved changes asks first. An untouched form closes at once.
  - After a token or channel change, the status reads "Desactualizado · Falta publicarlo con el bot o el canal nuevo", and the old pin stays as it is. One Publicar fixes it.
- **Constraints:** the temporary place, if built, has no tab, Más entry, Configuración group or "coming soon" mark, and no pin status or Publicar. The round trip back to the catalog screen to publish is by design.

#### Situation 9: Open an old path or a bookmark

- **Starts:** a bookmark or link to `/variedades`, `/mi-stock`, `/mi-stock/nueva-entrada`, `/catalogo`, or another old Stock, Variedades or Catálogo path.
- **Steps:** the address redirects permanently to the new route (`/varieties`, `/varieties/entries/new`, `/varieties/catalog`, and the others). The seller lands on the matching screen with no tap.
- **Ends:** on the new screen. Touch count: 0.
- **Recovery:** an unknown address goes to Inicio. A reload on any `/varieties` route opens the screen, never the API's JSON. For the parameterised routes that depends on a technical decision about ADR-0024. The seller's flow is the same either way.

#### Situation 10: Browse every stock entry ("Todas las entradas")

- **Starts:** the Variedades list, or a variety's page.
- **Steps**
  1. From the list: Variedades (touch 1), scroll to the foot, tap "Todas las entradas" (touch 2).
  2. From a variety's page: tap "Ver todas" in Stock y entradas (touch 3 from a tab root).
  3. Screen in both cases: TodasLasEntradas, a card with "‹ Variedades". It lists every entry of the business (date, variety, origin, grams, total cost, cost per gram), newest first. The prototype's "Ver todas" in Stock y entradas leads to this same global list, not to a list of that variety's entries alone.
  4. Tap an entry to edit or delete it (next touch). Saving an edit is confirmed. Deleting is confirmed with "Antes de borrar".
- **Ends:** back on the screen it came from, refreshed.
- **Touch count:** 2 to read from the list, 3 from a page. Editing or deleting one: 4 to 6.
- **Recovery:** with no entries: "Sin entradas de stock · Cargá tu stock inicial o una compra". An entry that is gone shows "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades".
- **Design note:** the product says a "Ver todas" list of a variety's page is a card with "‹ <variety>", but the prototype's Stock y entradas link opens a screen that goes back to "‹ Variedades". The seller who starts at a page and taps Back lands on the list, not on the page. UX Lead should decide whether this is accepted.

#### Flows unchanged

- Pricing, stock, status, days left, the catalog text, upkeep and isolation: no rule changes, so no flow in those jobs changes beyond where each is reached and shown.
- Configuración's own groups and any area outside Variedades and the catalog screen: not changed by this task.

#### Gaps the current screens cannot support (for UX Lead)

1. Aviso de stock bajo has a toggle and a fixed "Avisar con menos de 20 g" line, but no way to set the amount.
2. En el catálogo's fields are inline, with no drawn save, toast timing, inline refusals or unsaved-typing behaviour.
3. Compartir and Copiar on an empty catalog, and both failing at once, are not drawn.
4. "Oculta", "Sin precio: no se puede vender", the oculta and sin precio reasons, the delete-price confirmation copy, and the temporary place have no prototype.
5. Publicar's unavailable wording differs from the product's.
6. "Ver todas" in Stock y entradas opens the global entries list.

### Walk

Prototype: `docs/product/design-system/pantallas`. I walked the phone and computer versions of Variedades, VariedadDetalle, Catalogo, EntradaStock, VerTodas and TodasLasEntradas, plus Inicio.

**Situation 1: what I have / what it costs.**
- **Variedades (phone):**
  - The summary line "1 sin entradas · 1 agotada · 1 baja" is on top, followed by "Mandar catálogo", "Entrada de stock" (secondary), "Nueva variedad", the rows sorted by name and "Todas las entradas" at the foot. This matches the flow.
  - Each row shows a mark (empty circle, filled circle, empty square or filled square), the status word, grams · days, and the price.
  - The empty state draws "Mandar catálogo" disabled with "Cargá una variedad con precio y su stock", and no summary line. This matches.
  - Sin prototipo for a hidden row ("Oculta") and for "Sin precio: no se puede vender".
- **VariedadesCompu:**
  - Two panes, with Golden Cookies selected on the right.
  - The computer rows drop the status word and keep only the mark. Check this against "shape and word".
- **Touch counts:** 1 for the list and 2 for a page both hold. On the phone, Gastos and Análisis sit behind Más rather than being tab roots.

**Situation 2: stock entry.**
- **Path A:** Variedades → "Entrada de stock" → EntradaStock ("Nueva entrada", "Cerrar", date "Hoy", variety with "Hoy tiene N g", origin chips, cost, "Te queda en $ X el gramo", "Guardá la entrada"). This matches.
- **Path B:** VariedadDetalle "+ Entrada" → EntradaStock with the variety chosen. "Stock inicial" is preselected only for Mango Bomb, which has no entries. This matches.
- **"Ver todas" in Stock y entradas:** it opens TodasLasEntradas, the global list with "‹ Variedades", not a per-variety card with "‹ <variety>". The flow's recovery "from Ver todas on the variety's page" lands there.

**Situation 3: new price.**
- VariedadDetalle → "Nuevo precio" → PrecioNuevo ("Cerrar"). The Precios row has a delete button. "Ver todas" opens VerTodas with "‹" back to the variety. This matches.
- The delete confirmation copy is sin prototipo.

**Situation 4: low-stock warning and catalog attributes. Does not match.**
- **Aviso de stock bajo:** the prototype draws only an on/off toggle and the fixed line "Avisar con menos de 20 g". There is no edit action, no amount field and no Save. The flow's "tap edit action → set → Save" cannot be walked, and the prototype gives no way to change the amount.
- **En el catálogo:** "Mostrar en el catálogo" and the Emoji, % índica and Aroma/sabor fields are drawn inline, on the page. There is no edit action, no sheet, no Save and no Cerrar. The flow describes a sheet and a Save that are not there. It does not say how an inline change is saved, when "Catálogo guardado" shows, where the refusals appear, or what happens to unsaved typing when the seller leaves. This answers the flow's own hand-off flag: the design does not draw sheets.
- **Rename:** "Editar" next to the name opens VariedadNueva. The flow does not describe this step.

**Situation 5: Compartir.**
- Variedades → "Mandar catálogo" → Catalogo ("‹ Variedades", "Cómo se ve en Telegram", Compartir as the main action and Copiar as secondary). 3 taps from a tab root holds, on phone and computer.
- Sin prototipo for the no-share-sheet version, "No se pudo compartir.", "No se pudo copiar." and the empty catalog. Whether Compartir and Copiar are hidden or disabled on an empty catalog is still open, as the flow flagged.

**Situation 6: what does not go out.**
- "Qué no sale y por qué" lists Sir Jack (agotada) and Mango Bomb (sin entradas), each with its word. The names link to `VariedadDetalle#en-el-catalogo`. This matches.
- Sin prototipo for oculta and sin precio as reasons.

**Situation 7: pin and Publicar.**
- The "Al día" and "Desactualizado" cards each carry their word and fixed reason. Publicar is drawn disabled with "Conectá el bot en Integraciones…" and a link.
- The flow quotes different reason texts ("Probalo primero…"). Align the wording.
- The Inicio notice "Tu catálogo en Telegram está desactualizado" is sin prototipo: it is not on Inicio or InicioCompu.

**Situation 8: temporary place.** Sin prototipo. The prototype's links go to ConfiguracionMensaje and ConfiguracionIntegraciones, so it assumes Configuración exists. The flow's conditional branch covers that case, and the temporary place is judged on its text alone.

**Situation 9: redirects.** No screen to walk; this is a technical dependency on ADR-0024. Judged on the text, it is fine.

**Situation 10: Todas las entradas.** TodasLasEntradas has "‹ Variedades" and is reached from the list's foot. This matches. Its empty state is sin prototipo.

### Verdict

`returned`: Situation 4 describes edit actions, sheets and a Save that the prototype does not have. As drawn, the threshold amount has no step at all. UX Designer must rewrite Situation 4 to match the inline controls, including how changes are saved, where refusals show and how the threshold is set. They should also fix where "Ver todas" in Stock y entradas leads.

## UI Designer (v1)

### Screens

Built over the product's own `docs/product/design-system/pantallas/` files and its tokens ("Papel y azul": `--papel`, brown-black ink, one blue accent, amber for owing/low; never green↔red). Every state below is told by word and shape, never by color alone, in both themes (`InicioOscuro` follows the same tokens). Copy is Rioplatense Spanish with vos; money `$ 11.000`, grams `3,5 g`, dates `dd/mm`. No component, token or visual language is added. Where the design does not draw something the flow needs, it is listed under "Gaps for Design Lead" and is not drawn here.

### Variedades list (phone: `Variedades`; computer: left pane of `VariedadesCompu`)

- **Built from:** `Variedades` / `VariedadesCompu`, tab root (bar or sidebar, "+ Nueva venta", Variedades active with `aria-current` and a non-color mark). The Stock tab is gone; this screen takes its status, grams and days-left rows.
- **Structure, top to bottom:** summary line; "Mandar catálogo"; the secondary button "Entrada de stock" (not a floating button); "Nueva variedad"; the rows, sorted by name; "Todas las entradas" at the foot.
- **Row:** name; `MarcaEstado` mark plus word (square = agotada as the inverted chip, filled circle = baja, empty circle = ok, "Sin entradas"); detail line ("Sin gramos desde el 30/09", "Avisás abajo de 20 g", "Cargale la primera entrada"); grams and days left; today's list price per gram. On the computer the design shows the mark without the word.
- **States**
  - Filled, all ok: "N variedades · todo ok".
  - Filled, mixed: each non-ok status counted by word in stock-status order, e.g. "1 sin entradas · 1 agotada · 1 baja". Never blank.
  - Empty (no varieties): no summary line; fixed empty state inviting the first variety (the design's empty-list treatment); "Mandar catálogo" disabled with its reason in words.
  - "Mandar catálogo" disabled, reason in words ("Cargá una variedad con precio y su stock"), only while the first-run checklist shows and nothing qualifies.
  - Loading and error: the shared `Estados` loading (no figures, no digits, no placeholder amounts) and the generic retry; offline "Sin conexión".
  - Computer: nothing selected selects the first variety by name in the right pane (no tap). With no varieties the right pane holds no figure. Phone: the list never opens a variety by itself.
  - Selected or opened variety gone: `Estados` "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades", no id or name.
- **PRD change on this screen:** the Stock list merges into this one; add the summary line, "Mandar catálogo", "Entrada de stock", "Todas las entradas" and the price column. Old Stock/Variedades paths redirect.
- **Gaps (no prototype):** the "Oculta" tag beside the status of a hidden variety; "Sin precio: no se puede vender" as the price cell. Both are to be drawn with the existing chip/text treatments, and Design Lead should confirm.

### A variety's page (phone: `VariedadDetalle`; computer: right pane of `VariedadesCompu`)

- **Built from:** `VariedadDetalle`, a detail ("‹ Variedades", bar or sidebar, no "+ Nueva venta").
- **Structure:** variety name with "Editar"; one summary line (e.g. "64 g · ok · 21 días · $ 11.000 x g", plus "Oculta" when hidden); two equal buttons "+ Entrada" and "Nuevo precio"; then four sections.
  - **Stock y entradas:** last 3 entries and "Ver todas".
  - **Precios:** last 3 prices with "Rige desde", newest first, each with its delete button, and "Ver todas".
  - **Aviso de stock bajo:** the toggle and the line "Avisar con menos de N g" / "Avisando: quedan N g". No "Ver todas".
  - **En el catálogo** (anchor `#en-el-catalogo`, the target of every link from the catalog screen): toggle "Mostrar en el catálogo", fields Emoji, % índica, Aroma/sabor. No "Ver todas".
- **States:** filled; variety with no entries (Stock y entradas reads "Sin entradas de stock · Cargá tu stock inicial o una compra", and "+ Entrada" preselects "Stock inicial"); no prices ("Sin precio: no se puede vender"); hidden; warning off ("Aviso apagado"); loading, error and offline via `Estados`; gone via "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades".
- **Toasts (no data):** "Variedad guardada", "Precio guardado", "Precio eliminado", "Aviso guardado", "Catálogo guardado", "Entrada guardada".
- **PRD change:** the page absorbs the old Catálogo attributes and the threshold; they are edited here and nowhere else.
- **Gaps for Design Lead:**
  - Aviso de stock bajo draws no control to set or change the amount, and no rule for what applies when the warning is first turned on.
  - En el catálogo's inline fields draw no save moment, no "Catálogo guardado" timing, no inline refusals (emoji not exactly one, % outside 0–100, aroma over 200) and no rule for unsaved typing.
  - The delete-price confirmation ("Antes de borrar", saying what takes its place) is not drawn. The delete button points nowhere.
  - There is no `VariedadDetalleCompu`; the computer page is the right pane of `VariedadesCompu`.

### Forms opened from a variety: `EntradaStock`, `PrecioNuevo`, `VariedadNueva` (rename and add)

- **Built from:** `EntradaStock` / `EntradaStockCompu`, `PrecioNuevo` / `PrecioNuevoCompu`, `VariedadNueva` / `VariedadNuevaCompu`. Forms: "Cerrar", no bar, no "+ Nueva venta"; unsaved typing asks before discarding, an untouched form closes at once.
- **EntradaStock:** date filled with today, variety with "Hoy tiene N g", origin Compra · Cosecha · Stock inicial · Ajuste, total cost ("Obligatorio cuando es una compra"), note, "Te queda en $ X el gramo". Opened from the list, the variety is to be chosen. Opened from a page, the variety is chosen, and "Stock inicial" is preselected only if it has no entries. It saves with "Entrada guardada".
- **States:** empty; filled; inline refusals that keep what was typed (future date, purchase without cost, zero grams, duplicate price date, bulk not below list, bulk without minimum, duplicate name); variety gone, which closes the form and shows "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día."
- **PRD change:** none visual. The entry form is reached from the new places and the variety preselection is new behaviour.

### Todas las entradas and "Ver todas" lists (`TodasLasEntradas`, `VerTodas`)

- **Built from:** `TodasLasEntradas` (every entry of the business: date, variety, origin, grams, total cost, cost per gram, newest first, each editable or deletable) and `VerTodas` (price history). Both are cards: bar or sidebar and a "‹" back, no "+ Nueva venta".
- **States:** filled; empty "Sin entradas de stock · Cargá tu stock inicial o una compra"; loading, error and offline; edit confirmed before saving; delete "Antes de borrar" with toast "Entrada eliminada"; gone via "Eso ya no está." and "Volver a Variedades".
- **Gap:** the design's Stock y entradas "Ver todas" opens `TodasLasEntradas` (the global list, "‹ Variedades"), but the PRD calls for a card with "‹ <variety>". Design Lead should settle which applies.

### Catalog screen, "Mandar catálogo" (`Catalogo` / `CatalogoCompu`, route `/varieties/catalog`)

- **Built from:** `Catalogo` / `CatalogoCompu`, a detail ("‹ Variedades", bar or sidebar, no "+ Nueva venta"). Reached from Variedades and from Inicio's out-of-date notice.
- **Structure:** "Cómo se ve en Telegram" (preview with `**` pairing); Compartir as the main action with Copiar beside it; the pin status with Publicar ("Al día" or "Desactualizado" and its fixed reason, "El último fue el dd/mm"); "Qué no sale y por qué" (each variety named, one reason each, linking to that variety's `#en-el-catalogo`); "Editar encabezado y pie".
- **States**
  - Filled: all of the above.
  - Compartir shown only where `navigator.share` exists; otherwise only Copiar. Cancel shows nothing. A real failure shows the fixed line "No se pudo compartir." with Copiar still available. Copy success shows the toast "Catálogo copiado". Copy failure shows "No se pudo copiar." with no toast. No message carries catalog text.
  - Nothing qualifies: the screen still opens, with the empty state saying nothing is in the catalog today; the pin keeps "Al día" and its own empty message.
  - Pin states: before the first Publicar no status; "Desactualizado" with one of the five fixed reasons; the reason for "Es demasiado largo…" shown inline on a failed Publicar.
  - Publicar unavailable: disabled with the reason in words ("Probalo primero para ver cómo queda", or the channel is missing) and a link to the bot settings.
  - Toast "Catálogo publicado". Loading, error and offline via `Estados`.
- **PRD change:** the screen moves to `/varieties/catalog` (`/catalogo` redirects), gains Compartir as the main action, and links out to each variety's catalog section. It edits no header, footer or bot setting.
- **Gaps:**
  - Compartir and Copiar on an empty catalog, and both failing, are not drawn.
  - The "oculta" and "sin precio" reasons are not drawn; only agotada and sin entradas are.
  - The prototype's Publicar-unavailable wording ("Conectá el bot en Integraciones…") differs from the product's; the docs win.
  - Inicio's out-of-date notice is not on `Inicio` / `InicioCompu`.

### Temporary place for the bot settings and the header and footer

- **Built from:** no prototype. Until Configuración exists, the closest design is `ConfiguracionIntegraciones` / `ConfiguracionMensaje` (and their `Compu` versions), which are out of this task's scope. The existing components of today's bot and header/footer forms are reused as they are, with no rework.
- **Chrome:** a form, "Cerrar" (back by history, otherwise to the catalog screen) and confirmation on unsaved leave. It has no tab, Más entry, Configuración group or "coming soon" mark, and no pin status and no Publicar. Reached only from "Editar encabezado y pie" and Publicar's unavailable link.
- **States:** empty fields with no "Guardado" line; filled; token "Guardado el dd/mm · no se muestra más" with Cambiar; Probarlo success ("Prueba enviada", values saved); Probarlo failure (fixed inline reason, nothing saved, typing kept); header/footer saved ("Catálogo guardado"); the line "El mensaje fijado anterior queda como está; desfijalo vos en Telegram." after a token or channel change; loading, error and offline.
- **Gap for Design Lead:** the title and layout of this place are not designed. If Configuración ships first, link straight to its groups and do not build it.

### Shared states and redirects (`Estados`)

- **Built from:** `Estados`, used on every screen above: loading (no figures), empty, "Sin conexión", the generic server-error retry, "Eso ya no está." (opened record gone, with "Volver a Variedades") and the fixed notice "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día." after a save, delete or undo on a record that is gone. The notice is not a success toast, and it has no "Deshacer" and no "Reintentar".
- **Redirects:** `/variedades`, `/mi-stock`, `/mi-stock/nueva-entrada`, `/catalogo` and the other old Stock, Variedades and Catálogo paths redirect permanently, so they have no screen. An unknown path goes to Inicio.
- **PRD change:** no visual change. Computer layouts use the same words and shapes.

### Gaps for Design Lead (summary)

1. No control to set the threshold amount.
2. En el catálogo has no save moment, inline refusals or unsaved-typing rule.
3. The temporary place is not designed.
4. "Oculta", "Sin precio: no se puede vender", the oculta and sin precio reasons, and the delete-price confirmation are not drawn.
5. Catalog screen with an empty catalog, and Compartir plus Copiar both failing, are not drawn.
6. "Ver todas" in Stock y entradas opens the global entries list, which conflicts with the PRD's "‹ <variety>".
7. Publicar's unavailable wording in the prototype differs from the docs.
8. Inicio's out-of-date notice is not drawn.

## UI Designer

### Screens

Everything is composed from `docs/product/design-system/pantallas/` and its tokens ("Papel y azul": `--papel`, brown-black ink, one blue accent, amber for owing or low; never green↔red). No token, component or visual language is added. Every state is told by word and shape, never by color alone, in both themes. Copy is Rioplatense Spanish with vos; money `$ 11.000`, grams `3,5 g`, dates `dd/mm`. This revision answers Design Lead's return. The two blocking points, the threshold amount and the En el catálogo save states, are now specified below using only what Componentes holds: "Campo" (texto · monto · gramos · fecha · con error), "Encabezado de formulario/hoja" ("Cerrar", no bar), the Confirmación of Estados, and the existing toasts.

### Variedades list (phone `Variedades`; computer left pane of `VariedadesCompu`)

- **Built from:** `Variedades` / `VariedadesCompu`, a tab root (bar or sidebar, "+ Nueva venta", Variedades active with `aria-current` and a non-color mark). The Stock tab is gone; this screen takes its status, grams and days-left rows.
- **Order, top to bottom:** summary line; "Mandar catálogo"; "Entrada de stock" as a secondary button (not floating); "Nueva variedad"; rows sorted by name; "Todas las entradas" at the foot.
- **Row:** name; `MarcaEstado` mark plus word (square = agotada as the inverted chip, filled circle = baja, empty circle = ok, or "Sin entradas"); detail line; grams and days left; today's list price per gram. On the computer the design draws the mark without the word. The word stays available as the row's accessible name, so state is never told by shape alone. Design Lead accepted this as an observation.
- **"Oculta"** (not drawn in the prototype): the existing small chip treatment, placed beside the status mark and word, never replacing it.
- **"Sin precio: no se puede vender"** (not drawn): plain secondary text in the price cell, using the existing text style.
- **States**
  - Filled, all ok: "N variedades · todo ok".
  - Filled, mixed: each non-ok status counted by word in stock-status order (e.g. "1 sin entradas · 1 agotada · 1 baja"). Never blank.
  - Empty (no varieties): no summary line; the fixed empty state inviting the first variety, with "Nueva variedad"; "Mandar catálogo" disabled with its reason in words.
  - "Mandar catálogo" disabled ("Cargá una variedad con precio y su stock") only while the first-run checklist shows and nothing qualifies.
  - Loading, error and offline use `Estados`: loading holds no digit or amount; generic retry; "Sin conexión".
  - Computer: with nothing selected, the first variety by name is selected in the right pane, at no tap. With no varieties the right pane holds no figure. Phone: the list never opens a variety by itself.
  - Opened or selected variety gone: `Estados` "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades", no id or name.
- **PRD change:** merge the Stock list into this screen: add the summary line, "Mandar catálogo", "Entrada de stock", "Todas las entradas" and the price column. Old paths redirect.

### A variety's page (phone `VariedadDetalle`; computer right pane of `VariedadesCompu`)

- **Built from:** `VariedadDetalle`, a detail ("‹ Variedades", bar or sidebar, no "+ Nueva venta").
- **Structure:**
  - the name with "Editar" (opens `VariedadNueva`);
  - one summary line (e.g. "64 g · ok · 21 días · $ 11.000 x g", plus "Oculta" when hidden);
  - two equal buttons, "+ Entrada" and "Nuevo precio";
  - four sections: Stock y entradas, Precios, Aviso de stock bajo, En el catálogo.
- **Stock y entradas / Precios:** last 3 rows each and "Ver todas". Precios rows carry "Rige desde", newest first, and a delete button.

#### Aviso de stock bajo (revised: the amount can be set)

- **At rest:** the existing toggle plus the line as drawn. Set: "Avisar con menos de 20 g" and, when stock is positive, "Avisando: quedan 18,5 g". Off: "Aviso apagado". No "Ver todas".
- **Setting or changing the amount:** an "Editar" link in the section header, the same text-link treatment as the variety's "Editar", opens a small form built from "Encabezado de formulario/hoja" ("Cerrar", no bar, no "+ Nueva venta"; a centred dialog on the computer, as Componentes draws for forms).
  - One "Campo" of type gramos, label "Avisar con menos de", with the "g" unit.
  - Primary button "Guardá el aviso".
  - Entry is a single path: Editar → type grams → Guardá el aviso.
- **Turning on:** flipping the toggle from off to on with no amount saved opens this same form straight away, empty. The toggle only changes to on after the amount is saved. With a previously saved amount, the toggle turns on at that amount in one tap, and the toast "Aviso guardado" shows.
- **Turning off:** one tap on the toggle. It saves at once, shows "Aviso apagado" and the toast "Aviso guardado". The threshold value is turned off, not stored.
- **Refusals** use "Campo … con error": an inline message under the field, wording from the domain's fixed string, typed value kept. Zero, negative and more than two decimals are refused.
- **"Cerrar"** with a changed field opens the Confirmación of Estados. An untouched form closes at once.
- **Save:** toast "Aviso guardado" (no data), then the form closes and the page and list refresh.

#### En el catálogo (revised: one explicit save)

- **At rest:** a read view, not inline inputs. It shows "Mostrar en el catálogo" as a state line (word "Se muestra" or "Oculta", with the toggle shape), Emoji, % índica and Aroma/sabor as label and value, with "—" for an empty optional value. A single "Editar" link sits in the section header. No "Ver todas". Anchor `#en-el-catalogo`, the target of the catalog screen's links.
- **Editing:** "Editar" opens a form (same hoja chrome, "Cerrar", no bar) with:
  - the toggle "Mostrar en el catálogo";
  - "Campo" texto for Emoji;
  - "Campo" monto-style for % índica;
  - "Campo" texto for Aroma/sabor (one line, 200 characters);
  - primary button "Guardá el catálogo".
- **Why a form, not auto-save:** the PRD says refusals keep what was typed and unsaved changes ask before discarding. A form gives both with existing components and one save moment.
- **Save:** toast "Catálogo guardado" (no data), the form closes, the page refreshes.
- **Refusals** (inline, "Campo … con error", typed value kept, never repeating the input):
  - Emoji not exactly one emoji;
  - % índica not a whole number from 0 to 100;
  - Aroma over 200 characters.
- **Unsaved typing:** "Cerrar" opens the Confirmación of Estados. An untouched form closes at once.
- **Deep link:** a link with `#en-el-catalogo` scrolls to this section on the page (phone) or in the right pane (computer), with the variety selected.

#### Other states of the page

- **No entries:** Stock y entradas reads "Sin entradas de stock · Cargá tu stock inicial o una compra"; "+ Entrada" preselects "Stock inicial".
- **No prices:** Precios reads "Sin precio: no se puede vender".
- **Hidden:** "Oculta" in the summary line. **Warning off:** "Aviso apagado".
- **Loading, error, offline:** `Estados`.
- **Gone** (including another business's): "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades".
- **Toasts, no data:** "Variedad guardada", "Precio guardado", "Precio eliminado", "Aviso guardado", "Catálogo guardado", "Entrada guardada".

#### Delete a price (not drawn: composed)

- The delete button on a Precios row opens the Confirmación of Estados ("Antes de borrar"), with "No, dejalo" and "Sí, borralo". The copy names the date and amount and says what takes its place, and never mentions sales:
  - with an earlier entry: "¿Borrás el precio de $ 11.000 x g del 12/09? Vuelve a regir $ 9.000 x g desde el 05/08.";
  - first of several: "…Queda sin precio hasta el dd/mm.";
  - the only entry: "…La variedad queda «Sin precio: no se puede vender».".
- Then the toast "Precio eliminado".

### Forms opened from a variety: `EntradaStock`, `PrecioNuevo`, `VariedadNueva` (add and rename)

- **Built from:** `EntradaStock` / `EntradaStockCompu`, `PrecioNuevo` / `PrecioNuevoCompu`, `VariedadNueva` / `VariedadNuevaCompu`. Each is a form: "Cerrar", no bar, no "+ Nueva venta", a Confirmación on unsaved leave, and an untouched form closes at once.
- **EntradaStock:**
  - Fields: date filled with today; variety with "Hoy tiene N g"; origin Compra · Cosecha · Stock inicial · Ajuste; total cost ("Obligatorio cuando es una compra"); note; "Te queda en $ X el gramo" whenever there is a cost.
  - Opened from the list, the variety is to be chosen. Opened from a page, the variety is chosen, and "Stock inicial" is preselected only if it has no entries.
  - It saves with "Entrada guardada".
- **States:**
  - Empty and filled.
  - Inline refusals keeping what was typed: future date, purchase without cost, zero grams, duplicate price date, bulk not below list, bulk without minimum, duplicate name.
  - Variety gone: the form closes and the fixed notice "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día." shows.
- **PRD change:** no visual change. Only where each form is reached from, and the variety preselection, are new.

### Todas las entradas and "Ver todas" lists (`TodasLasEntradas`, `VerTodas`)

- **Built from:** `TodasLasEntradas` (every entry of the business: date, variety, origin, grams, total cost, cost per gram; newest first; each editable or deletable) and `VerTodas` (price history). Both are cards: bar or sidebar and a "‹" back, no "+ Nueva venta".
- **States:**
  - Filled.
  - Empty: "Sin entradas de stock · Cargá tu stock inicial o una compra".
  - Loading, error, offline.
  - Edit confirmed before saving.
  - Delete confirmed with "Antes de borrar", then the toast "Entrada eliminada".
  - Gone: "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades".
- **Stock y entradas "Ver todas":** it follows the prototype and opens `TodasLasEntradas` ("‹ Variedades"), not a per-variety list. This differs from the PRD's "‹ <variety>"; it goes on the PR's design-vs-docs list for Design Lead to settle. Precios' "Ver todas" opens `VerTodas` with "‹" back to the variety.

### Catalog screen, "Mandar catálogo" (`Catalogo` / `CatalogoCompu`, `/varieties/catalog`)

- **Built from:** `Catalogo` / `CatalogoCompu`, a detail ("‹ Variedades", bar or sidebar, no "+ Nueva venta"). Reached from Variedades and from Inicio's out-of-date notice.
- **Structure, top to bottom:**
  - "Cómo se ve en Telegram" (preview with left-to-right `**` pairing);
  - Compartir as the main action, with Copiar beside it;
  - the pin status with Publicar;
  - "Qué no sale y por qué";
  - "Editar encabezado y pie".
- **Compartir** appears only where `navigator.share` exists; otherwise only Copiar shows.
  - Cancel shows nothing.
  - A real failure shows the fixed line "No se pudo compartir." in the existing inline-message treatment, with Copiar still available.
- **Copiar:** success shows the toast "Catálogo copiado"; failure shows "No se pudo copiar." with no toast. No message carries catalog text.
- **Both failing:** both fixed lines show together under the actions. The preview text stays selectable, so the seller can copy it by hand. No new component.
- **Nothing qualifies (not drawn):**
  - The screen opens with the empty-state treatment in the preview's place: "Hoy no hay nada en el catálogo".
  - Compartir and Copiar are not shown, since there is no text. "Qué no sale y por qué" still lists every variety.
  - The pin keeps "Al día" and its own empty message.
- **Pin status:**
  - Before the first Publicar, none shows.
  - Otherwise "Al día", or "Desactualizado" with one of the five fixed reasons, plus "El último fue el dd/mm" when the current chat has been published to.
  - A failed Publicar shows its fixed reason inline, including "Es demasiado largo para Telegram: acortá aromas u ocultá variedades."
- **Publicar unavailable:** disabled, with the reason in words ("Probalo primero para ver cómo queda", or the channel is missing) and a link to the bot settings (the temporary place). The prototype's wording ("Conectá el bot en Integraciones…") is replaced by the docs' wording. After success, the toast "Catálogo publicado".
- **"Qué no sale y por qué":**
  - Each variety is named, with one reason, the first that applies: oculta, sin precio, agotada, or sin entradas.
  - Reasons are told by word, as small chips or text in the existing treatments; "oculta" and "sin precio" reuse the same chip form as "agotada" and "sin entradas".
  - Each name links to that variety's `#en-el-catalogo`.
- **Loading, error, offline:** `Estados`.
- **PRD change:** the screen moves to `/varieties/catalog` (`/catalogo` redirects), gains Compartir as the main action, and links out. It edits no header, footer or bot setting.

### Temporary place for the bot settings and the header and footer

- **Built from:** no prototype, by the PRD's design. It reuses today's bot and header/footer forms unchanged. If Configuración already exists, it is not built and both links go to Configuración → Integraciones and Mensaje del catálogo.
- **Title:** "Bot y mensaje del catálogo", in the "Encabezado de formulario/hoja" treatment (the title is a placeholder for Design Lead to confirm, since the prototype has none).
- **Chrome:**
  - A form: "Cerrar" goes back by history, or to the catalog screen when there is none.
  - Confirmación on unsaved leave.
  - No bar, no "+ Nueva venta".
  - No tab, Más entry, Configuración group or "coming soon" mark.
  - No pin status and no Publicar.
- **Reached only from** "Editar encabezado y pie" and Publicar's unavailable link.
- **States:**
  - Empty fields, no "Guardado" line.
  - Token saved: "Guardado el dd/mm · no se muestra más" with "Cambiar".
  - Probarlo success: toast "Prueba enviada", values saved.
  - Probarlo failure: a fixed inline reason, nothing saved, typing kept.
  - Header/footer saved: "Catálogo guardado".
  - After a token or channel change: "El mensaje fijado anterior queda como está; desfijalo vos en Telegram."
  - Loading, error, offline.

### Shared states and redirects (`Estados`)

- **Built from:** `Estados`, on every screen above: loading (no figures), empty, "Sin conexión", the generic retry, "Eso ya no está." (opened record gone, with its "Volver a …" button) and the fixed notice "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día." after a save, delete or undo on a gone record. The notice is announced like a toast and has no "Deshacer" or "Reintentar". Computer layouts use the same words and shapes.
- **Redirects:** `/variedades`, `/mi-stock`, `/mi-stock/nueva-entrada`, `/catalogo` and the other old Stock, Variedades and Catálogo paths redirect permanently; they have no screen. An unknown path goes to Inicio.

### Gaps for Design Lead

1. The three forms added here (threshold amount, catalog attributes, and the temporary place's title) are composed from Componentes without a prototype screen. Each should be confirmed by eye.
2. "Oculta" and "Sin precio" chips, the "oculta"/"sin precio" reasons, the empty catalog state and the delete-price confirmation reuse existing treatments but are not drawn in the prototype.
3. The prototype's Stock y entradas "Ver todas" opens the global entries list ("‹ Variedades"), against the PRD's "‹ <variety>".
4. Inicio's out-of-date notice is not on `Inicio` / `InicioCompu`.
5. The computer list drops the status word and keeps the mark; the word stays as the accessible name.

### Comparisons

I compared the screens design with the prototype in `docs/product/design-system/pantallas` (Variedades, VariedadesCompu, VariedadDetalle, Catalogo, CatalogoCompu, EntradaStock, Componentes). No side-by-side images reached this turn, so the judgement is on the text against the prototype.

**Variedades list (phone and computer)**
- **Matches the prototype:** the order is summary line, "Mandar catálogo", "Entrada de stock" as a secondary button, "Nueva variedad", rows sorted by name, and "Todas las entradas" at the foot. Both Variedades and VariedadesCompu draw it in that order.
- **Rows and states that match:** the status mark and word, the detail line, grams and days left, the `x g` price, and the "sin entradas" count.
- **Specified correctly:**
  - the summary states (all ok, mixed, none);
  - "Mandar catálogo" disabled with its reason;
  - the empty, loading, offline and gone states;
  - automatic selection on the computer.
- *Observation:* the computer rows show the mark without the word (criterion 4).
- *Observation:* "Oculta" and "Sin precio: no se puede vender" aren't drawn. Building them from the existing chip and text treatments is accepted.

**A variety's page (VariedadDetalle; right pane of VariedadesCompu)**
- **Matches the prototype:**
  - "‹ Variedades", "Editar" → VariedadNueva;
  - the summary line;
  - "+ Entrada" → EntradaStock and "Nuevo precio" → PrecioNuevo;
  - Stock y entradas and Precios with their last 3 rows and "Ver todas";
  - the En el catálogo anchor.
- **Blocking, Aviso de stock bajo:** the PRD requires the threshold *and its edit action*. The design has only a toggle and a fixed "Avisar con menos de 20 g". Nothing sets or changes the amount, nothing says which amount applies on first turn-on, and "Aviso guardado" has no moment to fire. This is a required state missing, and Componentes already has the grams field to build it.
- **Blocking, En el catálogo:** these are inline fields with no save moment, no "Catálogo guardado" timing, no inline refusals and no rule for unsaved typing. The PRD requires all of these, and "Campo … con error" in Componentes covers the refusals.
- *Observation:* the delete-price confirmation isn't drawn (`#borrar` goes nowhere). Its copy should be written.
- *Observation:* the Stock y entradas "Ver todas" goes to the global list, not "‹ <variety>". The prototype leads, so this goes on the PR's list of design-vs-docs differences.

**Forms (EntradaStock, PrecioNuevo, VariedadNueva)**
- They match: "Cerrar", "Guardá la entrada" / "Guardá el precio", the variety and origin preselection, and inline refusals that keep what was typed.

**Todas las entradas and Ver todas**
- They match as cards with no "+ Nueva venta". The empty state is specified.

**Catalog screen (Catalogo / CatalogoCompu)**
- **Matches the prototype:** the preview, Compartir as the main action with Copiar, the "Al día" and "Desactualizado" pin states with Publicar, "Qué no sale" and the "encabezado" link, on both phone and computer.
- **Behaviour:** the Compartir and Copiar behaviour and its fixed lines match the PRD.
- *Observation:* the empty catalog, and Compartir and Copiar failing together, aren't drawn.
- *Observation:* the "oculta" and "sin precio" reasons aren't drawn.
- *Observation:* the Publicar-unavailable wording differs from the docs. The docs win; list the difference in the PR.

**Temporary place**
- No prototype, by design. It reuses today's forms, and its chrome and limits follow the PRD.
- *Observation:* its title is still open.

**Estados and redirects**
- They match the shared Estados component. Redirects have no screen.

### Verdict

`returned`: on the variety's page, the threshold amount and the En el catálogo fields have no edit or save states, and the PRD requires both. UI Designer must specify them using Componentes' grams field, its error state and the existing save toasts.

## PM: consolidation

### Verdict

`approved`: the PRD, the validated flow and the validated screens agree. Two blocking design gaps go to the human as questions, and everything else travels as observations.

### PRD

#### Job to be done

**When** a customer asks me what I have or how much something costs, or flower comes in, runs out or gets a new price, **I want** to open one variety and find everything about it on its own page: its stock and entries, its prices, its low-stock warning and how it shows in the catalog. I also want the list to tell me at a glance what I have, what is running out and what I can't sell, and I want today's catalog one tap from that list, ready to share from my phone. **So I can** answer the customer, record what came in and send an up-to-date catalog without moving between screens, and my customers never get a stale price or a variety I can't sell.

- **The main situation** is mid-chat, between orders. Flower arriving, running out or being repriced comes second.
- **Not a new job.** This is the navigation job (navigation.md) applied to varieties, stock and the catalog. The stock and catalog jobs stay in stock.md and catalog.md, and upkeep stays in pinned-catalog.md. The computer is the same job, on two panes.
- **The pain today:**
  - one variety is split across Stock, Variedades and Catálogo;
  - sending the catalog means copying the text and switching apps;
  - the list does not show, on its own, what is running out or can't be sold.

**Success criteria**

1. **Two taps at most.** From any tab root, the catalog screen and the stock entry form open in at most 2 taps, and a computer never takes more taps than the phone. An entry started from a variety's page has that variety chosen, with "Stock inicial" preselected only when it has no entries.
2. **One variety, one page.** Stock, status, days left and today's price are read on the page. Prices, the threshold and catalog attributes are edited there and nowhere else.
3. **Sharing in one tap.** Compartir hands the share sheet exactly the text Copiar copies, `**` included. Without a share sheet, Compartir is not shown and Copiar remains. It works over plain http.
4. **Nothing guessed.** Status is told by shape and word, never by color alone. The summary line is never blank. Every variety left out of the catalog shows exactly one reason, in words.
5. **Nothing lost, nothing invented.** Every old action is still reachable, every old path redirects permanently, and no link points at a missing screen.
6. **Businesses never mix** (ADR-0004).

#### Competitors

There is no real data, and none was studied. The criteria come from the product's docs and PM's decisions.

#### How to read the flows

- A touch is one tap, plus one per field typed. The OS share sheet's own steps are not counted.
- The tab roots are Inicio, Ventas, Clientes, Variedades, Gastos, Análisis and Más.
- The visual language is "Papel y azul" with its tokens, and no token or component is added. Copy is Rioplatense Spanish with vos: `$ 11.000`, `3,5 g`, `dd/mm`, and the catalog text keeps `$11.000`.
- A step marked *composed* is built from Componentes and Estados, with no prototype screen of its own.

#### Situation 1: a customer asks what I have or what it costs

**Flow**
- **Phone:** Variedades (1 touch) shows the list. If the list doesn't answer it, the variety's row (2) opens its page.
- **Computer:** Variedades in the sidebar (1) already shows the first variety by name in the right pane, at no tap. Clicking another row (2) replaces it.
- **Ends** on the list or a variety's page.
- **Recovery:**
  - With no varieties: the empty state and "Nueva variedad".
  - A gone or foreign variety: "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades".
  - No network: "Sin conexión". A server error: the generic retry.

**Screen: the Variedades list** (`Variedades`; the left pane of `VariedadesCompu`), a tab root
- **Top to bottom:** the summary line, "Mandar catálogo", "Entrada de stock" (a secondary button, not floating), "Nueva variedad", rows by name, and "Todas las entradas" at the foot.
- **A row:**
  - the name;
  - `MarcaEstado`: square = agotada (the inverted chip), filled circle = baja, empty circle = ok, or "Sin entradas", each with its word;
  - the detail line;
  - grams and days left;
  - today's list price per gram, or "Sin precio: no se puede vender";
  - "Oculta" as a chip beside the status, never in its place.
- **The summary line:**
  - "N variedades · todo ok", or each non-ok status counted by word in stock-status order;
  - with no varieties, there is no summary line, and the fixed empty state shows instead.
- **"Mandar catálogo"** is disabled, with "Cargá una variedad con precio y su stock", only while the first-run checklist shows and nothing qualifies.
- **On a computer** with no varieties, the right pane shows no figure. **On the phone**, the list never opens a variety by itself.
- **Loading, error and offline** follow `Estados`. Loading shows no digit or amount.

#### Situation 2: flower comes in, I record a stock entry

**Flow**
- **From the list:** Variedades (1), "Entrada de stock" (2) opens the form with the date set to today. Choose the variety (3) and the origin (4), type the grams (5) and, for a purchase, the cost (6), then "Guardá la entrada" (7). A harvest takes 6.
- **From the page:** the page (2), "+ Entrada" (3) with the variety chosen, the grams (4) and the cost (5), then save (6). On a computer it takes 5.
- **Ends** where the form was opened, with the toast "Entrada guardada" and stock updated.
- **Recovery:**
  - Inline refusals (future date, a purchase without cost, zero grams) keep what was typed.
  - "Cerrar" with typing asks first. An untouched form closes at once.
  - A gone variety closes the form with "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día."

**Screen: `EntradaStock` / `EntradaStockCompu`**, a form ("Cerrar", no bar, no "+ Nueva venta")
- the date, filled with today;
- the variety, with "Hoy tiene N g";
- the origin: Compra · Cosecha · Stock inicial · Ajuste;
- the total cost, with "Obligatorio cuando es una compra";
- the note;
- "Te queda en $ X el gramo" whenever there is a cost.

No visual change from today. Only where it is reached from, and the preselection, are new.

#### Situation 3: a price changes

**Flow**
- The page (2), "Nuevo precio" (3), the list price (4), save (5): the toast "Precio guardado". A bulk price and its minimum add 2.
- **Fixing a typo:** the row's delete button (3), then confirm (4): the toast "Precio eliminado".
- **"Ver todas"** in Precios opens the full history.
- **Recovery:** inline refusals (a duplicate date, bulk not below list, bulk without its minimum) keep what was typed.

**Screens**
- **`PrecioNuevo` / `PrecioNuevoCompu`:** a form, unchanged.
- **`VerTodas`:** a card with "‹" back to the variety and no "+ Nueva venta".
- **Delete confirmation** (*composed*, the Estados Confirmación "Antes de borrar", "No, dejalo" / "Sí, borralo"). It never mentions sales.
