  - With an earlier entry: "¿Borrás el precio de $ 11.000 x g del 12/09? Vuelve a regir $ 9.000 x g desde el 05/08."
  - The first of several: "…Queda sin precio hasta el dd/mm."
  - The only entry: "…La variedad queda «Sin precio: no se puede vender»."

#### Situation 4: the low-stock warning and how the variety shows in the catalog

These two sections are the subject of the questions for the human below. The behaviour here is the validated screens design, pending the human's answer.

**The variety's page** (`VariedadDetalle`; the right pane of `VariedadesCompu`), a detail ("‹ Variedades", no "+ Nueva venta")
- The name, with "Editar", which opens `VariedadNueva` to rename: "Variedad guardada", and a duplicate name refused inline.
- One summary line, e.g. "64 g · ok · 21 días · $ 11.000 x g", plus "Oculta" when hidden.
- Two equal buttons: "+ Entrada" and "Nuevo precio".
- **Stock y entradas** and **Precios:** their last 3 rows and "Ver todas".
  - With no entries: "Sin entradas de stock · Cargá tu stock inicial o una compra".
  - With no prices: "Sin precio: no se puede vender".

**Aviso de stock bajo** (*composed* form)
- **At rest:** the toggle, plus "Avisar con menos de 20 g" / "Avisando: quedan 18,5 g", or "Aviso apagado". No "Ver todas".
- **Setting the amount:** an "Editar" link (3) opens a form with "Cerrar", one gramos field "Avisar con menos de" and "Guardá el aviso". About 5 touches.
- **Turning on** with no saved amount opens that form. With a saved amount, one tap turns it on at that amount.
- **Turning off** is one tap and saves at once.
- **The toast** is "Aviso guardado".
- **Refusals** (zero, negative, more than two decimals) show inline, through "Campo con error", and keep the value.
- **An unsaved "Cerrar"** asks first.

**En el catálogo** (*composed* form), anchor `#en-el-catalogo`
- **At rest:** a read view with "Se muestra" or "Oculta", and Emoji, % índica and Aroma/sabor ("—" when empty), with one "Editar". No "Ver todas".
- **Editing:** a form with the toggle "Mostrar en el catálogo", Emoji, % índica (no money prefix), Aroma/sabor (one line, 200 characters) and "Guardá el catálogo". The toast is "Catálogo guardado".
- **Refusals** show inline and never repeat the input:
  - an emoji that isn't exactly one emoji;
  - % índica that isn't a whole number from 0 to 100;
  - an aroma over 200 characters.
- **An unsaved "Cerrar"** asks first.
- **A link** to `#en-el-catalogo` scrolls to this section, with the variety selected on a computer.
- **These attributes and the threshold** are edited nowhere else.

#### Situation 5: send the catalog (Compartir)

**Flow**
- Variedades (1), "Mandar catálogo" (2), "Compartir" (3), then the app and chat are picked in the share sheet. On a computer it is the same.
- **Without a share sheet:** "Copiar" (3) gives the toast "Catálogo copiado", and the seller pastes by hand.
- **Ends** on the catalog screen, with no toast after sharing.
- **Recovery:**
  - A cancel shows nothing.
  - A real failure shows "No se pudo compartir.", and Copiar stays.
  - A copy failure shows "No se pudo copiar." and no toast.
  - If both fail, both lines show, and the preview stays selectable for copying by hand.

**Screen: `Catalogo` / `CatalogoCompu`** (`/varieties/catalog`), a detail ("‹ Variedades"), reached from Variedades and from Inicio's out-of-date notice
- **Top to bottom:** "Cómo se ve en Telegram" (`**` paired left to right), Compartir (the main action, shown only where `navigator.share` exists) with Copiar beside it, the pin status with Publicar, "Qué no sale y por qué", and "Editar encabezado y pie".
- **Nothing qualifies** (*composed*):
  - "Hoy no hay nada en el catálogo" shows in place of the preview;
  - Compartir and Copiar are not shown;
  - "Qué no sale y por qué" still lists every variety;
  - the pin keeps its empty message and "Al día".
- **No message** carries catalog text, a name or an amount.
- The screen itself edits no header, footer or bot setting.

#### Situation 6: why a variety isn't in the catalog, and the fix

**Flow**
- On the catalog screen, each left-out variety shows one reason, the first that applies: oculta, sin precio, agotada or sin entradas.
- Tapping the name (3 from a tab root) opens its page at `#en-el-catalogo`.
- **The fix, by reason:**
  - oculta: the En el catálogo form;
  - sin precio: "Nuevo precio";
  - agotada or sin entradas: "+ Entrada".
- Back returns to the catalog screen.

**Screen:** the reasons are told by word, as chips. "oculta" and "sin precio" reuse the chip form of "agotada" and "sin entradas".

#### Situation 7: keep the pinned message current

**Flow**
- On the catalog screen, read the status and tap Publicar (3 from a tab root, 2 from Inicio's notice).
- The toast is "Catálogo publicado", then "Al día" with "El último fue el dd/mm".
- **Recovery:**
  - A failure shows its fixed reason inline. For a text that is too long, it reads "Es demasiado largo para Telegram: acortá aromas u ocultá variedades.", and the seller shortens an aroma or hides a variety, then publishes again.
  - When Publicar is unavailable, it shows disabled with its reason in words ("Probalo primero para ver cómo queda", or that the channel is missing) and a link to the bot settings.

**Screen**
- Before the first Publicar ever, no status shows.
- Otherwise it reads "Al día", or "Desactualizado" with one of the five fixed reasons.
- The docs' wording replaces the prototype's.

#### Situation 8: connect the bot, or edit the header and footer (temporary place)

**Flow**
- **Header and footer:** Variedades (1), "Mandar catálogo" (2), "Editar encabezado y pie" (3), edit, then save: "Catálogo guardado". "Cerrar" goes back. About 7 touches.
- **The bot:** Publicar's unavailable link (3), then the token, the own chat and the channel chat, then Probarlo: "Prueba enviada", and the values are saved. Then "Cerrar" and Publicar. About 12 touches.
- **Recovery:**
  - A failed Probarlo saves nothing, keeps what was typed and shows a fixed reason.
  - Leaving with unsaved changes asks first.
  - After a token or channel change, the status reads "Desactualizado · Falta publicarlo con el bot o el canal nuevo".

**Screen: "Bot y mensaje del catálogo"** (no prototype, by design)
- It reuses today's bot and header/footer forms unchanged, as a form.
- "Cerrar" goes back by history, or to the catalog screen when there is none.
- It has no bar, tab, Más entry, Configuración group, "coming soon" mark, pin status or Publicar.
- **Its states:**
  - empty fields and no "Guardado" line;
  - "Guardado el dd/mm · no se muestra más" with "Cambiar";
  - after a token or channel change: "El mensaje fijado anterior queda como está; desfijalo vos en Telegram.".
- **The exception it makes:** this is a deliberate, temporary exception to catalog.md's "only in Configuración" and pinned-catalog.md's "Integraciones is the only place".
- **Removal:** Configuración's issue retargets both links and removes the temporary place. If Configuración ships first, the temporary place is not built.

#### Situation 9: old paths and bookmarks

- `/variedades`, `/mi-stock`, `/mi-stock/nueva-entrada`, `/catalogo` and every other old Stock, Variedades or Catálogo path redirect permanently to their route in `scripts/design/screens.json`. That takes 0 touches.
- An unknown address goes to Inicio.
- A reload on any `/varieties…` route opens the screen, never the API's JSON. The parameterised routes need the Tech Lead's decision under ADR-0024, through an amendment or a new ADR.

#### Situation 10: Todas las entradas

**Flow**
- From the list: Variedades (1), then "Todas las entradas" (2).
- From a page: "Ver todas" in Stock y entradas (3), which opens the same business-wide list.
- Editing an entry is confirmed before saving. Deleting is confirmed with "Antes de borrar", then the toast "Entrada eliminada". Each takes 4 to 6 touches.

**Screen: `TodasLasEntradas`**, a card ("‹ Variedades")
- It shows the date, variety, origin, grams, total cost and cost per gram, newest first.
- Empty: "Sin entradas de stock · Cargá tu stock inicial o una compra".
- An entry that is gone: "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades".

#### Shared states (`Estados`)

- **On every screen above:** loading with no figures, empty, "Sin conexión", the generic retry and "Eso ya no está." with its "Volver a …".
- **After a save, delete or undo on a gone record:** the fixed notice "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día.", announced like a toast, with no "Deshacer" or "Reintentar".
- **Computer layouts** use the same words and shapes.
- **No state** is told by color alone, in either theme.
- **Unchanged:** no rule of stock, status, days left, prices, catalog text, attributes, the pinned catalog, upkeep or isolation changes. Only where they are reached and shown changes.

#### Metrics

There is no telemetry. Each metric is a test or a check by eye.

- **Two taps (1):** the end-to-end run counts the taps from every tab root to the catalog screen and to the entry form, on phone and computer: 100% within 2, and a computer never more than the phone. The preselection from a page is checked.
- **One page (2):**
  - no other screen edits prices, the threshold or catalog attributes;
  - the end-to-end run adds a variety, adds and deletes a price, renames, sets and turns off a threshold, and edits the catalog attributes, all from the page;
  - only Stock y entradas and Precios carry their last 3 rows and "Ver todas".
- **Share = copy (3):**
  - with a stubbed share sheet, Compartir hands exactly the API's text;
  - with none, Compartir is hidden and Copiar works;
  - a cancel shows nothing;
  - a forced failure shows "No se pudo compartir." and leaves Copiar;
  - no message holds catalog text.
- **Status (4):**
  - the summary line is covered for no varieties, all ok and a mix;
  - each status shows its mark and word, with "Oculta" beside it;
  - every left-out variety has one reason, in priority order;
  - color is checked by eye in both themes.
- **Always opens (4):** the catalog screen opens with nothing qualifying. "Mandar catálogo" is disabled only while the checklist shows and nothing qualifies.
- **Two panes:**
  - on a computer, the first variety by name is selected;
  - with no varieties, the right pane holds no figure;
  - on the phone, the list never opens a variety.
- **Nothing lost (5):**
  - every old path redirects, tested one by one;
  - the ADR-0024 entries are covered by the contract test;
  - every old action maps to a new place;
  - no link points at a missing screen.
- **Temporary place (5):**
  - it saves the bot and the header and footer;
  - both links open it;
  - it shows no status and no Publicar, and nothing else points to it;
  - the end-to-end run goes through Probarlo, Publicar and editing the header and footer.

  If Configuración ships first, the same checks run against its groups.
- **Isolation (6):**
  - no new route answers business data;
  - another business's variety shows "Eso ya no está.";
  - the console and browser storage hold no names, amounts or catalog text.
- **Design:** `scripts/design/compare.sh` runs before and after for the list, the page and the catalog screen, phone and computer, attached to the PR. The composed forms and states are checked by eye, and every difference between the design and the docs is listed in the PR.
- **Speed:** Variedades and the catalog screen answer in under 300 ms with a few dozen varieties.
- **No regression:** the existing suite passes with no test edited to fit the new look, apart from tests of the removed screens.

#### Out of scope

- Configuración and its groups.
- Any redesign of the bot settings or of the header and footer editing.
- Other areas of the app.
- Removing anything beyond the screens named.
- A competitor study.

### Questions for the human

1. On a variety's page, the "Aviso de stock bajo" section in your design has an on/off switch and the fixed text "Avisar con menos de 20 g", but nothing to set or change that amount. A seller cannot choose when the warning fires, so the low-stock job cannot be completed as drawn. The screens design suggests an "Editar" link that opens a small form ("Avisar con menos de" in grams, "Guardá el aviso", "Cerrar"). Turning the warning on with no saved amount would open that same form, and turning it off would save at once. Do you accept this, or do you want to draw it yourself?
2. On a variety's page, the "En el catálogo" section in your design shows the switch "Mostrar en el catálogo" and the Emoji, % índica and Aroma/sabor fields as always-editable boxes. There is no Save button and no way to close, and nothing shows how a change is saved, where an error (for example an aroma over 200 characters) appears, or what happens to text typed and not saved. A seller cannot be sure their catalog change was kept. The screens design suggests showing the values read-only, with an "Editar" link that opens one form ending in "Guardá el catálogo". That form would show errors under each field and ask before discarding unsaved text. Do you accept this change from your drawing, or do you want to draw the save yourself?

### Observations on the design

- **Variedades list (computer):** rows show the mark without its word. The word is kept only as the accessible name, which sits uneasily with criterion 4.
- **Variedades list rows:** the "Oculta" chip and "Sin precio: no se puede vender" are not drawn. They reuse existing treatments.
- **Precios, deleting a price:** the delete button points to `#borrar`, which goes nowhere. The confirmation and its three copy cases are composed, not drawn.
- **Stock y entradas "Ver todas":** it opens the business-wide TodasLasEntradas ("‹ Variedades"), not a per-variety list with "‹ <variety>" as the PRD says. Back lands on the list. The prototype is followed, and the difference is listed in the PR.
- **En el catálogo form:** the % índica field must not carry the money prefix.
- **Catalog screen, Publicar unavailable:** the prototype's wording ("Conectá el bot en Integraciones…") differs from the docs'. The docs' wording is used, and the difference is listed in the PR.
- **Catalog screen:** the empty catalog, both actions failing together, and the "oculta" and "sin precio" reasons are composed, not drawn.
- **Catalog screen links:** the prototype points to ConfiguracionMensaje and ConfiguracionIntegraciones. While Configuración does not exist, they go to the temporary place.
- **Temporary place:** it has no prototype, by design. Its title "Bot y mensaje del catálogo" is a placeholder.
- **Inicio:** the notice "Tu catálogo en Telegram está desactualizado" is not drawn on Inicio or InicioCompu.

## Human

### Decision 1

Escalated: PM: consolidation could not complete the job without 2 point(s) UX marked as impeding it; see the questions

**Questions this answers:**

1. 1. On a variety's page, the "Aviso de stock bajo" section in your design has an on/off switch and the fixed text "Avisar con menos de 20 g", but nothing to set or change that amount. A seller cannot choose when the warning fires, so the low-stock job cannot be completed as drawn. The screens design suggests an "Editar" link that opens a small form ("Avisar con menos de" in grams, "Guardá el aviso", "Cerrar"). Turning the warning on with no saved amount would open that same form, and turning it off would save at once. Do you accept this, or do you want to draw it yourself?
2. 2. On a variety's page, the "En el catálogo" section in your design shows the switch "Mostrar en el catálogo" and the Emoji, % índica and Aroma/sabor fields as always-editable boxes. There is no Save button and no way to close, and nothing shows how a change is saved, where an error (for example an aroma over 200 characters) appears, or what happens to text typed and not saved. A seller cannot be sure their catalog change was kept. The screens design suggests showing the values read-only, with an "Editar" link that opens one form ending in "Guardá el catálogo". That form would show errors under each field and ask before discarding unsaved text. Do you accept this change from your drawing, or do you want to draw the save yourself?

Both accepted as the screens design suggests:
1. Low-stock warning: values shown read-only with an "Editar" link opening a small form ("Avisar con menos de" in grams, "Guardá el aviso", "Cerrar"). Turning the warning on with no saved amount opens that same form; turning it off saves at once.
2. "En el catálogo": values shown read-only with an "Editar" link opening one form that ends in "Guardá el catálogo", errors under each field, and a confirmation before discarding unsaved text.

## Interview: Tech Lead efímero→Tech Lead

### Round 1: questions

Round 1. I read `app/static_spa.py`, `web/src/router.tsx`, `web/src/redirects.ts`, `scripts/design/screens.json` and the varieties, stock and catalog API routers.

**A concrete way the plan fails today.** The PRD puts the screens at `/varieties`, `/varieties/{low}` and `/varieties/catalog`. The API already serves `GET /varieties` and `GET /varieties/{variety_id}`, and the stock hexagon mounts a second `/varieties` router. `SpaStatic._falls_back_to_index` returns False for any API first segment. ADR-0024's `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS` is an exact set (today `/expenses` and `/expenses/new`). So a reload or a shared link at `/varieties/catalog` matches `/{variety_id}` and returns JSON or a 401, not the SPA. `/varieties/{low}` and `/varieties/{low}/prices/new` can't be listed in an exact set at all.

The front router currently uses Spanish paths (`variedades`, `mi-stock`, `catalogo`), so these collisions don't exist yet. The plan creates them.

---

❓ **Q1** - **Route shape and ADR-0024 for `/varieties/*`**: How do the parameterised and sibling paths get served? What is the slug format of `{low}`? Do `catalog`, `entries` and `new` become reserved words that no variety name may produce? The `contract.json` `frontSegments` rule and the `tests/test_refusal_codes.py` intersection check currently assume exact paths. How will you rewrite them?
➡️ Recommendation: write a new ADR (or amend 0024) before any screen work. Either extend the allowlist to a typed matcher (a pattern list gated by `Accept: text/html`, GET/HEAD only), or keep the front under non-colliding segments and redirect. Reserve `catalog`, `entries` and `new` in the matcher and in variety-name slugging. The matcher needs a test that a `curl` without `Accept: text/html` on `/varieties/catalog` still gets the API's answer.

❓ **Q2** - **Where the code goes and which boundaries it crosses**: Is this front-only? The new "Aviso de stock bajo" form and the "En el catálogo" form need endpoints. `PUT /catalog/varieties/{variety_id}` exists. `PATCH /varieties/{variety_id}` exists. Does the threshold have a save endpoint, and a "turn off saves at once" call? Are their refusal codes in `contract.json` (ADR-0008) so field errors show under each field? Which backend module owns each?
➡️ Recommendation: list, per section of the variety's page, the exact endpoint, request and refusal codes, and mark any that don't exist as a backend subtask that lands first. Split `VariedadDetalle.tsx` into one component per section (Stock y entradas, Precios, Aviso, En el catálogo), each with its own form hook. Don't build the temporary bot-settings place. `/settings/integrations` and `/settings/catalog-message` already exist, so the catalog screen's links go straight to them. The plan should say that in so many words.

❓ **Q3** - **Who computes "what does not go out and why", the summary line and the status marks?** `GET /catalog` returns only `text` (ADR-0022: one author of the catalog text). If the front derives the reasons by comparing varieties and prices, it becomes a second author of the rule. The same applies to the status marks and "Oculta".
➡️ Recommendation: have the backend return the reasons (a code per excluded variety, with Spanish text from one place) beside `text`, and the summary and marks from the existing list endpoint. The front only renders them. If an endpoint change is out of scope, say which derivation is allowed in the front and show it is not a copy of a backend rule.

❓ **Q4** - **Test seams and the standards on a codebase that may not meet them**: What are the seams for `navigator.share` (present, absent on plain http, `AbortError`, other rejection), for `clipboard.ts` (the http fallback for Copiar), and for the two-pane layout (auto-selected first variety)? The redirects table is "one line each, with its unit test and every e2e `goto`". Removing `Stock.tsx` and the `mi-stock/*` and `catalogo` routes deletes their tests. How does that fit "no test edited"? Which engineering standards (§9 privacy, §10 `forbiddenApis.ts`) does the share path touch?
➡️ Recommendation: inject `share` and `clipboard` as one small adapter (`shareCatalog(text)`) with a stub in tests, and test the 4 outcomes through it: cancel shows nothing, failure shows only "No se pudo compartir.", success shows nothing, and copy shows "Catálogo copiado". Never log the text. Add a redirect-table test per old path, `/variedades`, `/variedades/:id`, `/mi-stock`, `/mi-stock/nueva-entrada`, `/mi-stock/entradas/:id` and `/catalogo`. The `:id` ones need a param-carrying redirect, which `redirectRoutes` (it takes a fixed `from`/`to`) can't express today. Extend it with a test, or say how it's handled.

❓ **Q5** - **Subtask split and parallelism**: I count four candidate pieces. (a) Routing, redirects, `screens.json` and the ADR. (b) The list with two panes, removing Stock, and the Entrada de stock flow. (c) The variety page sections and the two forms. (d) The catalog screen with Compartir and the pin status. `router.tsx`, `redirects.ts`, `routes.ts` and `screens.json` are touched by all four, so they conflict in parallel. Piece (c) alone is several modules (4 sections, 2 forms, the discard guard) plus tests.
➡️ Recommendation: land (a) first and alone, with the route constants and empty route stubs that (b), (c) and (d) then fill without touching the shared files. Split (c) into "read-only sections with Editar links" and "the two forms with their guards", so neither carries more than about four production modules plus tests. Say which of these needs its own seam, and which backend change (Q2/Q3) is its own first subtask.

### Invariants proposed

- The text passed to Compartir and Copiar is exactly the `text` field of `GET /catalog`, never assembled, trimmed or altered in the front.
- Every old Spanish path that moves or disappears gets one entry in the redirects table with its own test in the same diff as the route that replaces it.
- A front path whose first segment is also an API segment is served only through the ADR-0024 mechanism (GET/HEAD with `Accept: text/html`), never by widening the SPA fallback.
- A share or copy failure shows only the fixed Spanish string, and no catalog text, variety name or amount reaches the console, storage or a message.
- No front code states why a variety is left out of the catalog or derives a status mark. It renders what the API returns.

### Round 1: answers

### Context read
I read `app/static_spa.py` (ADR-0024: `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS`, `LEGACY_FRONT_PATHS`, `FrontNavigation`), `web/src/router.tsx`, `redirects.ts`, `contract.json`'s `frontSegments`, `scripts/design/screens.json`, the varieties, stock, catalog and pinned-catalog routers and schemas, `clipboard.ts`, `architecture/forbiddenApis.ts`, `Catalogo.tsx` and `PinPublish.tsx`.

Two facts change the plan:
- **Configuración already exists.** `settings/integrations` and `settings/catalog-message` are routed, and `PinPublish.tsx` already links to `/settings/integrations`. The temporary place is **not built**, as the PRD's own condition says. Its metrics run against those two groups instead.
- **The backend already answers every rule this task needs.** No backend use case or schema changes. The only backend change is the routing layer in `app/static_spa.py`.

**Q1**: Accepted. I'm writing a new ADR-0027, which amends ADR-0024, in the first subtask and before any screen work.

**What `{low}` is.** It is the variety's integer id, as `/variedades/:id` is today. In `screens.json`, `{low}` is a sample-app placeholder ("the low-stock variety"), like `{email}`, not a slug.
- There is no slugging, so a rename never breaks a link.
- `catalog`, `entries` and `new` can never collide with an id, because the matcher accepts only `[0-9]+` in that position. No variety name is reserved.

**The matcher.** `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS` stays exact and gains `/varieties`, `/varieties/new`, `/varieties/catalog`, `/varieties/entries` and `/varieties/entries/new`. Beside it goes `SHARED_FRONT_PATTERNS`, a closed tuple of compiled, fully anchored patterns whose only variable is `[0-9]+`:
- `^/varieties/[0-9]+$`
- `^/varieties/[0-9]+/prices$`
- `^/varieties/[0-9]+/prices/new$`
- `^/varieties/entries/[0-9]+$`, the edit form for an entry (see below)

The rest of ADR-0024 is unchanged. Only GET or HEAD with `Accept: text/html` gets the shell, and the one-trailing-slash 308 rule applies. Everything else reaches the API.

**How the screens fetch.** The API calls stay on `/varieties/{id}` and `/varieties/{id}/prices`, and `api/client.ts` already sends `Accept: application/json`, so those calls are never served the shell. Pinned by tests:
- a request to `/varieties/catalog` or `/varieties/7` without `text/html` gets the API's answer (401 signed out);
- `/varieties/7/prices` with `Accept: application/json` gets the API's JSON;
- a pattern near-miss (`/varieties/7a`, `/varieties/7/prices/new/x`) is not served the shell.

**Two route choices not in `screens.json`, both mine:**
- Editing an entry from "Todas las entradas" is `/varieties/entries/{id}`, the new home of `/mi-stock/entradas/:id`.
- A variety's "Ver todas" of entries is `/varieties/entries?variety={id}`, the same screen filtered, with "‹ <variety>" as its back label.

**Legacy redirects** go server-side, as permanent 308s, through the same `FrontNavigation` that already serves `/mas` and `/gastos`:
- `LEGACY_FRONT_PATHS` gains `/variedades` → `/varieties`, `/mi-stock` → `/varieties`, `/mi-stock/nueva-entrada` → `/varieties/entries/new` and `/catalogo` → `/varieties/catalog`.
- A new `LEGACY_FRONT_PATTERNS` carries the id across for two paths: `/variedades/{n}` → `/varieties/{n}` and `/mi-stock/entradas/{n}` → `/varieties/entries/{n}`.
- These are not entries in the client `redirects.ts`. The server's 308 is the one "permanent" author. The existing `/negocio` and `/gastos/categorias` client entries coexist as they are.

**Contract test.**
- `frontSegments` gains `varieties`. The Spanish segments stay listed while they redirect, as `mas` and `gastos` do today.
- The rule in `tests/test_refusal_codes.py` becomes: the API ∩ front intersection holds only first segments of `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS` ∪ `SHARED_FRONT_PATTERNS`. That set is derived from both tables, never written by hand.
- One test per legacy path asserts 308 + `Location`, query kept.

**Q2**: Accepted, with the endpoints settled from the code. There is no backend subtask and no new endpoint.

| Section | Read | Write | Refusal codes (already in `contract.json`) |
|---|---|---|---|
| Summary line, Stock y entradas | `GET /stock` (the variety's row: grams, status, sold_out_since, threshold, days_left; entries filtered by `variety_id` on the client, last 3) plus `GET /varieties/{id}` (current price) | entries through the existing `POST/PUT/DELETE /stock/entries…` form | `stock.*` |
| Precios | `GET /varieties/{id}/prices` | `POST /varieties/{id}/prices`, `DELETE /varieties/{id}/prices/{entry_id}` | `variety.*` |
| Rename | — | `PATCH /varieties/{id}` | `variety.name_invalid`, name taken |
| Aviso de stock bajo | the threshold from `GET /stock` | `PUT /stock/varieties/{id}/threshold` with `{grams}`; turning off is the same PUT with `{"grams": null}`, saved at once | `stock.threshold_invalid` |
| En el catálogo | the variety's row in `GET /catalog` (`shown`, emoji, indica_share, aroma) | `PUT /catalog/varieties/{id}` | `catalog.emoji_invalid`, `catalog.indica_share_invalid`, `catalog.aroma_too_long` |

**How the page is split.** `VariedadDetalle.tsx` becomes a thin page over one component per section: `VarietyStockSection`, `VarietyPricesSection`, `VarietyThresholdSection` and `VarietyCatalogSection`.
- The two new forms, `ThresholdForm` and `CatalogAttributesForm`, are forms under the human's Decision 1. Each has "Cerrar", the existing discard guard, errors under each field mapped from the codes above, and the toast "Aviso guardado" or "Catálogo guardado".
- The attribute editing in `Catalogo.tsx` (its `setAttr*` state and the `catalog.*` code map) moves into `CatalogAttributesForm`; the catalog screen keeps none of it.

**What I'm stating outright:** the temporary place is not built. "Editar encabezado y pie" links to `/settings/catalog-message`, and Publicar's "unavailable" link stays `/settings/integrations`, as `PinPublish.tsx` already does.

**Q3**: Accepted in substance. The backend already returns all of it, so no endpoint change is needed.
- **The reasons** come from `GET /catalog`'s `varieties[].state`: `"in" | "hidden" | "no_price" | "sold_out" | "no_entries"`. The catalog domain computes it in priority order (`app/catalog/domain/variety.py`). The front maps each code to its Spanish word through one table and never compares prices, stock or `shown` itself.
- **The status marks** come from `GET /stock`'s `status`, and "Oculta" from `GET /catalog`'s `shown: false`.
- **The summary line** counts the statuses `GET /stock` answered and orders them by a fixed display order. That is a tally and a wording, not a rule; `stock_level` stays the only author of each status. The same counting is allowed in exactly one pure function, `summaryLine(statuses)`, with unit tests for no varieties, all ok and a mix. No other component counts.
- **The text** handed to the preview, Compartir and Copiar is `GET /catalog`'s `text`, unaltered (ADR-0022).

**Q4**: Accepted, with these specifics.

**Share seam.** A new `web/src/share.ts` is the only module allowed to name `navigator.share`. It gets its own rule in `forbiddenApis.ts`, allowed by exact path as `clipboard.ts` is, and `forbiddenApis.test.ts` gets a failing fixture first. It exports:
- `canShare(): boolean`, which checks `window.isSecureContext && typeof navigator.share === 'function'`;
- `shareText(text): Promise<'shared' | 'cancelled' | 'failed'>`, where a `DOMException` named `AbortError` is `cancelled` and anything else is `failed`.

It never logs. The error object is dropped, never shown.

**How it's tested.**
- `share.ts` gets unit tests with `navigator` stubbed for all four cases: present, absent, abort and other rejection.
- The catalog screen gets Testing Library tests with `share.ts` and `clipboard.ts` mocked at the module seam:
  - Compartir is hidden when `canShare()` is false, and Copiar is still there;
  - success shows nothing;
  - cancel shows nothing;
  - failure shows only "No se pudo compartir.", and Copiar stays;
  - Copiar's success shows "Catálogo copiado";
  - Copiar's failure shows "No se pudo copiar.";
  - the argument passed to Compartir and Copiar equals the faked API `text` byte for byte.
- `catalogInsecureOrigin.spec.ts` asserts that over http, Compartir is absent and Copiar works.

**Two panes.** The computer layout's auto-selection is one hook, `useSelectedVariety(varieties, isComputer)`, which picks the first by name only when nothing is selected. Front tests with the viewport faked cover:
- the first variety selected on a computer;
- no varieties: the right pane has no figure;
- on the phone, the list never opens a variety.

**Standards touched:**
- **§9:** no catalog text, name or amount reaches the console or storage. The e2e console collector and the storage helper cover the new screens.
- **§10:** `forbiddenApis` gets the new `navigator.share` rule; colors come only from tokens; the design comparisons run before and after.

**Tests that change.** `Stock.tsx` and `Stock.test.tsx` are deleted with the screen. The attribute-editing tests in `Catalogo.test.tsx` move to `CatalogAttributesForm`'s tests. The PRD allows both ("apart from tests of removed screens"). The e2e `goto`s of old paths change to the new paths in the diff that moves each route. No other test is edited to fit the new look.

**Q5**: Accepted, with a tighter order. The PRD's "nothing lost" rule forbids removing the catalog screen's attribute editing before the page's form exists.
1. **(a) Routing.** One subtask, alone, and the only one that touches `static_spa.py`, `router.tsx`, `routes.ts`, `contract.json` and the legacy tables. It contains:
   - ADR-0027 and the matcher;
   - the legacy 308s and their tests, and the contract-test change;
   - route constants in `routes.ts`;
   - the router remounting the existing screens under the new paths unchanged: `Variedades` on `/varieties`, `VariedadDetalle` on `/varieties/:id`, `Catalogo` on `/varieties/catalog`, `EntradaStock` on `/varieties/entries/new` and `/varieties/entries/:id`;
   - new routes for the not-yet-built screens (`/varieties/new`, `/varieties/:id/prices`, `/varieties/:id/prices/new`, `/varieties/entries`) pointing at stub modules that later subtasks fill in. Until then each stub is a placeholder that existing screens don't link to, so no link points at a missing screen;
   - in-app links updated to the new paths, and the tab bar renamed to Variedades with `/mi-stock` dropped from it.

   The app works throughout. The **only backend change in the task** is here.
2. **(c1) Read-only page sections**, in parallel with (d1). The four section components, the summary line, "Ver todas" lists with the card chrome, and the priced-entry form at `/varieties/:id/prices/new`. It owns `screens/varieties/page/*`.
3. **(d1) Catalog screen**, in parallel with (c1). `share.ts` and its forbidden-API rule, Compartir and Copiar, the reasons table, and the links out. It leaves the existing attribute editing in place. It owns `Catalogo.tsx`, `share.ts` and `forbiddenApis.ts`.
4. **(c2) The two forms**, after (c1). `ThresholdForm` and `CatalogAttributesForm`, each with its discard guard and per-field errors. This is the one place a seam is agreed test-first: the code → field mapping. In the same diff, `Catalogo.tsx` loses its attribute editing, since the replacement now exists.
5. **(b) The list and the removal of Stock**, after (c1). It contains:
   - the list rows, `summaryLine` and its tests, and `useSelectedVariety`;
   - the two panes, embedding (c1)'s page component through one prop, `varietyId`;
   - "+ Entrada de stock", "Todas las entradas" at `/varieties/entries`, and "Stock inicial" preselected from the variety's page;
   - deleting `Stock.tsx` and its test;
   - `screens.json` set to `route = path` for each shipped screen, in the subtask that ships it.

Seams of their own: `share.ts` in (d1), the routing matcher in (a), and `summaryLine` and `useSelectedVariety` in (b). Each subtask stays within about four production modules plus tests.

### Invariants: my position
All five are accepted, with two refinements:
- **The redirect invariant** reads "one entry in `LEGACY_FRONT_PATHS` or `LEGACY_FRONT_PATTERNS`, with its own 308 test, in the diff that moves the route". Server-side, not the client table.
- **Pattern safety:** a shared front pattern's only variable is `[0-9]+`, fully anchored. Any wider pattern is refused by the contract test.

### Round 1: PM answers

### Applying the human's decisions

Both decisions are settled, and I have written them into varieties.md and navigation.md.

1. **Aviso de stock bajo**
   - The section shows the current warning read-only: "Avisame abajo de 15 g", "Avisando: quedan 18,5 g" or "Aviso apagado".
   - Its on/off switch sits beside an "Editar" link. "Editar" opens a small form with "Avisar con menos de" in grams, "Guardá el aviso" and "Cerrar".
   - **Turning the warning on** opens that same form. When the warning is off, the variety has no threshold (stock.md), so there is never a saved amount to bring back. Closing the form without saving leaves the warning off.
   - **Turning the warning off** saves at once, with the toast "Aviso guardado".
2. **En el catálogo**
   - The section shows its values read-only: shown or hidden, emoji, % índica and aroma/sabor.
   - "Editar" opens one form holding all four fields, including the switch "Mostrar en el catálogo". The form ends in "Guardá el catálogo".
   - Errors show under each field and keep what was typed.
   - Leaving with unsaved text asks before discarding it.
   - A save shows "Catálogo guardado".

Both forms follow the form chrome in navigation.md: "Cerrar", no bar and no "+ Nueva venta". They need no address of their own. If you give them one, register it in `scripts/design/screens.json`.

### Answer to Tech Lead, round 1

1. **Editing a stock entry: `/varieties/entries/{id}` is accepted.**
   - `/mi-stock/entradas/{id}` redirects to it permanently, tested like the other old paths.
   - It is a form: "Cerrar" goes back by history. With no history, it goes to "Todas las entradas" (`/varieties/entries`).
   - An entry that is gone, or belongs to another business, shows "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades".
   - A record id in the path is fine. ADR-0004 forbids only a business in the URL.
2. **A variety's "Ver todas" of entries goes to `/varieties/{low}/entries`, not `/varieties/entries?variety={id}`.**
   - The reason is that the prices list already lives at `/varieties/{low}/prices`. A variety's two "Ver todas" lists should have addresses of the same shape, so both stay under the variety and both go back to it.
   - You may build it with the same component as "Todas las entradas", filtered to that variety.
   - Its chrome is a card's: "‹ <variety>", and back with no history goes to the variety's page.
   - Its entries can be edited and deleted there, through the same form at `/varieties/entries/{id}`. It is one form for one record, so no fact is edited in two places.
3. **Register both new addresses in `scripts/design/screens.json` in this change.** If they share their first segment with the API (`varieties`), they fall under the parameterised-path question I already raised (ADR-0024 or an amendment to it). The rule is the same: a reload opens the screen, never the API's answer.

### Round 2: questions

Round 2. Your plan is sound on the API surface, and I have no argument left with the `LEGACY_FRONT_*` design, the `share.ts` seam or the `state` code table. These are the places where it still breaks.

**A concrete failure.** PM moved a variety's "Ver todas" of entries to `/varieties/{low}/entries`. Your pattern list has `^/varieties/[0-9]+$`, `/prices`, `/prices/new` and `^/varieties/entries/[0-9]+$`, and no pattern for `/entries`. A reload of that page falls to the API's `GET /varieties/{variety_id}/…`, which answers with JSON or a 401. The contract test would pass anyway, because it derives from the tables. A table that is missing a row can't fail it.

---

❓ **Q1** - **A test that fails when a front route lacks its pattern**: Add `^/varieties/[0-9]+/entries$` to the patterns. What stops the next route you add to `router.tsx` from skipping the matcher the same way? The contract test derives from `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS` and `SHARED_FRONT_PATTERNS`, so it only checks that the matcher is safe. It never checks that the matcher is complete.
➡️ Recommendation: derive the check from the other side. A test lists the concrete front route paths under `varieties` from `routes.ts` (with `:id` replaced by `7`) and asserts each one gets the shell for GET with `Accept: text/html` through `FrontNavigation`. One extra case covers each near-miss: `/varieties/7/entries/x` and `/varieties/entries/7a` do not get the shell. A route can then only be added together with its pattern.

❓ **Q2** - **Subtask (a) is over the cap**: As you describe it, (a) holds ADR-0027, `static_spa.py` patterns and 308s, the contract-test rewrite, `routes.ts`, `router.tsx` with 4 stub modules, and every in-app link and e2e `goto` of old paths, including the tab bar in `Shell.tsx`. That is more than four production modules plus tests plus an ADR, and the first half is Python while the second is the front. It's also the one subtask everything waits on. What is the seam between its halves?
➡️ Recommendation: split it into (a1) server: ADR-0027, `static_spa.py`, `contract.json`, the contract test and the 308 tests. It can land alone and breaks nothing, because the old routes still exist. Then (a2) front: `routes.ts`, the remount, the stubs, links and `goto`s. The seam is the route table: (a2) states its paths, and (a1) must make every one of them reload. Because of the previous question, the completeness test from Q1 belongs in (a2) and runs against (a1).

❓ **Q3** - **Stale catalog after a stock or price write**: The catalog preview and Compartir read `GET /catalog`. A variety's state there depends on entries, prices, the threshold and `shown`. Before this change the writes lived on other screens and nothing was shared. Now all four sections of the page write, and the catalog screen is one tap away (the tap budget). What do the mutations invalidate in `api/keys.ts`? Take this case: a stock entry is saved so a variety goes from `sold_out` to `in`. The user opens the catalog and shares text that still says it is out. That is a stale preview sent to a customer.
➡️ Recommendation: a shared helper, for example `invalidateCatalogDependents(queryClient)`, that every mutation of entries, prices, threshold, `shown` and attributes calls from its `onSuccess`. The catalog screen also refetches on mount, and Compartir and Copiar use the data currently on screen, never a copy kept from an earlier visit. Test it test-first for each mutation, and name the helper's owner as (a2) or (c1), so (c2), (b) and (d1) don't each invent their own.

❓ **Q4** - **Where the selected variety lives on a computer**: With two panes and the first variety auto-selected, is the selection in the URL (`/varieties/7` showing both panes), in component state, or in storage? ADR-0026 makes the theme the only browser storage, so storage is out. If it's component state, a reload loses it. The back button then leaves the screen instead of un-selecting. A shared `/varieties/7` link on a computer shows the list with variety 7, and on a phone shows only its page, and both must come from the same route. And the `VariedadDetalle` page reads `useParams` today, while (b) embeds it through a `varietyId` prop. Two sources of truth for one id.
➡️ Recommendation: the URL is the selection. On a computer, `/varieties` redirects (`replace`) to the first variety and `/varieties/:id` renders both panes. The page component takes `varietyId` as a prop only, never `useParams`. The route element is the one that reads the param and passes it. Test it with a faked viewport at `/varieties/7`, a reload, and Back from a form. And state what a first-variety redirect does when the list is empty or still loading, so it does not bounce.

❓ **Q5** - **The warning switch and an entry that is gone**: (i) PM says closing the "turn on" form without saving leaves the warning off. If the switch holds its own optimistic state, it shows "on" while the form is open and then has to be undone. (ii) You send `PUT …/threshold` with `{"grams": null}` for off. Does the switch show the new state only after the answer, and what does it show when that PUT is refused or offline? (iii) PM says an entry that is gone *or belongs to another business* shows "Eso ya no está.". Which endpoint fetches one entry for `/varieties/entries/{id}`, and does it answer the same for both cases? §9 forbids saying which.
➡️ Recommendation: the switch is a pure function of the server value (`threshold != null`). Toggling on opens the form without changing anything. Toggling off sends the PUT, keeps the switch disabled while it runs, and reverts on refusal with the existing refusal-code message. For the entry, use the same 404 for both cases and map it to "Eso ya no está." in one place. If there's no single-entry endpoint and you'd filter `GET /stock`, say so, because then another business's id is simply "not in the list" and the case is covered.

### Invariants proposed

- Every route path the front declares under `varieties` has a matching entry in `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS` or `SHARED_FRONT_PATTERNS`, checked by a test that lists the declared routes and requests each one.
- Every mutation of a stock entry, a price, a threshold, `shown` or a catalog attribute invalidates the catalog query on success through one shared helper, never an inline `invalidateQueries` of its own.
- A page component of a variety receives its id only as a prop, and only the route element reads the URL parameter.
- The warning switch's state is derived from the saved threshold, and changes only after the server answers.

### Round 2: answers

### What changed since round 1
I applied PM's round-1 answers:
- Editing an entry lives at `/varieties/entries/{id}`, and the old `/mi-stock/entradas/{id}` redirects there with a 308.
- A variety's "Ver todas" of entries lives at `/varieties/{id}/entries`. It reuses the "Todas las entradas" component, filtered to that variety, with the card chrome "‹ <variety>". Back with no history goes to the variety's page.
- **Both addresses go into `scripts/design/screens.json`.** Neither has a design file of its own, so each entry names an existing file with `design`, as Reset does with Invitacion: `TodasLasEntradas` and `EntradaStock`. They are added in subtask (b).

The two section forms (Aviso, En el catálogo) get **no address of their own**. They open over the variety's page as forms, with "Cerrar" and the discard guard (`useBlocker` catches Back), so nothing new goes into `screens.json` for them.

I read `web/src/api/keys.ts`, `Catalogo.tsx`'s query options and `GET /stock/entries/{id}` before answering.

**Q1**: Accepted. I'm adding `^/varieties/[0-9]+/entries$`. Completeness is checked from the front's side and across both languages through `contract.json`, which is already the bridge between them (ADR-0008):
- **`contract.json` gains `sharedFrontRoutes`.** It is a list of concrete sample paths, ids written as `7`:
  - `/varieties`, `/varieties/new`, `/varieties/catalog`, `/varieties/entries`, `/varieties/entries/new`, `/varieties/entries/7`;
  - `/varieties/7`, `/varieties/7/prices`, `/varieties/7/prices/new`, `/varieties/7/entries`;
  - `/expenses`, `/expenses/new`.
- **Vitest, front side.** Every route path declared in `routes.ts` whose first segment is an API segment from `contract.json`, with `:id` replaced by `7`, must appear in `sharedFrontRoutes`. Each route constant in `routes.ts` is the only way `router.tsx` names a path under `varieties`; a literal string there is a review finding.
- **Pytest, server side.** Every path in `sharedFrontRoutes` gets the shell through `FrontNavigation` for GET and HEAD with `Accept: text/html`, and gets the API's answer with `Accept: application/json`. A fixed list of near-misses must not get the shell: `/varieties/7a`, `/varieties/7/entries/x`, `/varieties/entries/7a`, `/varieties/7/prices/new/x`.

So adding a front route without its pattern fails the vitest, or the pytest once the path is listed. The existing safety test stays: only `[0-9]+`, fully anchored.

**Q2**: Accepted. (a) splits into two subtasks.
- **(a1) Server.** It holds:
  - ADR-0027;
  - in `static_spa.py`, `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS` and `SHARED_FRONT_PATTERNS`, plus `LEGACY_FRONT_PATHS` and `LEGACY_FRONT_PATTERNS` with their 308 tests, one per old path;
  - in `contract.json`, `frontSegments` gaining `varieties`, plus the new `sharedFrontRoutes`;
  - the rewritten intersection rule in `tests/test_refusal_codes.py`, and the server half of the Q1 test.

  It lands alone and breaks nothing:
  - the Spanish front routes still exist in the router;
  - the 308s apply only to paths the front is about to leave, and they arrive before the client router sees them;
  - every in-app link still uses Spanish paths, but a reload on one is redirected to the new path, and the new path is already served once (a2) lands.

  To avoid any window where a 308 lands on a front route that isn't mounted yet, **(a1)'s legacy entries are merged together with (a2)**: (a1) ships the matcher and the shared paths, and (a2) adds the four `LEGACY_*` rows in its own diff. That is one table edit in Python, next to the route it serves.
- **(a2) Front.** It holds:
  - the constants in `routes.ts`, and remounting the existing screens on the new paths in `router.tsx`;
  - the stub modules for the screens not built yet;
  - the tab bar in `Shell.tsx`, the in-app links, and the e2e `goto`s;
  - the four legacy rows;
  - the front half of the Q1 test;
  - the catalog invalidation rule from Q3.
- **The seam is `contract.json`'s `sharedFrontRoutes`.** (a1) states it, and (a2)'s vitest pins `routes.ts` to it.

**Q3**: Accepted in substance, through the mechanism the code already has. I'm not adding a new helper beside it.
- **Invalidation.** `keys.ts` already has one author of invalidation, `invalidateResource(queryClient, resource)`, with its `CROSS_INVALIDATES` table. (a2) adds a `catalog` resource whose prefix covers `GET /catalog`. It also adds cross rules so that writes on `stock` (entries and threshold) and on `varieties` (prices, rename) also invalidate `catalog`. The attribute and `shown` save invalidates `catalog` directly. Every mutation calls `invalidateResource` and never an inline `invalidateQueries`. The two inline calls in `EntradaStock.tsx` are moved onto it when (b) touches that form.
- **Tests.** `keys.test.ts` checks each cross rule first. The mutation tests in (c2) and (b) each assert that the `catalog` key is stale after success.
- **The catalog screen can't show stale text on open.** Its query already uses `gcTime: 0`, so it is discarded on unmount and refetched on every open, and Copiar renders only after the current fetch resolved (ADR-0022). (d1) keeps both rules. Compartir and Copiar pass the `text` of the query currently on screen, never a value held in component state from an earlier render.
- **Where invalidation does matter** is the variety page's En el catálogo section and the "Oculta" mark, which read `GET /catalog` while staying mounted. They are covered by the cross rules.

**Q4**: Accepted. The URL is the selection.
- **The page component** (`VarietyPage`) takes `varietyId: number` as a prop only and never calls `useParams`. The route element `VarietyRoute` is the only reader of `:id`. It parses the param, and a non-integer goes to "Eso ya no está.".
- **On a computer, `/varieties/:id`** renders both panes. **On a phone**, it renders the page alone. One route, with the layout chosen by viewport.
- **On a computer, `/varieties`** redirects with `replace` to the first variety by name (from `GET /varieties`), but only once the list has resolved and is non-empty:
  - while the list is loading, it shows the list's loading state and does not redirect;
  - with an empty list, it stays on `/varieties`, with the empty state and an empty right pane;
  - on the phone, it never redirects.
- **Because of the `replace`**, Back from `/varieties/7` leaves Variedades instead of bouncing back to the auto-selection. Back from a form opened on the page returns to `/varieties/7`.
- **Front tests**, with the viewport faked:
  - a computer at `/varieties` lands on the first id;
  - a direct load of `/varieties/7` shows both panes with 7 selected;
  - Back from the entry form returns to `/varieties/7`;
  - a loading list does not redirect, and an empty one does not either;
  - the phone at `/varieties` shows the list only.
- **Storage:** none (ADR-0026).

**Q5**: Accepted.
1. **The switch is derived** from `threshold !== null`, as answered by `GET /stock`. Turning it on only opens `ThresholdForm` and changes nothing. Closing without saving leaves it off because nothing was written. Saving the form writes the threshold, and the switch follows the refetched value.
2. **Turning it off** sends `PUT /stock/varieties/{id}/threshold` with `{"grams": null}`.
   - While the PUT runs, the switch is disabled and still shows "on".
   - On success, the threshold resource is invalidated, the switch reads off, and the toast is "Aviso guardado".
   - On a refusal, it stays on and shows the refusal code's message.
   - Offline, it stays on and the app shows "Sin conexión".
   - With the variety gone (404), it shows "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día."
3. **The entry form** at `/varieties/entries/{id}` fetches through `GET /stock/entries/{id}`, which already exists.
   - Its repository is built for the session's business, so a missing id and another business's id are both 404 `stock.entry_not_found`, kind `gone`. Nothing tells them apart.
   - The form maps that code through the app's one existing gone dispatch to "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades". It has no mapping of its own.
   - The isolation sweep already covers this route. A front test fakes the 404 and checks the screen.

### Invariants
All four are accepted, with these refinements:
- **Route completeness:** stated through `contract.json`'s `sharedFrontRoutes` and checked from both sides, as in Q1.
- **Catalog invalidation:** through `invalidateResource` and its `CROSS_INVALIDATES` rules, never inline.
- **Variety id:** a prop only. `VarietyRoute` is the only reader of the param.
- **Warning switch:** derived from the saved threshold, and it changes only after the server answers.

### Round 3: questions

Round 3. Rounds 1 and 2 are settled and I won't reopen them. Three things in the new answers can still go wrong.

**A concrete failure.** You say `Catalogo.tsx`'s query uses `gcTime: 0`, and you also have the variety page's "En el catálogo" section and "Oculta" read `GET /catalog` while mounted, with cross-invalidation keeping them fresh. If both readers use the same key with different options, `gcTime` is query-level and the longest observer wins. The page's section keeps the entry cached for minutes. A catalog screen opened after it can then render cached `text` that was never refetched, which is exactly the stale-text case ADR-0022 forbids for Copiar.

---

❓ **Q1** - **One key, two readers of `GET /catalog` with different needs**: The catalog screen needs `text` fresh on every open (`gcTime: 0`, Copiar only after the fetch resolves). The page section needs one variety's row and tolerates a cache. If they share a key through separate inline `useQuery` calls, the page's observer overrides the screen's `gcTime`. If `staleTime` is above 0, the screen opens on cached text with no refetch.
➡️ Recommendation: one `catalogQueryOptions` factory in `api/` that is the only place that names the key and sets `gcTime: 0` and `staleTime: 0`, used by both readers. The page selects its row with `select`. Then the freshness rule is true for every reader by construction. Add a test that mounts the page section, unmounts it, opens the catalog screen, and asserts a second request went out.

❓ **Q2** - **Back with an open section form**: Aviso and En el catálogo open over the variety's page with no address. `useBlocker` only fires when the location changes. Browser Back (phone or computer) with `ThresholdForm` open therefore leaves the variety for the previous screen. It neither closes the form nor reaches the discard guard when the guard has nothing to discard, and with typed text it asks "¿Descartás…?" and then leaves the page. PM's rule for form chrome is "Cerrar", and Back from a form returns to the page.
➡️ Recommendation: opening a form pushes one history entry with `navigate('.', { state: { form: 'threshold' } })`, with no new path, no `screens.json` row and no storage. The form is shown when `location.state.form` matches, so Back closes it, "Cerrar" calls `navigate(-1)`, and `useBlocker` guards only unsaved text. A reload while a form is open lands on the page without the form, and that is the stated behaviour. Which of the two do you choose, and what does a deep reload show?

❓ **Q3** - **Subtasks (b) and (c1) are still over the cap**: (b) as you list it holds the list rows, `summaryLine`, `useSelectedVariety` with its redirect, the two panes, "+ Entrada de stock", "Todas las entradas", the per-variety entries list, the entry form remount with `invalidateResource` migrated off two inline calls, deleting `Stock.tsx` and two `screens.json` rows. That is more than four production modules plus tests, and the two halves share nothing but the route. (c1) holds four sections, "Ver todas" prices, the price form and rename.
➡️ Recommendation: split (b) into (b1) list, summary, selection, panes, and (b2) entries: `/varieties/entries`, `/varieties/:id/entries`, the entry form on the new paths, the `invalidateResource` migration, deleting `Stock.tsx` and the two registry rows. Split (c1) into (c1a) the four sections and (c1b) price list, price form and rename. (b2) and (c1b) then don't share a file with each other or with (b1). Name the file each pair would both touch, if any.

❓ **Q4** - **How many entries `GET /stock` sends and how "Ver todas" reads them**: you filter a variety's entries from `GET /stock` on the client and show the last 3. "Ver todas" and "Todas las entradas" then render all of them. Is every entry in that payload? A business with a few thousand entries makes the list screen, the page, every pane switch and every invalidation (now also triggered by a catalog-related write through `CROSS_INVALIDATES`) carry and re-parse all of it. What stops this from becoming the slow path?
➡️ Recommendation: state what `GET /stock` returns today. If it is every entry, say whether the new screens cap or page them (the PRD asks only for "last 3" and "Ver todas"). If it is a bounded set, "Ver todas" must call an endpoint that returns the rest, and that endpoint is a backend subtask for (b2). Either way the filter "entries of variety N" is written once, in one function, not repeated in the page and in the list.

❓ **Q5** - **(a1)/(a2) contradict each other on the legacy table, and the stubs are reachable**: Your answer to round 2 says (a1)'s legacy entries "are merged together with (a2)", then says (a1) ships only the matcher and (a2) adds the four `LEGACY_*` rows. ADR-0027 and the `LEGACY_FRONT_PATTERNS` mechanism (the one that carries an id across) are code, not rows. Which subtask owns the mechanism, and does it land with an empty table or a test table? Separately, the stubs for `/varieties/new`, `/varieties/:id/prices`, `/varieties/:id/prices/new` and `/varieties/entries` are reachable by URL and, now, by reload through the new matcher.
➡️ Recommendation: (a1) ships the mechanism for both tables, with tests that use a table injected by the test, and an empty production `LEGACY_FRONT_*` for the Spanish paths. (a2) adds each row in the same diff as the route that replaces it, which keeps the "tested one by one" rule on one diff. Stubs render a plain "Próximamente" with no data call, and no in-app link points at one until the subtask that fills it lands. Say which of those holds for you.

### Invariants proposed

- Every reader of `GET /catalog` gets its query options from the one `catalogQueryOptions` factory, and no component or hook names the catalog query key inline.
- A form opened over a variety's page, with no path of its own, is opened by pushing a history entry, so browser Back closes the form.
- A stub screen makes no data call and no in-app link points at it.

### Round 3: answers

### What I checked this round
- `app/stock/application/read_stock.py`: `GET /stock` answers **every** entry of the business, sorted newest first. It loads them all anyway to compute stock (`entries.for_varieties(None)`).
- `Catalogo.tsx`'s query options: `gcTime: 0`, `staleTime: Infinity`, and no refetch on focus or reconnect.

**Q1**: Accepted.
- **One factory.** `catalogQueryOptions()` lives in a new `web/src/api/catalog.ts`, owned by (a2). It is the only place that names the catalog key and sets the options:
  - `gcTime: 0` and `staleTime: 0`;
  - `refetchOnWindowFocus: false` and `refetchOnReconnect: false`, so the preview never changes under the seller's finger.
  The catalog screen and the page section both read through it. The page takes one variety's row with `select`. With `staleTime: 0`, every mount refetches, whatever another observer did, so freshness holds for every reader by construction.
- **Copiar and Compartir** are enabled only once this mount's own fetch has resolved: `isFetchedAfterMount && !isFetching`. They pass the `text` of that answer, as ADR-0022 requires.
- **Test (d1)**: mount the page section, unmount it, open the catalog screen, and assert that a second `GET /catalog` went out and that Copiar is disabled until it resolves.
- **Lint rule (a2)**: an `architecture` rule allows the string `'catalog'` as a query key only in `api/keys.ts` and `api/catalog.ts`.

**Q2**: History state. A form opened over the page pushes one entry, `navigate('.', { state: { form: 'threshold' | 'catalog' } })`. It has no new path, no `screens.json` row and no storage.
- The form shows exactly when `location.state?.form` matches.
- **Browser Back** closes the form and returns to the page.
- **"Cerrar"**, and a successful save, call `navigate(-1)`.
- **`useBlocker`** guards only typed, unsaved text, so Back with nothing typed closes at once.
- **A deep reload while a form is open.** Browsers keep `history.state` across a reload, so the form opens again, filled from the server's saved values. Nothing typed survives, since nothing is stored. I'm stating this rather than suppressing it: the URL is the page, and the entry says a form is open over it.
- **Opened from a fresh tab, or a shared link**: there is no state, so only the page shows.
- **Front tests**:
  - open the form, Back, and the page shows with no form;
  - type, Back, the guard asks, choose "Sí", and the page shows;
  - "Cerrar" closes the form;
  - a reload with state shows the form with the saved values.

**Q3**: Accepted, with both splits.
- **(b1)** The list rows, `summaryLine`, `useSelectedVariety` with its redirect, and the two panes.
- **(b2)** The entries screens and the cleanup:
  - `/varieties/entries` and `/varieties/:id/entries` (one component with an optional variety filter);
  - the entry form on `/varieties/entries/new` and `/varieties/entries/:id`;
  - "+ Entrada de stock" with "Stock inicial" preselected;
  - `EntradaStock.tsx`'s two inline invalidations moved onto `invalidateResource`;
  - deleting `Stock.tsx` and its test;
  - the two `screens.json` rows.
- **(c1a)** The four read-only sections and the summary line inside `VarietyPage`.
- **(c1b)** The price list at `/varieties/:id/prices`, the price form at `/varieties/:id/prices/new`, and rename.

**Which files each pair touches**:
- (b2) and (c1b) touch no common file.
- (b1) imports (c1a)'s `VarietyPage` and never edits it. That is the one dependency, and (b1) lands after (c1a).
- Every cross-link (a section's "Ver todas", the list's "Todas las entradas") goes through route constants that (a2) already ships in `routes.ts`, so no subtask edits `routes.ts`, `router.tsx` or `keys.ts` after (a2).
- (a2) also ships the shared helpers (b2) and (c1a) both use: `entriesOf` (Q4) and `catalogQueryOptions` (Q1).

**Order**:
1. a1;
2. a2;
3. c1a ∥ d1;
4. c1b ∥ c2 ∥ b1 ∥ b2. In this wave, c2 removes the attribute editing from the catalog screen.

**Q4**: `GET /stock` sends every entry, and it stays that way. There is no paging and no new endpoint.
- **Cost shape (§8).** The server already pays O(entries + sale lines) on every `GET /stock` to compute stock and status (ADR-0010), so the payload grows in the same shape the work does. Shipping the entries adds parsing, not a new class of cost.
- **Scale.** The PRD's scale is a few dozen varieties, and a seller records a few entries a week, so hundreds of entries a year. The speed metric (under 300 ms) is measured on a seeded year, as ADR-0010 and ADR-0020 do. Missing it is the trigger to page "Ver todas" through a backend subtask, not before.
- **Invalidation.** It isn't widened the way the question assumes. The cross rules run from `stock` and `varieties` to `catalog`. A catalog attribute write invalidates only `catalog` and never refetches `/stock`.
- **One filter.** "Entries of variety N" is written once: `entriesOf(stock, varietyId)` in `api/stock.ts`, owned by (a2) and unit-tested there, with the order kept as the server sent it. The page's last 3 is `entriesOf(...).slice(0, 3)`. Both "Ver todas" and "Todas las entradas" use the same function, or no filter.

**Q5**: Accepted, with one change: no stubs at all.
- **(a1)** owns the mechanism for both legacy tables, `LEGACY_FRONT_PATHS` and `LEGACY_FRONT_PATTERNS`, matching and id carrying included. It also owns ADR-0027, the shared matcher and `sharedFrontRoutes`. Its tests run on tables they inject themselves. The production Spanish rows it adds are none. `/mas` and `/gastos` stay as they are.
- **(a2)** adds each Spanish row (`/variedades`, `/variedades/{n}`, `/mi-stock`, `/mi-stock/nueva-entrada`, `/mi-stock/entradas/{n}`, `/catalogo`), each in the same diff as the route that replaces it and with its own 308 test.
- **No stubs.** A "Próximamente" screen would break the navigation PRD's "nothing invented, no coming soon". (a2) mounts only routes whose screen already exists (`/varieties`, `/varieties/:id`, `/varieties/catalog`, `/varieties/entries/new`, `/varieties/entries/:id`). Each later subtask mounts its own route in its own diff, alongside its screen. `router.tsx` then has one more editor per wave; each of those diffs adds a line and they don't overlap.
- **Unmounted paths in the meantime.** `sharedFrontRoutes` may list paths not yet mounted: the vitest only checks that every mounted route is listed, not the reverse. Reloading such a path gets the shell, and the router's `*` sends it to Inicio, which is the existing rule for an unknown address.

### Invariants
Accepted:
- Only `catalogQueryOptions` names the catalog query.
- A form with no path opens by pushing a history entry, so Back closes it.

Replaced: "a stub screen makes no data call" becomes "no stub screens; a route is mounted only in the diff that ships its screen".

### Closing

### Closing summary (round 4 of 4)

Your round-3 answers close the open points. The one failure left is a risk to name, not a question to ask.

**Concrete failure that remains.** Between (a2) and (b1)/(b2), `/mi-stock` already answers with a 308 to `/varieties`, but the list there does not yet carry the stock summary, status marks or "+ Entrada de stock". If the waves are released one by one, the seller loses the Stock screen's function for the length of that window.

**What was decided**
- **Routing (ADR-0027, amending ADR-0024).** `{low}` is the integer id. The matcher is a closed set of anchored `[0-9]+` patterns, served only to GET/HEAD with `Accept: text/html`. It includes `^/varieties/[0-9]+/entries$` and `/varieties/entries/{id}`.
- **Route completeness.** `contract.json` gains `sharedFrontRoutes`. The front test pins `routes.ts` to it, and the server test requests each path.
- **Legacy paths.** Server-side 308s. (a1) owns the mechanism and tests it on injected tables. (a2) adds each Spanish row in the diff of the route that replaces it.
- **Backend.** No use case, schema or endpoint changes. The only backend change is the routing layer in `static_spa.py`.
- **Existing endpoints.** The two section forms use `PUT /stock/varieties/{id}/threshold` and `PUT /catalog/varieties/{id}`, with refusal codes already in `contract.json`.
- **Configuración exists.** The temporary place is not built. The links go to `/settings/integrations` and `/settings/catalog-message`.
- **Derived values.** The "not shown, and why" reasons come from `GET /catalog`'s `state`. Status marks come from `GET /stock`. The front only maps and counts (`summaryLine`).
- **Catalog reads.** One `catalogQueryOptions` factory with `staleTime: 0` and `gcTime: 0`, and Compartir and Copiar enabled only after this mount's own fetch. Invalidation goes through `invalidateResource` and its `CROSS_INVALIDATES` rules.
- **Share seam.** `share.ts` is the only module that names `navigator.share`. It has its own `forbiddenApis` rule, four outcomes under test, and no logging.
- **Selection and forms.** The URL is the selection. `VarietyPage` takes `varietyId` as a prop and only the route element reads the parameter. Section forms open by pushing a history entry, so Back closes them. The warning switch derives from the saved threshold.
- **Entries.** `GET /stock` stays unpaged, and `entriesOf` is the single filter. A missing or foreign entry gives the same 404, mapped once to "Eso ya no está.".
- **No stubs.** A route is mounted only in the diff that ships its screen.
- **Subtasks and order.** a1, then a2, then (c1a ∥ d1), then (c1b ∥ c2 ∥ b1 ∥ b2). The shared helpers `catalogQueryOptions` and `entriesOf` ship in (a2), so no later subtask edits `routes.ts`, `keys.ts` or `api/stock.ts`.

**Known risks**
- **Release window.** Merge (a2) through (b2) as one release, or accept that Stock's function is missing in between. Decide this before the wave order is fixed.
- **Unpaged `GET /stock`.** If it misses the 300 ms measurement on a seeded year, "Ver todas" has to move to a paged backend endpoint. That is a later subtask.
- **Reload with an open form.** `history.state` survives a reload, so the form reopens with the saved values and not what was typed. The behaviour is deliberate. Check it against what PM expects in review.
- **`router.tsx` editors.** It has one more editor per wave (each adds one line), so merge conflicts there are likely and cheap to resolve.
- **ADR-0027.** (a1)'s developer writes it. If it's missing or only restates ADR-0024, review (a1) against the matcher and `sharedFrontRoutes`.

### Invariants proposed

- A route is added to `router.tsx` only in the diff that ships its screen, and no screen is a placeholder or "Próximamente".

## Tech Lead

### Shape of the solution

This is a front-structure task. **No backend use case, schema, endpoint or refusal code changes.** The only backend change is to the routing layer in `app/static_spa.py`, so that the new `/varieties/*` front paths reload as the app and never as the API's JSON. That is recorded in a new **ADR-0027**, which amends ADR-0024.

**Configuración already exists** (`/settings/integrations` and `/settings/catalog-message` are routed). Following the PRD's own condition, **the temporary place is not built**. The catalog screen links straight to those two groups, and the temporary-place metrics run against them instead.

### Decisions

**Routing (ADR-0027, amending ADR-0024)**
- **`{low}` is the variety's integer id**, not a slug. A rename never breaks a link, and because the matcher accepts only digits, no variety name collides with `catalog`, `entries` or `new`.
- **Exact shared paths.** `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS` stays exact and gains `/varieties`, `/varieties/new`, `/varieties/catalog`, `/varieties/entries` and `/varieties/entries/new`.
- **Parameterised shared paths.** A new `SHARED_FRONT_PATTERNS` is a closed tuple of fully anchored patterns whose only variable is `[0-9]+`:
  - `^/varieties/[0-9]+$`
  - `^/varieties/[0-9]+/prices$`
  - `^/varieties/[0-9]+/prices/new$`
  - `^/varieties/[0-9]+/entries$`
  - `^/varieties/entries/[0-9]+$`
- **ADR-0024's gate is unchanged.** Only GET or HEAD with `Accept: text/html` gets the shell. A shared path plus one trailing slash is a 308 to the slashless path. Everything else reaches the API.
- **Legacy redirects are server-side 308s**, through `FrontNavigation`. `LEGACY_FRONT_PATHS` holds exact paths. A new `LEGACY_FRONT_PATTERNS` carries the id across: `/variedades/{n}` → `/varieties/{n}` and `/mi-stock/entradas/{n}` → `/varieties/entries/{n}`.
  - The client `redirects.ts` gains nothing. Its existing entries coexist as they are.
- **Completeness, checked from both sides.** `contract.json` gains `sharedFrontRoutes`, a list of concrete sample paths with ids written as `7`.
  - A vitest checks that every route constant in `routes.ts` whose first segment is an API segment appears in that list, with its ids written as `7`.
  - A pytest requests each listed path. It must get the shell for HTML navigations and the API for `Accept: application/json`. Near-misses must never get the shell.
- **Contract rule.** `frontSegments` gains `varieties`. The intersection rule in `tests/test_refusal_codes.py` becomes: the API's first segments ∩ `frontSegments` ⊆ the first segments of `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS` ∪ `SHARED_FRONT_PATTERNS`, derived from the tables. A pattern wider than anchored `[0-9]+` is refused by a test.
- **§8, what this widens.** A browser navigation to those exact paths and patterns now gets the static shell. The shell holds no data and goes to anyone, signed in or not, as every front path already does. Its cost does not grow with data.

**Data the screens read** (all existing endpoints, codes already in `contract.json`):

| What | Read | Write |
|---|---|---|
| Summary line, status marks, Stock y entradas | `GET /stock` (each variety's row; every entry, newest first) | the entry form: `POST`, `PUT` and `DELETE /stock/entries…` |
| Today's list price | `GET /varieties`, `GET /varieties/{id}` | — |
| Precios | `GET /varieties/{id}/prices` | `POST /varieties/{id}/prices`, `DELETE …/prices/{entry_id}` |
| Rename, Nueva variedad | — | `PATCH /varieties/{id}`, `POST /varieties` |
| Aviso de stock bajo | the threshold from `GET /stock` | `PUT /stock/varieties/{id}/threshold` with `{grams}`; turning off sends `{"grams": null}` |
| En el catálogo, "Oculta", reasons, text | `GET /catalog` (`text`; per variety `shown`, emoji, indica share, aroma, `state`) | `PUT /catalog/varieties/{id}` |

- **No second author of a rule.** The front never decides a status, a reason or the text.
  - The reasons map `state` (`hidden`, `no_price`, `sold_out`, `no_entries`) to words through one table.
  - The summary line is one pure function, `summaryLine(statuses)`, that counts the statuses as answered and orders them by stock-status order.
  - "Entries of variety N" is written once, as `entriesOf(stock, varietyId)` in `api/stock.ts`, keeping the server's order.
- **`GET /stock` stays unpaged** (§8). It already loads every entry to compute stock: O(entries + sale lines) per read, ADR-0010. If the 300 ms measurement on a seeded year is missed, "Ver todas" moves to a paged endpoint in a later task.

**Catalog freshness (ADR-0022)**
- **One query factory.** `catalogQueryOptions()` in `web/src/api/catalog.ts` is the only place that names the catalog key. It sets `staleTime: 0` and `gcTime: 0`, and no refetch on focus or reconnect. Every reader uses it; the variety page takes its row with `select`.
- **Compartir and Copiar** are enabled only once this mount's own fetch has resolved. They pass that answer's `text`, unaltered.
- **Invalidation goes through the existing `invalidateResource`.** It gains a `catalog` resource, and `CROSS_INVALIDATES` rules so that writes on `stock` and `varieties` also invalidate `catalog`.

**Compartir**
- **`web/src/share.ts`** is the only module that names `navigator.share`, with its own `forbiddenApis` rule allowed by exact path. It exports:
  - `canShare()`, which checks for a secure context and a share function;
  - `shareText(text) → 'shared' | 'cancelled' | 'failed'`, where `AbortError` is `cancelled`.
- **What the seller sees.** It logs nothing, and the error object is dropped. Shared and cancelled show nothing. Failed shows only "No se pudo compartir.". Copiar stays on `clipboard.ts` as it is.

**Selection, panes and forms**
- **The URL is the selection.** `VarietyPage` takes `varietyId` as a prop only. The route element `VarietyRoute` is the only reader of `:id`, and a non-integer id is "Eso ya no está.".
- **On a computer:**
  - `VarietiesLayout` (as `ClientesLayout` does) shows the list beside an outlet;
  - `/varieties` redirects with `replace` to the first variety by name, but only once the list has resolved and is non-empty. While loading there is no redirect; with no varieties the right pane shows no figure.
- **On a phone:** the list alone, and it never opens a variety.
- **Section forms** (Aviso, En el catálogo) have no address of their own. Opening one pushes a history entry, `navigate('.', {state: {form}})`:
  - the form shows when that state matches, and Back closes it;
  - "Cerrar" and a successful save call `navigate(-1)`;
  - `useBlocker` guards only typed, unsaved text;
  - a reload with that state open reopens the form with the saved values; a fresh tab shows the page only.
- **The warning switch** is derived from the saved threshold.
  - Turning it on only opens the form.
  - Turning it off sends the PUT. The switch stays disabled and "on" while it runs, and changes only after the server answers. A refusal, "Sin conexión" or a gone variety leaves it on.
- **The entry form** at `/varieties/entries/{id}` reads `GET /stock/entries/{id}`. A missing entry and another business's entry are the same 404 `stock.entry_not_found`, mapped by the app's one existing gone dispatch to "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades".
- **Preselection.** "+ Entrada" from a variety's page opens `/varieties/entries/new?variety={id}`. The form chooses that variety, and preselects "Stock inicial" only when `entriesOf` returns none for it.

**No stubs.** A route is mounted only in the diff that ships its screen. No placeholder and no "Próximamente".

### Order and why
1. **routing-server.**
2. **routing-front.**
3. **catalog-screen, entries-screens and prices-and-rename**, in parallel. Each is a standalone screen.
4. **variety-page.** It links to "Ver todas" of entries and of prices, so it waits for both.
5. **section-forms and list-and-panes**, in parallel.

**Nothing is lost between waves.**
- Stock (`/mi-stock`) keeps working, and its tab stays, until **list-and-panes** gives the list its status, summary and "+ Entrada de stock". That subtask then deletes Stock and adds the `/mi-stock` 308.
- The catalog screen keeps its attribute editing until **section-forms** ships the replacement and removes it.

The whole task merges as one PR, so no intermediate state reaches `master`.

### Where new code coexists with old shapes
- The client `redirects.ts` (`/negocio`, `/gastos/categorias`) stays beside the new server 308s.
- `/mas` and `/gastos` stay in `LEGACY_FRONT_PATHS` as they are.
- Spanish `frontSegments` stay listed while their paths redirect.
- Untouched screens keep Spanish file names (`Variedades.tsx`, `EntradaStock.tsx`). New modules use English names from `CONTEXT.md`.

### Known risks
- **`router.tsx` and `screens.json`** get one small edit per subtask, so merge conflicts there are likely and cheap.
- **A reload while a section form is open** reopens it with the saved values, deliberately. Review this against what PM expects.
- **ADR-0027** must state the matcher, the patterns and `sharedFrontRoutes`. Review routing-server against it.

### Subtasks

`### Subtasks` written by the system from the structured plan: no exact `### Subtasks` line (outside code fences) was found in this section, so the block below was built from the structured plan. Any list the Tech Lead wrote under another heading is not the one the Developers read.

#### ADR-0027: serve /varieties/* front paths and carry legacy ids across, server side

Backend routing layer only; no route, use case or schema changes.

Build:
- `docs/adr/0027-…md`, amending ADR-0024.
