It covers: `{low}` is the integer id; exact `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS` plus a closed `SHARED_FRONT_PATTERNS` (anchored, only `[0-9]+`); `LEGACY_FRONT_PATTERNS` that carry an id across; `contract.json`'s `sharedFrontRoutes`; the rewritten intersection rule; and what this widens (§8).
- `app/static_spa.py`:
  - `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS` gains `/varieties`, `/varieties/new`, `/varieties/catalog`, `/varieties/entries`, `/varieties/entries/new`.
  - New `SHARED_FRONT_PATTERNS` (compiled, anchored): `^/varieties/[0-9]+$`, `^/varieties/[0-9]+/prices$`, `^/varieties/[0-9]+/prices/new$`, `^/varieties/[0-9]+/entries$`, `^/varieties/entries/[0-9]+$`.
  - The same `Accept: text/html` GET/HEAD gate and one-trailing-slash 308 apply to the patterns.
  - New `LEGACY_FRONT_PATTERNS` mechanism: a match is a 308 to its target with the captured id substituted, for any method or `Accept`, keeping the query string.
  - The production Spanish rows for varieties stay empty here; routing-front adds them.
- `web/src/api/contract.json`: `frontSegments` gains `varieties`. New `sharedFrontRoutes`: `/varieties`, `/varieties/new`, `/varieties/catalog`, `/varieties/entries`, `/varieties/entries/new`, `/varieties/entries/7`, `/varieties/7`, `/varieties/7/prices`, `/varieties/7/prices/new`, `/varieties/7/entries`, `/expenses`, `/expenses/new`.
- `tests/test_refusal_codes.py`: the API's first segments ∩ `frontSegments` ⊆ the first segments derived from `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS` ∪ `SHARED_FRONT_PATTERNS`. A test refuses any pattern that is not fully anchored or has a variable other than `[0-9]+`.

Acceptance (pytest through `FrontNavigation`/`TestClient`):
- Every `sharedFrontRoutes` path gets `index.html` for GET and HEAD with `Accept: text/html`, and gets the API's own answer with `Accept: application/json` (401 signed out).
- `/varieties/7/prices` with JSON `Accept` reaches the API.
- Near-misses never get the shell: `/varieties/7a`, `/varieties/7/entries/x`, `/varieties/entries/7a`, `/varieties/7/prices/new/x`.
- `LEGACY_FRONT_PATTERNS` is tested on an injected table: 308, id carried, query kept, any method.
- The OpenAPI document, the gated-route test and the isolation sweep are unchanged.

Seams: `FrontNavigation ASGI middleware over HTTP (TestClient)`, `contract.json ↔ static_spa tables (tests/test_refusal_codes.py)`.

Depends on: nothing.

#### Move existing variety, catalog and entry screens to English routes; shared catalog/stock helpers

Front routing and shared helpers, plus the legacy rows for the paths it retires.

Routes:
- `web/src/routes.ts` gains constants for every `/varieties/*` path: `VARIETIES`, `VARIETY(id)`, `VARIETY_PRICES(id)`, `VARIETY_PRICE_NEW(id)`, `VARIETY_ENTRIES(id)`, `VARIETY_NEW`, `CATALOG`, `ENTRIES`, `ENTRY_NEW`, `ENTRY(id)`.
  - `router.tsx` names `/varieties` paths only through these.
- `router.tsx` remounts **only existing screens**, unchanged:
  - `Variedades` on `/varieties`, `VariedadDetalle` on `/varieties/:id`, `Catalogo` on `/varieties/catalog`;
  - `EntradaStock` on `/varieties/entries/new` (mode nuevo) and `/varieties/entries/:id` (mode editar).
  - It removes the Spanish client routes `variedades`, `variedades/:id`, `catalogo`, `mi-stock/nueva-entrada` and `mi-stock/entradas/:id`.
  - `mi-stock` (Stock.tsx) and its tab stay until list-and-panes. No stubs: no route for a screen not yet built.
- Every in-app link to the moved paths is updated (Variedades, VariedadDetalle, Stock.tsx, Inicio, PinPublish, the shell's Variedades tab), along with the e2e `goto`s.
- `app/static_spa.py` legacy rows, each with its own 308 test: `/variedades` → `/varieties`, `/variedades/{n}` → `/varieties/{n}`, `/catalogo` → `/varieties/catalog`, `/mi-stock/nueva-entrada` → `/varieties/entries/new`, `/mi-stock/entradas/{n}` → `/varieties/entries/{n}`.
- `scripts/design/screens.json`: `route` = `path` for the moved screens (Variedades, VariedadDetalle, Catalogo, EntradaStock).

Shared helpers (no later subtask edits these files):
- `web/src/api/catalog.ts`: `catalogQueryOptions()` is the only place that names the catalog key, with `staleTime: 0`, `gcTime: 0` and no refetch on focus or reconnect. `Catalogo.tsx` switches to it.
- `web/src/api/keys.ts`: a `catalog` resource, and `CROSS_INVALIDATES` so that `stock` and `varieties` writes also invalidate `catalog`. Test-first in `keys.test.ts`.
- `web/src/api/stock.ts`: `entriesOf(stock, varietyId)`, keeping the server's order, with unit tests.
- An `architecture` rule: the catalog query key appears only in `api/keys.ts` and `api/catalog.ts`.
- A vitest: every route constant whose first segment is an API segment from `contract.json`, with ids written as `7`, appears in `contract.json`'s `sharedFrontRoutes`.

Acceptance:
- The app works end to end on the new paths, and reloading each mounted path opens the screen.
- The existing suite passes, with only `goto`s and links of moved paths edited.

Seams: `routes.ts ↔ contract.json sharedFrontRoutes (vitest)`, `invalidateResource / CROSS_INVALIDATES (keys.test.ts)`, `entriesOf (unit)`, `FrontNavigation legacy 308s over HTTP (pytest)`.

  - `PUT /catalog/varieties/{id}`;

## Developer: routing-server

### Checks

Run by the system on `ebef51f` when the Developer delivered.

| Check | Command | Result |
|---|---|---|
| `tests` | `uv run --no-project pytest` | passed |
| `lint` | `uv run --no-project ruff check .` | passed |
| `types` | `uv run --no-project mypy app tests` | passed |
| `web-types` | `npm --prefix web run typecheck` | passed |
| `web-lint` | `npm --prefix web run lint` | passed |
| `web-tests` | `npm --prefix web test` | passed |
| `web-e2e` | `npm --prefix web run e2e` | passed |

### Changes
- `docs/adr/0027-…md`: amends ADR-0024. It records:
  - `{low}` is the integer id.
  - Exact `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS` plus a closed `SHARED_FRONT_PATTERNS`.
  - `LEGACY_FRONT_PATTERNS` carries an id across a redirect.
  - `sharedFrontRoutes` in `contract.json`.
  - The rewritten intersection rule.
  - What this widens, under section 8.
- `app/static_spa.py`:
  - `SHARED_FRONT_PATHS` gains five exact `/varieties…` paths.
  - `SHARED_FRONT_PATTERNS` holds five anchored `[0-9]+` patterns, matched with `fullmatch`.
  - `LEGACY_FRONT_PATTERNS` is new and empty. `FrontNavigation` takes the table as an argument, so a test can inject its own.
  - The `Accept: text/html` GET/HEAD gate and the one-trailing-slash 308 apply to the patterns too.
  - A legacy hit is a 308 for any method and any `Accept`, with the id carried across and the query string kept.
- `web/src/api/contract.json`: `frontSegments` gains `varieties`, and the new `sharedFrontRoutes` lists the twelve sample paths.

### Tests
- `tests/test_serving.py`, through `FrontNavigation` over HTTP with `TestClient`:
  - Every `sharedFrontRoutes` path is the shell for GET and HEAD with HTML `Accept`.
  - With JSON `Accept`, the same paths reach the API, and `/varieties/7/prices` answers 401 signed out.
  - Writes to a parameterised shared path are never the shell.
  - Near-misses reach the API: `7a`, `-7`, a trailing newline, a double slash and a non-ASCII digit.
  - A shared path plus one trailing slash is a 308 to the slashless path. This holds for HEAD too, and JSON reads and writes are never redirected (ebef51f).
  - `LEGACY_FRONT_PATTERNS` is tested on an injected table: 308, id carried, query kept, five methods, both `Accept` values, with and without a slash. Non-matches reach the API.
- `tests/test_refusal_codes.py`, at the contract ↔ tables seam:
  - The API's first segments ∩ `frontSegments` must sit inside the first segments derived from both tables.
  - `pattern_problems` is tested on inputs it must reject: unanchored at the start or end, `.*`, `[0-9]*`, `\d+`, `[^/]+`, an alternation and an escaped `$`.
  - Every production pattern passes `pattern_problems`.

### Decisions and assumptions
- Same as the assumptions field: the `routes.ts` vitest is left to routing-front, and the production legacy table stays empty here.

### For the Tech Lead
- Start with `FrontNavigation.__call__` and `_legacy_target` in `app/static_spa.py`, then ADR-0027 against the code.
- `pattern_problems` allows only lowercase literal segments and `[0-9]+`. A future pattern with another variable fails that test on purpose.

### Changes
- `web/src/screens/Catalogo.tsx`: redrawn.
  - "‹ Variedades" link back to the list and a "Catálogo" title.
  - Preview built only from `telegramPreview`.
  - Compartir, shown only when `canShare()`, sits beside Copiar. Both are disabled until `isFetchedAfterMount && !isFetching` and both receive the query's `text` unaltered.

  - "Qué no sale y por qué" lists every variety whose `state` is not `in`, with one word from the single `REASON_LABEL` table. Each name links to `VARIETY(id)` with the `CATALOG_SECTION_ID` hash.
  - `PinPublish` is unchanged.
  - "Editar encabezado y pie" links to `/settings/catalog-message`.
  - The attribute editing stays for now, retitled "Atributos en el catálogo".
- `web/src/routes.ts`: new `CATALOG_SECTION_ID`, the anchor shared with variety-page.
- `web/src/routes.test.ts`: `sampleRoutes()` now keeps only values starting with '/'.
- `web/src/styles/app.css`: Compartir/Copiar row (1.4 : 1), the reasons list, link styling for variety names.
- `web/src/screens/settings/UsersGroup.tsx`: the invitation share goes through `share.ts`.
- `web/e2e/catalogInsecureOrigin.spec.ts`: over http, Compartir is absent and Copiar works.
- `web/e2e/catalog.spec.ts`: dropped the check of the removed Encabezado/Pie summary.

- `share.test.ts`: share present, absent, abort, other rejection.
- `forbiddenApis.test.ts`: the failing fixture for the `navigatorShare` rule.

## Tech Lead: review catalog-screen (v1)

### Verdict

**returned** (`spec-behaviour`): Fix first: the catalog route still shows "+ Nueva venta" on a detail. After that, build the CatalogoCompu two-column layout and replace the raw 10px gap with a token.

### Findings

| severity | category | location | claim | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| blocking | spec-behaviour | web/src/router.tsx:139 | The catalog route has no `handle.chrome`, so the Shell draws "+ Nueva venta" on this detail. No test covers it. | Brief: "A detail: '‹ Variedades', no '+ Nueva venta'". router.tsx:139 has no handle. Shell.tsx:165 `showNuevaVenta = chrome === undefined`. Grep for `Nueva venta\|chrome` in Catalogo.test.tsx: no match |
| blocking | spec-scope | web/src/styles/app.css:2377 | CatalogoCompu's two-column computer layout is not built; the screen is one column at every width. | Brief: "Redraw … from `pantallas/Catalogo` and `CatalogoCompu`". §10: "A screen looks like its design". Grep finds no computer-width catalog rule. The Developer confirms they did not build it |
| blocking | plan-invariant | web/src/styles/app.css:2379 | `gap: 10px` is a raw spacing value, not a token (invariant 16). | Diff: `.catalogo__acciones { … gap: 10px; }`. tokens.css has only --espacio-xs…xxl. §10: "spacing only from the design tokens" |
| observation | process-suite-red | Developer: catalog-screen, ### Checks | The suite was red on the head, but I could not trace it to this diff. | `npm --prefix web run e2e -- e2e/settings.spec.ts …` fails at :37, at sign-up, with "Creá el negocio" still disabled. `npm --prefix web test src/screens/settings` → 80 passed. Seam tests → 73 passed |
| observation | spec-scope | web/src/screens/settings/UsersGroup.tsx:78 | The invite share now sends the link as `text`, not `url`. Its button still shows where `canShare()` is false, and a tap does nothing. | — |
| observation | standard-other | web/src/architecture/forbiddenApis.ts:60 | The pattern misses a destructured `const { share } = navigator`. The clipboard rule has the same gap. | — |

Everything else in the brief checks out:
- **share.ts:** `canShare` requires a secure context; `AbortError` returns `cancelled`; it never logs.
- **forbiddenApis rule:** it has rejecting fixtures, and the seam tests pass.
- **Compartir and Copiar:** both are gated on `isFetchedAfterMount && !isFetching` and get the query's `text` unaltered (invariant 6).
- **Reasons:** one `REASON_LABEL` table maps the server's `state` (invariant 10).
- **Console and storage:** a test checks that nothing reaches them (invariant 15).
- **Links:** to `VARIETY(id)#en-el-catalogo` and to `/settings/catalog-message`.

### Changes
- `web/src/screens/EntriesList.tsx` (new): one list component with an optional `varietyId`. It reads `GET /stock` and filters only through `entriesOf`. Each row shows date, variety, origin, grams, total cost and cost per gram. Editar links to `ENTRY(id)`. Borrar asks "Antes de borrar" (date, variety, grams) and then flashes "Entrada eliminada". It has the empty state. The card's "‹ Variedades" or "‹ <variety>" goes back by history, else to `VARIETIES` / `VARIETY(id)`.
- `web/src/screens/VarietyEntriesRoute.tsx` (new): reads `:id` for `/varieties/:id/entries`. A non-integer id shows the gone screen.
- `web/src/components/useHistoryBack.ts` (new): back by history, else a fallback with `replace`.
- `web/src/screens/EntradaStock.tsx`:
  - Moved to the new paths.
  - Header with "Cerrar".
  - Cerrar and a save go back by history; with none, new goes to `VARIETIES` and edit goes to `ENTRIES`.
  - The toast is armed through `flash.armAfterLeaving`.
  - `?variety=` chooses that variety. "Stock inicial" is preselected only when `entriesOf` is empty for it; the form waits for `/stock` for that.
  - A `useBlocker` discard guard covers typed grams, cost and note.
  - A 404 `stock.entry_not_found` shows the gone screen with "Volver a Variedades".
  - The two inline `invalidateQueries` calls and `invalidateStockAfterDeleting` are gone, replaced by `invalidateResource('stock', { refetchType: 'none' })`.
- `web/src/router.tsx`: mounts `ENTRIES` and `VARIETY_ENTRIES(':id')` with chrome 'card', and the entry form routes with chrome 'none'.
- `web/src/styles/app.css`: row layout for the entries list (figures column, actions, back link).
- `web/src/components/AvisoExito.tsx`: adds the toasts 'Entrada guardada' and 'Entrada eliminada' to the `SuccessToast` union.
- `scripts/design/screens.json` and `sample_app.py`: new rows `EntradaStockEditar` and `TodasLasEntradasVariedad`; `TodasLasEntradas` now has route = path; the new `entry` fact.

### Tests
At the declared seam (Testing Library with `fetch` faked):
- `EntriesList.test.tsx` (10): all entries newest first; one variety's entries only, with Back to the variety; Editar link; delete with "Antes de borrar" and toast; declining; delete of an already-gone entry; empty state; gone variety or id; a delete marks the catalog stale (checked on a seeded catalog query, via `catalogQueryOptions`).
- `EntradaStock.test.tsx` (18): the original tests moved to the new paths, plus:
  - preselection with no entries, with entries, and with another variety's entries only;
  - no variety chosen from "+ Entrada de stock";
  - Cerrar by history and with no history (new and edit);
  - a save going back by history with its toast;
  - a missing or foreign entry showing the gone screen;
  - a save marking the catalog stale;
  - the discard guard, exercised on typed text for both "keep editing" and "discard".

### Decisions and assumptions
See the assumptions field.

### For the Tech Lead
- Look first at `EntradaStock.tsx` (the outer component waits for `/stock` only when `?variety=` is present) and at `EntriesList.tsx`.
- Checks in the system's runs: `tests` was cut off at 600 s with no failure. `web-tests` had three 5 s timeouts in files I did not touch, under a load average of 21–37; those pass when I run them alone (23 tests). `web-e2e` I did not run.
- `compare.sh`: `EntradaStock` and `TodasLasEntradas` captured. The first capture showed the lists with the "+ Nueva venta" button and cramped rows; I fixed both (chrome 'card', row CSS) and did not re-run it. `EntradaStockCompu` reports "todavía no existe en la app", so there is no built screen to compare for the computer form.
- In the capture, the Stock tab was still in the bar. The list-and-panes subtask removes it.
- Check by eye that the entries lists keep Variedades as the active tab.

### Changes
- `web/src/screens/varieties/PriceHistory.tsx`: the price history. It shows "‹ <variety>", "Precios", every price newest first with "Rige desde", "Vigente hoy", the bulk tier and a Borrar button per row. Delete asks through ConfirmDialog with `deleteNotice`, then toasts "Precio eliminado". It also shows the design's closing note.
- `web/src/screens/varieties/PriceForm.tsx`: the pure price form. It posts the price, invalidates through `invalidateResource('varieties')`, and shows each refusal under its own field while keeping what was typed. The host supplies the exits and the dirty flag.
- `web/src/screens/varieties/PriceNew.tsx`: the host for the price form. "Cerrar" and a save go back by history, or to `VARIETY(id)` when there is no history. A save arms "Precio guardado" for the screen it returns to. It uses the leave guard with "¿Descartás el precio?".
- `web/src/screens/varieties/RenameForm.tsx`: the exported rename form (`PATCH /varieties/{id}`), same pattern as PriceForm, with "Variedad guardada" handed to its host.
- `web/src/screens/varieties/VarietyRoute.tsx`: the one reader of `:id`. A non-integer id shows the gone screen and the API is never asked.
- `web/src/router.tsx`: mounts the two routes, with `chrome: 'card'` for the history and `chrome: 'none'` for the form.
- `scripts/design/screens.json`: route = path for VerTodas, PrecioNuevo and PrecioNuevoCompu.

### Tests
- `PriceHistory.test.tsx`, 8 tests, Testing Library with `fetch` faked:
  - list order, "Vigente hoy", bulk tier and the closing note;
  - back by history and back with no history;
  - delete notice copy and the DELETE call;
  - the `catalog` key is stale after a delete;
  - gone variety, non-numeric id, and a price gone on delete.
- `PriceNew.test.tsx`, 12 tests, through the real data router:
  - the date starts as today, a save posts and toasts once, and bulk fields are sent;
  - the `catalog` key is stale after a save;
  - each of the three refusal codes under its field with typed values kept;
  - an unparseable amount sends nothing;
  - "Cerrar" with and without history;
  - the discard guard: stay, then discard, with nothing written;
  - a gone variety on load and on save.
- `RenameForm.test.tsx`, 7 tests: the PATCH body, the catalog goes stale, the dirty flag, refusals under the name with typed text kept, gone and "Cerrar" handed to the host.
- `priceScreens.test.ts`: the three `screens.json` entries have route = path, equal to the `routes.ts` path.

### Decisions and assumptions
See the assumptions field. Design differences left on purpose:
- The computer layout shows the form as a page, not the design's modal.
- No "$" prefix on the amount inputs.
- A native date input instead of the "Hoy 05/10" chip.
- The built bar still shows the old "Stock" tab, because list-and-panes removes it.

### For the Tech Lead

- The rename form has no host or opener here by design, so variety-page must wire it through history state (`navigate('.', {state:{form:'rename'}})`) and its own leave guard.
- The gate's runs failed on machine load: pytest hit 600 s twice with no failing test shown, e2e `flow.spec.ts` timed out once, and web-tests timed out in NuevaVenta and four other files. At the time, the load average was 25–39. In the foreground, `flow.spec.ts` passes alone, and the ventas, settings, clientes, expenses and EntradaStock tests pass (18 files, 183 tests). This diff changes no backend file. I did not re-run pytest.
- The `VariedadDetalle` screen still has its own inline price and rename forms. I left it alone until variety-page replaces it.

### For the Developer

Nothing blocks. I verified:
- **The three screens:**
  - The price history is mounted at `VARIETY_PRICES(id)` as a card. Back with no history goes to `VARIETY(id)`. The list is newest first with "Rige desde". Delete asks first, using `deleteNotice`, then shows "Precio eliminado".

- **Invariants:**
  - Every write goes through `invalidateResource('varieties')`, and `CROSS_INVALIDATES` marks the catalog stale.

  - `router.tsx` names the routes only through `routes.ts` constants.
  - `VarietyRoute` is the only new reader of `:id`.
  - There are no loose hex values, inline styles, console calls or storage use.

### Changes
- `web/src/screens/varieties/VarietyPage.tsx` (new): the page. It takes `varietyId` as a prop only and holds the summary line, the two actions, the four sections, the rename form via history state, and the leave guard. It scrolls to the catalog anchor, and a gone variety shows "Eso ya no está.".
- `VarietyStockSection`, `VarietyPricesSection`, `VarietyThresholdSection`, `VarietyCatalogSection` (new): one read-only component per section. The threshold switch is derived from the stock row; turning it off sends `{grams:null}` and stays on and disabled until the server answers and `GET /stock` is re-fetched.
- `PriceRows.tsx` (new): price rows with the delete flow, shared by `PriceHistory` (all rows) and the prices section (last 3). `PriceHistory.tsx` now uses it.
- `varietyForms.ts` (new): `readForm` and `useOpenForm`, which push `{form}` as history state.
- `router.tsx`: `VARIETY(':id')` now mounts `VarietyRoute` → `VarietyPage` with `chrome: 'card'`. `VariedadDetalle.tsx` and its test are removed.
- `stock/detail.ts` gains `warningFor`, moved from Variedades. `screens/originLabel.ts` is new, shared with `EntriesList`.
- `styles/app.css`: tokens-only styles for the page.
- e2e specs (flow, catalog, analysis, customers, panel, payments, sales, pinnedCatalog, catalogInsecureOrigin): "Nuevo precio" is a link, the price form's button is "Guardá el precio", the amount is found by `/^Precio de lista/`, and the flow's threshold step is removed until section-forms.

### Tests
`VarietyPage.test.tsx` at the declared seams (VarietyPage via Testing Library with `fetch` faked, and `VarietyRoute` param parsing):
- summary per status, no price, and "Oculta";
- the "+ Entrada" link carries the variety, and the page has no "+ Nueva venta";
- only Stock y entradas and Precios carry the last 3 rows and "Ver todas";
- the threshold and catalog sections read their values;
- the openers push history state and Back closes the form;
- the warning off: switch on and disabled while the PUT runs, flips after the answer, and stays on after a refusal or a dropped network;
- a gone id and a non-integer id both show "Eso ya no está.", with no API call for the latter.

### Decisions and assumptions
See the assumptions field.

### For the Tech Lead
- I merged entries-screens as asked: `screens.json` and `router.tsx` conflicts keep both sides, and the merge is committed.
- `compare.sh --only VariedadDetalle` ran, but only after the change; I did not take a before run. The page matches the design's structure. Differences: "Borrar" word instead of the trash icon, the tab bar still shows Stock until list-and-panes, and the catalog section shows values read-only where the design draws editable fields (the human's decision).
- Look first at `VarietyThresholdSection.tsx` (switch behaviour) and `VarietyPage.tsx` (form-state handling).
- The gate's earlier e2e failures came from the price form's two labels starting with "Precio", which broke strict-mode lookups, and from the analysis and catalog specs still using the old button. Both are fixed and flow, catalog and analysis pass on the secure origin. I did not re-run the other e2e specs or the insecure-origin one. ## Developer: variety-page (v2)

### Checks

Run by the system on `e78d24a` when the Developer delivered.

| Check | Command | Result |
|---|---|---|
| `tests` | `uv run --no-project pytest` | passed |
| `lint` | `uv run --no-project ruff check .` | passed |
| `types` | `uv run --no-project mypy app tests` | passed |
| `web-types` | `npm --prefix web run typecheck` | passed |
| `web-lint` | `npm --prefix web run lint` | passed |
| `web-tests` | `npm --prefix web test` | passed |
| `web-e2e` | `npm --prefix web run e2e` | passed |

### Changes
This round changes tests only; page code is as delivered before (head e78d24a on top of cd7fbf6).
- `web/src/screens/varieties/VarietyPage.test.tsx`: the harness mounts the page inside the real `Shell` and takes the variety route's `handle` from the app's own `router.tsx`, so card chrome is exercised. The weak "unknown form state" test is replaced by real ones. New `renaming from the page` group.
- `web/src/routes.test.ts`: pins `routes.VARIETY('7')` to `handle: { chrome: 'card' }`.

### Tests
- Form states (seam: VarietyPage with history state): `{form:'bogus'}`, `{form: 7}`, a state with no `form` and a non-object state each render no form and no "Próximamente". `'threshold'` and `'catalog'` render nothing here (section-forms draws them) and no placeholder.
- Rename (seam: opener to form): "Editar nombre" pushes `{form:'rename'}` and shows the form with the current name. Cerrar returns to null state. Back (`navigate(-1)`) closes it. Saving PATCHes once, closes and toasts "Variedad guardada". Typing then Cerrar asks "¿Descartás el cambio?"; "No, seguí escribiendo" stays with the text, discarding leaves with no write. A taken name is refused inline and keeps the typed text.
- Chrome: the page inside the Shell shows no "Nueva venta" while navigation stays. `routes.test.ts` pins the handle as `{ chrome: 'card' }`; a missing handle fails both.
- Run: varieties, routes and shell test files (10 files, 122 tests), web typecheck and lint pass.

### compare.sh (`--only VariedadDetalle`)
Before = `3c4c48b` (git-archive copy), after = head. Only the phone viewport exists for this screen: `VariedadesCompu` (the 1280-wide `/varieties/{low}` entry) has `route: null`, and compare.sh printed "todavía no existe en la app" on both runs. The computer view belongs to list-and-panes.
- **Before**: the old `VariedadDetalle`. Name and "Editar", a "Rige desde" price block (Lista / Desde 5 g), "Nuevo precio", an always-open threshold form ("Umbral de aviso (g)", "Guardar aviso", "Apagar aviso"), a "Historial" with "Borrar", and "+ Nueva venta" at the bottom. None of the design's summary, sections or switch.
- **After**: "‹ Variedades" back link, name with "Editar", summary line `18 g · ● baja · 24 días · $14.000 x g`, "+ Entrada" and "Nuevo precio". Four sections: Stock y entradas and Precios with "Ver todas", Aviso de stock bajo with a switch and "Editar", En el catálogo with read-only values and "Editar". No "+ Nueva venta".

Differences left between the design and the delivered page:
1. A price row says "Borrar" where the design shows a trash icon.
2. The tab bar still reads "Stock" and the design says "Variedades"; the tab bar belongs to list-and-panes.
3. En el catálogo shows read-only text ("Se muestra", emoji, "70 %", aroma) with "Editar" instead of the design's switch and editable inputs; this is the human's Decision 1, and section-forms draws the forms.
4. The design's switch row has a hint line "Avisar con menos de 20 g" under "Avisando: quedan 18 g"; the page shows only the first line.
5. The design's catalog footnote ("Cada venta usa el precio que regía ese día…") is not on the page.
6. The summary status is the shape-plus-word mark (● baja) where the design has plain coloured text.
7. The back link is underlined; the design's is plain.
8. Entry and price dates differ (27/08 vs 26/08) because the sample app seeds relative to today; not a code difference.
9. Section titles sit slightly further from their rows than in the design.

### Decisions and assumptions
See the assumptions field: the before run used `git archive` (worktree creation is denied), the computer comparison does not exist yet, the chrome check lives in the page test, and the taken-name label is matched by regex.

### For the Tech Lead
Look first at `VarietyPage.test.tsx` lines 326-441 (new groups) and the harness at its top (`handle` taken from the app router). The first delivery's full gate had unrelated load flakes in Gastos / ExpenseNewRoute / NuevaVenta tests (they pass alone) and a 600 s pytest cut-off; I did not rerun the full suite here.

### Changes
- `web/src/screens/varieties/ThresholdForm.tsx` is new. It has the field "Avisar con menos de", "Guardá el aviso" and "Cerrar", and sends `PUT /stock/varieties/{id}/threshold`. A refusal shows under the field and keeps what was typed. It reports whether anything was typed to its host.
- `web/src/screens/varieties/thresholdMessages.ts` is new. It holds the threshold's fixed strings and refusal table, so the form and the switch section share one copy.
- `web/src/screens/varieties/CatalogAttributesForm.tsx` is new. It has the switch "Mostrar en el catálogo", Emoji, % índica and Aroma/sabor, ending in "Guardá el catálogo". It sends `PUT /catalog/varieties/{id}` and invalidates `catalog` through `invalidateResource`. Each refusal shows under its own field.
- `web/src/screens/varieties/catalogFormErrors.ts` is new: the one table from the three `catalog.*` refusal codes to a field and its message.
- `web/src/screens/varieties/VarietyPage.tsx` renders the two forms when `location.state.form` is `threshold` or `catalog`. Cerrar and a successful save call `navigate(-1)`, and the existing leave guard asks only when text was typed. The "gone" handling is now shared with the rename form.
- `web/src/screens/varieties/VarietyThresholdSection.tsx` imports the shared strings. It keeps the switch derived from the saved threshold, changing only after the server answers.
- `web/src/screens/Catalogo.tsx` loses all attribute editing: the `setAttr*` state, the refusal-code map, the "Atributos en el catálogo" list and its form, the gone notice, and `writeCatalog`.
- `web/src/screens/Catalogo.test.tsx` loses the edit tests and gains one that asserts there is no Editar button and no field.
- `web/e2e/flow.spec.ts` adds the threshold steps (on through the form, then off), which the review had noted as missing. `web/e2e/catalog.spec.ts` edits Rosa's attributes from her page.

### Tests
- `catalogFormErrors.test.ts` is the plan's test-first seam. Each code lands under its own field, and four other codes land under none.
- `VarietyPage.test.tsx` covers both forms, with the real router and `fetch` faked:
  - **Read-only until Editar:** both sections show values only until Editar.
  - **Switch on:** opens an empty form, and Cerrar leaves it off with no request.
  - **Aviso save:** sends `{grams: 15.5}`, closes, toasts, and asks `/stock` and `/catalog` again.
  - **Refusals:** a refused amount, or text that is no amount, shows under the field and keeps what was typed. A too-long aroma shows under Aroma/sabor only, with Emoji and % índica kept. The emoji and índica refusals and a non-digit índica show under their own field.
  - **Gone:** a 404 on save shows "Eso ya no está.".
  - **Leaving:** Back closes the form. Typed text asks first (keep stays, discard closes with no write). An untouched form closes without asking.
  - **Reload:** a reload with that state reopens the form with the saved values.
  - **Catalog save:** sends the four values and asks `/catalog` again.
  - **Catalog form fields:** it carries no maxLength.
  - **Switch off and the rename form:** existing tests, all still passing.
- The threshold and catalog saves are checked as refetches of the keys they invalidate, not by reading query state, because the page's queries are active.

### Decisions and assumptions
See `assumptions`. The two drawing departures for the PR text are: (1) "Aviso de stock bajo" and "En el catálogo" show read-only values with "Editar" instead of the drawing's always-editable boxes and fixed text, per the human's decision; (2) the "Avisar con menos de 20 g" helper line under the switch is not drawn.

### Changes
- `web/src/stock/summaryLine.ts`: the pure summary line (null for an empty list; "N variedades · todo ok" or the joined counts of sin entradas / agotadas / bajas).
- `web/src/components/useIsComputer.ts`: `matchMedia('(min-width: 900px)')` through `useSyncExternalStore`.
- `web/src/screens/varieties/`:
  - `varietiesByName.ts`: the sort by name.
  - `useSelectedVariety.ts`: the first variety by name, only once the list has resolved.
  - `VarietiesLayout.tsx`: phone is the Outlet alone; computer is the list on the left and the Outlet on the right.
  - `VarietiesIndex.tsx`: phone shows the list; computer replaces to the first variety.
  - `VarietyNew.tsx`: the new-variety form.
- `web/src/screens/Variedades.tsx`: rewritten as the list with the summary, actions, rows (status, "Oculta", figures, price or "Sin precio: no se puede vender") and the "Todas las entradas" foot link.
- `web/src/router.tsx`: the `/varieties` route is now a layout with the index, `VARIETY_NEW` and `VARIETY(':id')` children; the `mi-stock` route and the Stock import are gone.
- `web/src/shell/Shell.tsx`: the Stock tab is replaced by Variedades.
- Deleted `Stock.tsx` and `Stock.test.tsx`.
- `web/src/styles/app.css`: tokens only; the list rows, panes and the computer-only modal for the new-variety form.
- `app/static_spa.py`, `tests/test_serving.py`: the `/mi-stock` → `/varieties` row and its 308 test.
- `scripts/design/screens.json`: `route` = `path` for VariedadesCompu, VariedadNueva and VariedadNuevaCompu.
- `web/e2e/*.spec.ts` (analysis, catalog, catalogInsecureOrigin, customers, flow, panel, payments, pinnedCatalog, sales, tapBudget): moved to the new flow. "Nueva variedad" is a link and its save button is "Agregá la variedad"; the tab bar reads "Variedades"; entries are read and edited in "Todas las entradas"; where the list and a variety's page both show a price, the assertion takes `.first()`; the entry form is reached by clicking "+ Entrada de stock".

### Tests
- `summaryLine.test.ts`: unit test, written first, covering the empty list, all ok, singular, and the mixed order.
- `Variedades` list (Testing Library, `fetch` faked): sorting, status word, "Oculta", price or "Sin precio", the summary, and the empty, error and offline states. The first list test now has a 5 s `findAllByRole` timeout because it failed under load on a cold start.
- `useSelectedVariety` (Testing Library, faked viewport): redirect with replace on a computer once the list is non-empty; no redirect on a phone or while loading or empty.
- `VarietyNew`: success with toast and invalidation; the name_taken, name_invalid and generic refusals keep the typed text; the leave guard.
- `tests/test_serving.py`: `FrontNavigation /mi-stock 308`, one parametrised row.
- Adjusted: `Loading.screens.test.tsx`, `barActiveTab.test.ts`, `Shell.test.tsx` and `routes.test.ts` (index-route trailing slash).
- e2e: each spec the gate reported failing (analysis, catalog, customers, flow, panel, payments, pinnedCatalog, sales, catalogInsecureOrigin) was re-run on its own after its edit and passes.

### Decisions and assumptions
- "Mandar catálogo" is never disabled: the app has no first-run checklist source.
- The summary line uses the plural forms above.
- `useIsComputer` was duplicated rather than refactoring `Settings.tsx`, which this subtask does not touch.
- The computer modal is CSS only, so Back and Cerrar work through history.
- Toast loss: reaching a form with no history on a computer (a reload on it, or a direct URL), then saving, leaves to `/varieties`, which redirects to the first variety. The flash reducer dismisses a toast when the location key changes, so the toast never shows in that case. A user who opens the form from the list (history present) is unaffected. The e2e specs click through the list instead.

### For the Tech Lead
- Compare results: each of the four screens ran ok. Remaining differences from the design:
  - Phone list: `MarcaEstado` puts shape and word together at the right; the design puts the shape left of the name and the word as a chip.
  - Computer new-variety modal: the card is now 480 px and centred horizontally with a left-aligned hint, but it sits vertically centred instead of at 80 px from the top, and it has no header divider or shadow.
  - The computer comparison has no `apostrophe'` wordmark on the rail.
  - The design's disabled "Mandar catálogo" first-run state is not built.
- Checks:
  - Passed: web typecheck, web lint, ruff.
  - pytest reached 93% and then hit the 600 s timeout at the gate; no test failed in the output. I did not re-run it.
  - After the final fixes I did not re-run the full web-tests or the full e2e suite; the last full gate run had one web-test failure, now fixed and passing alone, and two e2e failures, now fixed and passing alone.
- Look first at `Variedades.tsx` (rows and the catalog/stock join), `VarietiesIndex.tsx` and `VarietyNew.tsx` (leave and flash behaviour), then the toast-loss note above.

### Integration

All eight subtask branches (`routing-server`, `routing-front`, `catalog-screen`, `entries-screens`, `prices-and-rename`, `variety-page`, `section-forms`, `list-and-panes`) were already in `agentic/GH-47` when I started. I checked each with `git merge-base --is-ancestor`. There was nothing left to merge and no conflict to resolve. I needed no code fix, so the one commit I added is an empty marker commit (`44d3e6c`) that records the integration pass.

I ran each check once over the whole branch, in the foreground:

- **ruff, mypy (433 files), `tsc -b` and oxlint** all passed.
- **pytest:** my foreground run was cut off by the 600 s timeout, and I did not run it again. The run it moved to the background then finished with exit code 0. The presubmit's own pytest run was also cut off at 600 s, at 93%, with no failing test.
- **web-tests:** 793 of 819 passed. The 26 failures were all 5 s timeouts, spread over 12 files, including ones this task never touched (Gastos, Invitacion, settings groups, NuevaVenta, ClienteForm). I re-ran the failing files that touch this task. `Variedades.test.tsx` passes alone (20 of 20), and `VarietyPage.test.tsx` and `NuevaVenta.test.tsx` pass alone (74 of 74). With a 30 s timeout, `Variedades.test.tsx` had one failure, "picking another variety replaces the selection", which passes alone. The presubmit's run failed 19 tests in 11 files, again all 5 s timeouts, including the VarietyPage threshold and catalog form tests that pass alone.
- **web-e2e:** 20 passed and 1 failed in my run. `flow.spec.ts` exceeded the whole-test 30 s limit at line 212, the last step, after every variety step had passed. The presubmit's run failed 7 specs (analysis, flow, passwords, payments, sales, settings, tapBudget), a different set from mine. The tapBudget failure was "Protocol error: Internal server error, session closed" on the expense form, which this task does not change.

The machine's load average was 36 to 70 during all of this, mostly from other worktrees' vitest processes (for example `GH-110.vitest-waits`). I found nothing in the merged code that needed fixing, so I added no enforcement artefacts.

No plan invariant turned out unsatisfiable.

### For the Tech Lead

- **Run the delivery gate on a quiet machine.** Every red result I saw is a timeout under heavy load, in files that pass alone, and the failing set changes from run to run. I could not confirm the full web-tests, e2e and pytest runs under normal load.
- **Findings table, rows I did not change:**
  - The three design departures disclosed by prices-and-rename, entries-screens and list-and-panes remain: the computer form as a page instead of a modal, no "$" prefix, a native date input instead of the "Hoy" chip, and the shape and word on the right in the phone list.
  - The duplicated strings remain (price messages and toasts, and `CATALOG_ATTRIBUTES_SAVED` against `CATALOG_SAVED`), as does the second copy of the computer-viewport hook (`useIsComputer.ts` against `Settings.tsx`).
  - On a computer, a save from `/varieties/new` with no history still loses the "Variedad guardada" toast, because `/varieties` redirects with `replace`.
- **`compare.sh`:** section-forms reported only one before and after run, for VariedadDetalle. Invariant 16 asks for all three screens, on phone and computer.
- **Reload with a section form open** reopens the form with the saved values. This was a known risk in the plan, and it is worth checking against what PM expects.

**No live walk was possible.** `scripts/design/sample-app.sh` printed nothing, so there was no address or user to walk with, and this seat cannot run commands. I checked the built routes and screens against the validated flow, situation by situation. A by-eye walk of the running app is still owed before the PR is merged.

**Situation 1: what I have / what it costs.**
- Variedades is mounted at `/varieties`. On a computer, `VarietiesLayout` shows the list beside the selected variety's page.
- `useSelectedVariety` picks the first variety by name only once the list has loaded and has at least one variety.
- Each row shows `MarcaEstado`, the name, "Oculta" beside the status, grams and days left, and the price or "Sin precio: no se puede vender".
- The summary line comes from `summaryLine`: none with no varieties, "N variedades · todo ok", or the non-ok statuses counted by word. The empty state is in place.
- *Observation:* a variety missing from `GET /stock`'s answer would be dropped from the list.

**Situation 2: stock entry.**
- "+ Entrada de stock" on the list opens `/varieties/entries/new`.
- "+ Entrada" on the page opens `?variety={id}`. The form then chooses that variety and preselects "Stock inicial" only when the variety has no entries. This matches the flow.
- *Observation:* on the page, Stock y entradas shows an empty list instead of the "Sin entradas de stock" line.

**Situation 3: new price.**
- "Nuevo precio" opens `/varieties/{id}/prices/new`.
- Precios has its last 3 rows and "Ver todas", which opens `/varieties/{id}/prices`.

**Situation 4: warning and catalog attributes.**
- This is built as the human decided.
- **Aviso de stock bajo:** the section is read-only, with "Editar" and a switch. Turning the switch on only opens `ThresholdForm`. Turning it off saves at once, shows "Aviso guardado", and stays on after a refusal, "Sin conexión" or a gone variety.
- **En el catálogo:** the section is read-only, with "Editar" opening `CatalogAttributesForm`.
- Both forms open over the page by history state. Leaving with typed text asks first ("¿Descartás el cambio?").
- Rename goes through "Editar" next to the name.
- These two blocking items from my earlier walk are closed.

**Situation 5: Compartir.**
- Variedades → "Mandar catálogo" → `/varieties/catalog`.
- Compartir shows only when `canShare()`. A failure shows "No se pudo compartir.", and a cancel shows nothing.
- Copiar shows "Catálogo copiado" on success and "No se pudo copiar." on failure.
- Both buttons hand over exactly the API's `text`, and only after this visit's own fetch has finished.
- With nothing to send, both are hidden and the empty state shows.
- *Observation:* "Mandar catálogo" is never disabled, because the first-run checklist has no source in the app yet.
- *Observation:* the empty-state wording differs from the validated screens.

**Situation 6: what does not go out.**
- Each left-out variety gets one word from the server's `state`: oculta, sin precio, agotada or sin entradas.
- Each name links to `VARIETY(id)#en-el-catalogo`, and the page scrolls to that section.

**Situation 7: pin and Publicar.**
- This stays on `PinPublish`, on the catalog screen.
- Inicio has no out-of-date notice. The flow does not depend on it.

**Situation 8: bot and header/footer.**
- Configuración exists, so no temporary place was built.
- The links go straight to `/settings/catalog-message` and `/settings/integrations`, which is the PRD's own condition.

**Situation 9: old paths.**
- These are server-side 308 redirects:
  - `/variedades` → `/varieties`
  - `/variedades/{n}` → `/varieties/{n}`
  - `/catalogo` → `/varieties/catalog`
  - `/mi-stock` → `/varieties`
  - `/mi-stock/nueva-entrada` → `/varieties/entries/new`
  - `/mi-stock/entradas/{n}` → `/varieties/entries/{n}`
- Every `/varieties` route is served to page loads, including the ones with an id. Unknown paths go to Inicio.
- The Stock screen and its tab are gone.

**Situation 10: Todas las entradas.**
- The list foot opens `/varieties/entries` ("‹ Variedades"), with the "Sin entradas de stock" empty state.
- *Observation:* Stock y entradas "Ver todas" on a variety's page opens `/varieties/{id}/entries` ("‹ <variety>"). That follows the PRD and PM's round-1 answer, not the prototype my walk accepted. Back now returns to the page.

### Verdict

`approved`: judged from the code, since the app never started, every job in the validated flow can be completed and both earlier blocking gaps are built as the human decided. The rest are observations, and a live walk is still owed.

## UI Lead: final comparisons

_The system denied 1 write attempt: this role writes nothing; reproduce by reading the code, the task documents and the existing tests, or by running the declared checks. A denial here is not a syntax problem._

### Comparisons

I ran `scripts/design/compare.sh` on the task's screens and judged ten comparisons: Variedades, VariedadesCompu, VariedadDetalle, Catalogo, CatalogoCompu, TodasLasEntradas, VerTodas, EntradaStock, PrecioNuevo and VariedadNueva. All ten rendered with no failed step. I judged them by eye: same design, not pixel-exact.

**Variedades (phone)**
- **Matches:** the order (summary line, "Mandar catálogo", "+ Entrada de stock" as a secondary button, "Nueva variedad", rows by name, "Todas las entradas"), the summary "1 sin entradas · 1 agotada · 1 baja", grams and days left, the price per gram, and the tab bar with "+ Nueva venta".
- *Observation:* the status sits differently. The design puts the mark by the name and the word as a chip on the right, with agotada inverted and baja in amber. The build puts mark and word together on the right as plain text, so agotada loses its inverted chip. The summary line is also indented more than drawn.
- *Observation:* the empty state and the disabled "Mandar catálogo" had no built side in the capture. They rest on the tests.

**VariedadesCompu (two panes)**
- **Matches:** the list on the left, the first variety (Golden Cookies) selected on the right with no tap, and the page in the right pane.
- *Observation:* the computer rows show mark *and* word, which goes beyond the drawing and helps criterion 4.
- *Observation:* the sidebar has neither the "apostrophe" wordmark nor "+ Nueva venta", which the design draws on this tab root.
- *Observation:* rows whose price wraps put it on a second line.

**VariedadDetalle**
- **Matches:** "‹ Variedades", the name with "Editar", the summary line (now with the baja mark), two equal buttons, Stock y entradas and Precios with "Ver todas", and no "+ Nueva venta".
- **The two departures the human accepted** (Decision 1) shipped: Aviso de stock bajo and En el catálogo are read views with "Editar". The catalog view shows "Se muestra", the emoji, "70 %" and the aroma.
- *Observation:* price delete is the text "Borrar", not the drawn trash icon.
- *Observation:* the price-rule footnote is missing on the page. It is still on VerTodas.
- *Observation:* "Avisar con menos de 20 g" did not show under the warning line in the sample.

**Catalogo / CatalogoCompu**
- **Matches:** "‹ Variedades", "Catálogo", the "Cómo se ve en Telegram" preview with bold names, Copiar, Publicar with its reason in words and a link, "Qué no sale y por qué" with the chips "sin entradas" and "agotada" (agotada inverted), and "Editar encabezado y pie".
- *Observation:* Compartir is missing from the capture. The capture runs over http with no share sheet, which is the case where the PRD hides it. Where `navigator.share` exists, its presence rests on tests.
- *Observation:* the preview drops the Telegram channel framing: the avatar, "canal" and the inner white bubble.
- *Observation:* the disabled Publicar is a solid mid-grey pill and can look active.
- *Observation:* the unavailable wording follows the docs, not the prototype, as agreed.
- *Observation:* the left-out order differs from the drawing.

**TodasLasEntradas / VerTodas**
- They match as cards with "‹ Variedades" and "‹ Golden Cookies" respectively, rows with Editar/Borrar, and the price footnote.

**EntradaStock / PrecioNuevo / VariedadNueva**
- They match as forms: title, "Cerrar", no bar, fields, helper lines and the primary button.
- *Observation:* they use native controls. The date input reads 10/06/2026 instead of "Hoy 05/10". The variety is a plain select without "Hoy tiene N g". Origen has a bordered fieldset. Gramos is not beside Fecha.
- *Observation:* the confirmations and the rename frame were not in the capture.

### Verdict

`approved`: every screen and state the PRD requires shipped and reads as the same design. Everything above is an observation to list in the PR, mainly:
- the status-chip styling;
- the missing sidebar "+ Nueva venta";
- the Telegram framing;
- the active-looking disabled Publicar;
- the native date and select inputs.

## PM: final validation

### Verdict

`approved`: neither report found anything blocking. UX Lead checked every situation in the validated flow against the code, and each one can be completed. Both of the human's decisions (Aviso de stock bajo and En el catálogo as read-only views with "Editar" forms) are built. UI Lead found that all ten comparisons rendered and read as the same design.

The live walk of the running app could not be done because `sample-app.sh` printed nothing. A by-eye walk is still owed before merge. That is an observation, not a blocking finding, so it does not return the task.

### For Tech Lead

Nothing blocks. These observations should be listed in the PR:
- "Mandar catálogo" is never disabled, because the first-run checklist has no source yet.
- The catalog screen's empty-state wording differs from the validated screens.
- Stock y entradas on a variety's page shows an empty list instead of "Sin entradas de stock".
- Inicio has no out-of-date notice.
- On the phone, the status chips differ from the drawing: agotada loses its inverted chip.
- The computer sidebar has no "+ Nueva venta" and no wordmark.
- The Telegram channel framing is gone from the preview.
- The disabled Publicar looks active.
- The entry form uses native date and select inputs (no "Hoy 05/10", no "Hoy tiene N g").
- Price delete is the word "Borrar", not the drawn trash icon.
